  push:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  build-and-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - uses: actions/setup-dotnet@v6
        with:
          dotnet-version: 10.0.x

      - uses: actions/setup-node@v7
        with:
          node-version: lts/*

      - name: Build and test
        run: scripts/run-all.sh

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        if: always()
        with:
          name: artifacts
          path: artifacts/
          if-no-files-found: ignore

name: Deploy to velixir

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    concurrency:
      group: velixir-deploy
      cancel-in-progress: false
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Install the velixir CLI
        shell: bash
        run: |
          curl -fsSL https://velixir.net/install.sh | sh
          echo "$HOME/.velixir/bin" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"

      - name: Deploy
        env:
          VELIXIR_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.VELIXIR_API_KEY }}
          VELIXIR_APP_ID: ${{ vars.VELIXIR_APP_ID }}
        run: |
          if [ -z "$VELIXIR_API_KEY" ]; then
            echo "::error::Add a repository secret named VELIXIR_API_KEY with a velixir API key that has Write permission (Settings > Secrets and variables > Actions)."
            exit 1
          fi
          if [ -z "$VELIXIR_APP_ID" ]; then
            echo "::error::Add a repository variable named VELIXIR_APP_ID with the velixir app id for nest.velixir.run (Settings > Secrets and variables > Actions > Variables)."
            exit 1
          fi
          velixir deploy --app "$VELIXIR_APP_ID" --source-dir ./src/Nest.Web

# nest

A shared notebook for house hunting. Paste a link from Zillow, Redfin, Realtor.com or any site, fill in the details together and leave comments. No accounts, no cookies, no database. Blazor Server on .NET 10, on the [velixir](https://velixir.net) free plan (0.25 vCPU, 256 MB). Live at https://nest.velixir.run/.

- The server keeps the newest 1,000 entries (links, details, comments) in memory and forgets them on restart. Each browser keeps up to 20,000 in IndexedDB and offers back what the server lacks.
- A home is every entry that shares a key with it: Zillow `zpid`, Redfin id, Realtor.com id, normalized street and ZIP, or a hashed link. Different query strings are the same link. Entries from different people and sites merge onto one page; the newest value of each detail wins.
- Dotted border: only browsers still have it.
- No third-party code. Strict CSP with no inline scripts.

## Requirements

- .NET SDK 10.0.100 or later in the 10.0 band
- Node.js 22 or later (JavaScript tests)
- Podman 4 or later (end-to-end tests)

## Commands

```
dotnet run --project src/Nest.Web
scripts/run-all.sh [--skip-e2e] [--skip-js] [--skip-export]
```

`run-all.sh` builds with warnings as errors, runs the .NET and JavaScript tests with coverage, runs Playwright in Podman, then writes `docs/llm/dump.txt`. Output lands in `artifacts/`. Exit code 2 is a usage error, 127 a missing tool.

## Layout

| Path | What |
| --- | --- |
| `src/Nest.Web` | The app. The only folder velixir builds. |
| `src/Nest.Web/wwwroot/js` | Shared modules: keys, fields, merge, storage, sync |
| `tests/Nest.Web.Tests` | xUnit v3 unit and integration tests |
| `tests/Nest.E2E` | Playwright tests, run in Podman |
| `tests/js` | Vitest tests for the hand-written modules |
| `tests/fixtures` | Cases both test suites run, so C# and JavaScript agree |
| `scripts` | Build, test and export scripts |
| `docs/adr` | Architecture decision records |

## Deploy

Every push to `main` runs `.github/workflows/velixir.yml`, which uploads `src/Nest.Web` only. It needs the `VELIXIR_API_KEY` repository secret and the `VELIXIR_APP_ID` repository variable. Every push also runs `.github/workflows/ci.yml`. Turn on the HTTP health probe (`/health`) in velixir.

Limits can be changed without a rebuild through environment variables, for example `Nest__Capacity=2000`. See `src/Nest.Web/Infrastructure/NestOptions.cs`.

## License

AGPL-3.0-or-later. See `LICENSE` and `REUSE.toml`.

# 0002 Static SSR, one sync island, views in JavaScript

Status: accepted

- Every page is static server-side rendering. Navigation uses Blazor enhanced navigation.
- `/` (home list) and `/homes/{provider}/{token}` (one home) each host one `InteractiveServer` island, `SyncIsland`. It renders nothing; it is the transport between the server and the browser. `/about` and the not-found page open no circuit.
- The home list is the front page because adding a link is the main task; a static landing page would cost phone users a tap.
- The server prerenders its own view of the list or the home so the page is useful before JavaScript runs. The view modules (`Pages/HomesPage.razor.js`, `Pages/ListingPage.razor.js`) then render the merge of server and local data. They wait for the first reconciliation or 3 s before replacing the prerendered list, and keep it while offline with nothing stored locally.
- Forms are plain HTML driven by the view modules, not Blazor forms: their values depend on data only the browser has.
- `App.razor.js` mounts the view for the element with `data-view` on load and on `enhancedload`, and disposes the previous one. The view root is `data-permanent` with a per-page id, so enhanced navigation does not overwrite JavaScript-owned markup and two different homes never share one.
- Shared logic is in plain modules under `wwwroot/js` (`keys`, `fields`, `merge`, `store`, `sync`), served at `/js/`. One `Nest` instance per document holds the entries, so the island and the view see the same state.
- Reusable Razor components: `ListingCard`, `CommentItem`, `FactList`, `FieldInput`.
- Razor components are generated as public classes, so their parameters use only public types (strings, numbers, `Guid`, framework collections). Domain types stay internal; a component that needs one resolves it from a key, as `FieldInput` does with `ListingFields.Find`.
- No bUnit: components are thin; logic lives in services and modules tested directly, and pages are covered by integration and Playwright tests.

# 0003 Deploy only the web project

Status: accepted

velixir builds with a Nixpacks-style plan: `dotnet restore` and `dotnet publish -c Release -o out` at the upload root, then starts `./out/<first csproj>`. Uploading the repository root would also build the test projects, including the Playwright driver.

- `velixir.yml` runs `velixir deploy --app "$VELIXIR_APP_ID" --source-dir ./src/Nest.Web`. The API key is the `VELIXIR_API_KEY` secret; the app id is the `VELIXIR_APP_ID` repository variable, so the workflow holds no project-specific value.
- `Nest.Web.csproj` is self-sufficient: target framework, nullable, implicit usings and runtime settings are in the project. It has no package references.
- The web root is set to `wwwroot` next to the assembly when it exists. The app binds to `PORT` when set.
- Deploys are not gated on CI. A failing velixir build keeps the previous release; the `/health` probe stops a release that does not start.

# 0004 Entries, UUIDv7 ids and the handshake

Status: accepted

- Everything users add is an immutable entry: `link` (a URL), `fact` (one or more detail fields) or `comment` (text). Each carries the keys of its home (ADR 0005). Nothing is edited or deleted; a newer fact replaces an older value.
- The server mints every id with `Uuid7.After` from its own clock, so ids are unique, increasing and sortable as strings. Clients never mint ids, so adding needs a connection.
- The server keeps the newest 1,000 entries (`Nest__Capacity`).
- Handshake, in this order:
  1. The browser calls `Join`. The server sends a snapshot of ids only, plus its capacity.
  2. The browser fetches the entries it lacks, 50 ids per call.
  3. The browser offers entries the server lacks, newest first, at most the capacity, and only ones newer than the server's oldest when the server is full. Offers are sent in chunks under 24 KB of JSON.
- An offered entry is accepted only if its id is a valid UUIDv7, not in the future, older than the server's start, and the entry is already in normal form (rebuilding it gives the same entry). The server keeps the newest 1,000 and its own copy on id ties. Offers beyond the capacity per circuit are ignored.
- The start-time rule rejects forged recent entries: anything stamped after the server started came from this server, so if the server does not have it, it is forged or already evicted.
- A link whose key the server already has on a link entry returns `exists` and the home's key instead of a new entry.
- The same comment or fact on the same keys as one of the 20 newest entries is refused as a repeat.
- Interop uses `JsonElement` arguments and results with internal records, so no wire type is public.

SHA256:   5547f2819bf7947e5f658f9ffc967df5a4f8f6f5829e7484ddf2ab9b870827fc

# 0005 Listing keys, union-find merge, newest value wins

Status: accepted

- A home has no id of its own. It is the set of keys its entries carry: `zillow:<zpid>`, `redfin:<id>`, `realtor:m<id>`, `address:<zip>-<street>` and `url:<hash>` (ADR 0006).
- An entry's keys are the key derived from its own content (the link, or the street and ZIP of a fact) plus the keys of the home it was added to, at most 8, sorted.
- Merge: union-find over keys. Two entries that share any key belong to the same home, directly or through others. The result does not depend on order, so every browser and the server agree on the same entries.
- The page address is the key with the highest rank (Zillow, Redfin, Realtor.com, address, link, then text order). Any key of the home opens the same page.
- Details: for each field, the value from the entry with the newest id wins. An empty value clears the field. The previous different value is shown as "was …".
- Comments are listed oldest first. Links are shown once per key, newest URL.
- An address key is derived only from a fact that has both street and ZIP. Saving any address field sends all five so the key is consistent.
- Merging cannot be undone. A wrong link or address joins two homes for everyone. Accepted: there are no accounts to decide who may split them.
- The same algorithm exists in C# (`ListingIndex`, for the prerender) and JavaScript (`merge.js`). `tests/fixtures/listings.json` pins both to the same result.

# 0006 Reading listing links and addresses

Status: accepted

- The first `http(s)://` URL in the pasted text is used, so text shared from an app works. The URL ends at the first character outside printable ASCII, a space, `"`, `'`, `<` or `>`; trailing `.,;:!?)]}` are dropped. At most 2,048 characters.
- Query strings and fragments are dropped everywhere. Hosts are lowercased; user info in the host is rejected.
- Zillow: `<digits>_zpid` anywhere in the path. Redfin: `/home/<digits>`. Realtor.com: `_M<digits>-<digits>`. Leading zeros are dropped. These links are stored as `https://` with the original host and path.
- Other links: `url:` plus the 64-bit FNV-1a hash of host without `www.` or port plus the path without trailing slashes. Scheme does not count.
- Addresses: ASCII letters are lowercased, everything that is not an ASCII letter or digit separates words, common street words are shortened to USPS forms (`street` → `st`, `route` → `rte`, `north` → `n`, …). `apt`, `apartment`, `unit`, `suite`, `ste` and `#` start the unit; an explicit unit field wins. Key: `address:<zip>-<street words>[-unit-<unit words>]`, at most 100 characters.
- Non-ASCII letters are separators, not folded. Rare in US addresses; it keeps C# and JavaScript identical without Unicode tables.
- Regular expressions avoid case-insensitive flags and use `\z` in .NET, because the engines differ on Unicode case folding and on `$` before a newline.
- The browser also guesses an address from Zillow, Redfin and Realtor.com paths to prefill the details form. Zillow slugs mix street and city, so that guess is marked for checking. Guesses are never saved without the user pressing Save.
- `tests/fixtures/links.json` and `addresses.json` are run by both test suites.
- Nest never fetches the listing sites. There is no API access, and fetching would add third-party requests and terms-of-use questions.

# 0007 Details fields and their normal form

Status: accepted

| Field | Normal form |
| --- | --- |
| Price, HOA per month, taxes per year | Whole dollars. `$`, commas, spaces, `_` ignored. `450k`, `1.25m` accepted. At most 11 digits. |
| Bedrooms | 0–99 |
| Bathrooms | 0–99 with up to two decimals, trailing zeros dropped |
| Square feet | 1–10,000,000 |
| Year built | 1600–2100 |
| Status, type | One of a fixed list, matched ignoring ASCII case |
| State | Two letters, uppercased |
| ZIP code | Five digits; ZIP+4 is cut to five |
| Lot size, parking, heating and cooling, MLS number, street, unit, city | Text: control characters removed, spaces collapsed, cut to 20–120 characters |

- The browser validates before sending and shows the reason next to the field. The server applies the same rules and refuses an entry with any invalid value. `tests/fixtures/fields.json` pins both.
- Only changed fields are sent, except the address group (ADR 0005).
- Values are self-reported and unverified. There is no history editing.
- Comments: up to 4,000 UTF-16 code units, cut on grapheme boundaries, three or more blank lines collapsed, emoji kept.


================================================================================
FILE:     docs/adr/0008-local-storage.md
SIZE:     1.28 KB (1313 bytes)
LINES:    12
SHA256:   2f946c16997f1af0ad770ebaa2763cdee4409560c14d71e9a0e31d972673358f
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:37:53 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0008 IndexedDB, drafts and clearing

Status: accepted

- Database `nest`, store `entries`, key path `id`, values are entries plus `mine: true` for entries posted from this browser.
- Schema changes are numbered migrations in `wwwroot/js/store.js`; `onupgradeneeded` runs every step from the stored version. A database from a newer schema (`VersionError`) is deleted and recreated, since the server and other browsers can refill it. Other tabs close their connection on `versionchange`.
- Cap: 20,000 entries. After each write the oldest are deleted with one key-range delete and dropped from memory.
- All entries are loaded into memory once per page load to build the home list. 20,000 short entries fit comfortably on a phone.
- Unsent comments are kept per home in `localStorage` (`nest-draft:<key>`) until sent. The "only homes I added to" filter is `nest-mine-only`. The theme is `nest-theme`.
- Solid border: the server has the entry. Dotted: only browsers have it. A home card is dotted when none of its entries are on the server.
- The About page deletes the database, the drafts and the filter, keeps the theme, and empties the in-memory copy. It does not touch the server or other browsers. The next visit fills the database from the server.
- Without IndexedDB the site still works, without history.


================================================================================
FILE:     docs/adr/0009-realtime-delivery.md
SIZE:     0.89 KB (913 bytes)
LINES:    10
SHA256:   7f460c586cc6f57d5f9ab0565c711777f14b5363895785f2f17b56545435350d
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:37:53 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0009 Per-client outboxes over the circuit

Status: accepted

- Transport is the Blazor circuit: JavaScript calls `[JSInvokable]` methods (`Join`, `Ping`, `Fetch`, `Offer`, `Post`); the server calls the module's `apply`. No separate SignalR hub.
- Each island owns a bounded `Outbox<NestUpdate>` drained by one pump that sends batches in order and waits for the browser. A full outbox, a failed call or a 20 s stall drops the subscriber.
- The pump waits until the module is attached, because `Join` can arrive before `attach` returns to .NET.
- Subscribing enqueues the snapshot under the same lock as updates, so nothing is missed or duplicated.
- Deltas carry `version` and `previous`; a gap triggers a fresh `Join`. `apply` does not wait for fetching and offering, so the outbox never stalls on them.
- The browser pings every 25 s and rejoins when the server says it was dropped, and after every reconnect.

FILE:     docs/adr/0010-abuse-limits.md
SIZE:     0.91 KB (934 bytes)
LINES:    24
SHA256:   4764befc56878713a9f8f2d25a1e1d53b04d8bc1c8d16771ff1a1717a788ba8b
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:37:53 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0010 Rate and connection limits

Status: accepted

Defaults live in `NestOptions` and can be overridden with `Nest__*` environment variables.

| Limit | Default |
| --- | --- |
| HTTP requests per client | 120 burst, 10 per second |
| WebSocket connections per client | 8 |
| WebSocket connections in total | 80, then 503 |
| Entries per client | 6 burst, 1 every 2 s |
| Entries kept by the server | 1,000 |
| Offered entries per circuit | 1,000 |
| Fetched ids per call | 100 |
| Keys per entry | 8 |
| Comment | 4,000 characters |
| Link | 2,048 characters |
| Hub message | 32 KB |

- A client is its IP after `X-Forwarded-For`, or its /64 for IPv6. Keys live in memory only and are never logged or shown.
- The hub (`/_blazor`) only accepts WebSocket connections.
- Invalid entries do not use up the rate limit.
- There is no moderation. The About page says plainly that content spreads to every client and cannot be recalled.

FILE:     docs/adr/0011-security-headers.md
SIZE:     1.01 KB (1037 bytes)

SHA256:   bc85d71004e3cab6408000b6a5a011d9fd24614bdad07ace9f5edbb0c343c99c
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:37:53 (UTC)

# 0011 Security headers, CSP, origin check

Status: accepted

- CSP: `default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self'; font-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'`. No nonce is needed.
- `<ImportMap />` is left out because it emits an inline script. Modules load from fingerprinted URLs through `Assets[...]`.
- `Blazor.start` is called from `App.razor.js`. The reconnect UI is our own element. No markup uses `style` attributes.
- Also sent: `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, `Permissions-Policy` denying camera, microphone, geolocation, payment and USB, `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy`, `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy`, HSTS outside development. No `Server` header.
- Outbound listing links open in a new tab with `rel="noopener noreferrer"`.
- `/_blazor` requests with an `Origin` header must match the request host or `Nest:AllowedOrigins`.
- User text is only ever set with `textContent` or Razor encoding.


================================================================================
FILE:     docs/adr/0012-no-cookies.md
SIZE:     0.33 KB (334 bytes)
LINES:    5
SHA256:   ce8cc650a4381b91aabc25804fc0fa8bf7f19d12df164d8c37a75a8c39048d88
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:37:53 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0012 No cookies

Status: accepted

Blazor issues an antiforgery cookie on pages that host interactive components. Nothing posts forms to the server, so `CookielessAntiforgery` replaces `IAntiforgery`: it issues no tokens and rejects unsafe form posts. Data protection keys are in memory. With no cookies there is no consent banner.


================================================================================
FILE:     docs/adr/0013-theme.md
SIZE:     0.38 KB (384 bytes)
LINES:    7
SHA256:   1d2caf673b1476606f7110fc4d3712d87734c57754e7197059a20849be959dee
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:37:53 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0013 Theme without a flash

Status: accepted

- Colors are `light-dark()` tokens on `:root`. `data-theme="light"` or `"dark"` on `<html>` forces the scheme; without it the OS setting wins.
- `theme-init.js` is a blocking external script in `<head>` that applies the stored choice before first paint, so the CSP needs no hash.
- `App.razor.js` reapplies the theme on `enhancedload`.


================================================================================
FILE:     docs/adr/0014-reconnect-and-cold-start.md
SIZE:     0.59 KB (602 bytes)
LINES:    9
SHA256:   73e013201d39f26fe8c4c0c97fc1d1de65b1787c9494e59a0a1d87dada50b847
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:37:53 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0014 Reconnect, backoff, cold start

Status: accepted

- The free plan sleeps after 15 idle minutes and restarts without warning.
- Retries: first at once, then exponential with equal jitter, capped at 30 s, 30 attempts.
- `ReconnectBanner` shows "Reconnecting", then "Waking up the server", then a retry button. Retrying also happens when the tab becomes visible.
- A rejected reconnect means the server lost the circuit, so the page reloads and rebuilds from the server and IndexedDB.
- While not connected, pages show what this browser has, the status line says so, and send buttons are disabled.


================================================================================
FILE:     docs/adr/0015-runtime-footprint.md
SIZE:     0.65 KB (664 bytes)
LINES:    10
SHA256:   6a0ad7e10587148c8e2f65bcfab3b44278fa9c71179e2e1c390794e83ffe5125
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:37:53 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0015 Memory and CPU settings

Status: accepted

- Workstation, non-concurrent GC, `ConserveMemory=7`, no tiered PGO, invariant globalization, English resources only.
- Disconnected circuits: 20 kept for one minute. Two unacknowledged render batches per circuit.
- WebSocket compression is off. Response compression (Brotli, gzip) at the fastest level.
- No ReadyToRun and no trimming: velixir runs a portable `dotnet publish`, and Blazor Server is not trim-safe.
- The server's home list is computed once per change of its entries and cached.
- Logs at `Warning`, except host lifetime messages. `/health` is exempt from rate limiting. Shutdown waits up to 10 s.

FILE:     docs/adr/0016-testing.md
SIZE:     0.96 KB (980 bytes)
LINES:    9
SHA256:   75fbecb4662861a2729b142ed89ffb08bb3ea178a0d9279dfac7afba0f4d2b30
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:37:53 (UTC)
================================================================================

# 0016 Test strategy

Status: accepted

- .NET: xUnit v3 (`xunit.v3` 4.x) on Microsoft Testing Platform, enabled in `global.json`. Unit tests cover keys, links, fields, entry rules, the merge, the service and infrastructure. Integration tests use `WebApplicationFactory` for pages, headers, cookies, the hub guard and compression.
- JavaScript: Vitest in Node tests the pure exports and the `Nest` sync class with a fake server and store. DOM and IndexedDB are covered by Playwright. Vitest maps `/js/` imports to `wwwroot/js`.
- Shared fixtures in `tests/fixtures` are run by both suites, so C# and JavaScript parse links, addresses and fields, and merge homes, the same way.
- End to end: Playwright .NET in `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright/dotnet:v<version>-noble`. The version comes from `Directory.Packages.props` only. The app is published and run inside the container on localhost with relaxed limits. Tests run serially.
- Coverage is reported (Cobertura) with no threshold.

# 0017 Dependencies and versions

- Dependabot opens weekly grouped updates for NuGet, npm and GitHub Actions.

# 0018 AGPL and REUSE

- SPDX headers would be comments, which this project avoids. `REUSE.toml` declares the license and copyright for every file.
- AGPL section 13: every page links to the source in the footer, and the About page explains the license.

# 0019 Scripts and CI

Status: accepted

- All logic is in `scripts/`. Each script uses `set -euo pipefail`, can be rerun, and cleans its own output in `artifacts/`.
- `run-all.sh` runs build, unit, JavaScript, end-to-end and export, with `--skip-e2e`, `--skip-js` and `--skip-export`. Exit codes: 0 success, 2 usage, 127 missing tool, otherwise the failing step's code.
- Podman runs with `--security-opt label=disable`; the repository is mounted read-only and copied inside the container; NuGet packages are cached in a named volume.
- `ci.yml` runs `run-all.sh` on every push. `velixir.yml` deploys on every push to `main`.

FILE:     scripts/e2e-container.sh
SIZE:     1.35 KB (1387 bytes)
LINES:    54
SHA256:   68d910fb9c40ec117520cee556e3f2aca2caa347109a52a57fb92b3d4f0c1c9d

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

export DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT=1
export DOTNET_NOLOGO=1
export TESTINGPLATFORM_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT=1

port=5080
work=/work
app=/tmp/app

mkdir -p "$work"
tar -C /src \
    --exclude=./.git --exclude=./artifacts \
    --exclude='*/bin' --exclude='*/obj' --exclude='*/node_modules' \
    -cf - . | tar -C "$work" -xf -
cd "$work"

dotnet publish src/Nest.Web --configuration Release --output "$app"

env \
    ASPNETCORE_URLS="http://127.0.0.1:$port" \
    ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Production \
    Nest__PostBurst=1000 \
    Nest__PostsPerSecond=1000 \
    Nest__MaxConnectionsPerClient=50 \
    Nest__RequestBurst=100000 \
    Nest__RequestsPerSecond=100000 \
    dotnet "$app/Nest.Web.dll" >/out/app.log 2>&1 &
server=$!
trap 'kill "$server" 2>/dev/null || true' EXIT

for _ in $(seq 1 60); do
    if (exec 3<>"/dev/tcp/127.0.0.1/$port") 2>/dev/null; then
        break
    fi
    if ! kill -0 "$server" 2>/dev/null; then
        printf 'error: the app exited during startup\n' >&2
        cat /out/app.log >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    sleep 1
done

status=0
NEST_BASE_URL="http://127.0.0.1:$port/" \
    dotnet test --project tests/Nest.E2E --configuration Release --results-directory /out/results || status=$?

if [ "$status" -ne 0 ]; then
    printf '\n==> Last 50 lines of the app log\n' >&2
    tail -n 50 /out/app.log >&2
fi

exit "$status"

FILE:     scripts/export.sh
SIZE:     7.64 KB (7821 bytes)
LINES:    260
SHA256:   9ddae0823b8a703504ac990ee70959878d47992bbedee74dd009991d0018e0cd
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:13:16 (UTC)

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"

PROJECT_PATH="${1:-$SCRIPT_DIR/..}"
OUTPUT_FILE="${2:-docs/llm/dump.txt}"

INCLUDE_EXTENSIONS="cs csproj slnx props targets json razor js mjs css html svg yml yaml sh md toml"
INCLUDE_NAMES=".gitignore .gitattributes .editorconfig"
EXCLUDE_NAMES="package-lock.json LICENSE"
EXCLUDE_DIRS="bin obj .vs .git node_modules .vscode .idea TestResults artifacts coverage .velixir"
EXCLUDE_PREFIXES="docs/llm/"

PROJECT_PATH="$(cd "$PROJECT_PATH" && pwd)"
cd "$PROJECT_PATH"

case "$OUTPUT_FILE" in
    /*) OUTPUT_PATH="$OUTPUT_FILE" ;;
    *)  OUTPUT_PATH="$PROJECT_PATH/$OUTPUT_FILE" ;;
esac
OUTPUT_DIR="$(dirname "$OUTPUT_PATH")"
mkdir -p "$OUTPUT_DIR"

OUTPUT_REL="${OUTPUT_PATH#"$PROJECT_PATH"/}"

if [ -t 1 ]; then
    GREEN='\033[0;32m'; YELLOW='\033[0;33m'; CYAN='\033[0;36m'; NC='\033[0m'
else
    GREEN=''; YELLOW=''; CYAN=''; NC=''
fi
log() { printf "%b%s%b\n" "$1" "$2" "$NC"; }

sha256_of() {
    if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        sha256sum "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
    elif command -v shasum >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        shasum -a 256 "$1" | awk '{print $1}'
    else
        printf 'unavailable'
    fi
}

mod_time_utc_of() {
    local epoch
    if epoch="$(stat -c '%Y' "$1" 2>/dev/null)"; then
        date -u -d "@$epoch" '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'
    else
        epoch="$(stat -f '%m' "$1")"
        date -u -r "$epoch" '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'
    fi
}

line_count_of() {
    awk 'END { print NR }' "$1"
}

is_included_file() {
    local name="${1##*/}" ext entry
    for entry in $EXCLUDE_NAMES; do
        [ "$name" = "$entry" ] && return 1
    done
    case "$name" in
        .env|.env.*) return 1 ;;
    esac
    for entry in $INCLUDE_NAMES; do
        [ "$name" = "$entry" ] && return 0
    done
    ext="${name##*.}"
    [ "$ext" != "$name" ] || return 1
    ext="$(printf '%s' "$ext" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')"
    for entry in $INCLUDE_EXTENSIONS; do
        [ "$ext" = "$entry" ] && return 0
    done
    return 1
}

is_excluded_path() {
    local path="$1" entry
    for entry in $EXCLUDE_PREFIXES; do
        case "$path" in
            "$entry"*) return 0 ;;
        esac
    done
    for entry in $EXCLUDE_DIRS; do
        case "$path" in
            "$entry"/*|*/"$entry"/*) return 0 ;;
        esac
    done
    return 1
}

is_text() {
    [ ! -s "$1" ] || grep -Iq . "$1"
}

in_git_repo() { git rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree >/dev/null 2>&1; }

render_tree() {
    local -a parts prev
    local rel n common i j indent
    prev=()
    echo "."
    while IFS= read -r -d '' rel; do
        IFS='/' read -r -d '' -a parts < <(printf '%s\0' "$rel") || true
        n=${#parts[@]}

        common=0
        while [ "$common" -lt $((n - 1)) ] && [ "$common" -lt "${#prev[@]}" ] \
              && [ "${parts[$common]}" = "${prev[$common]}" ]; do
            common=$((common + 1))
        done

        for ((i = common; i < n; i++)); do
            indent="    "
            for ((j = 0; j < i; j++)); do indent="$indent    "; done
            if [ "$i" -lt $((n - 1)) ]; then
                printf '%s%s/\n' "$indent" "${parts[$i]}"
            else
                printf '%s%s\n' "$indent" "${parts[$i]}"
            fi
        done

        if [ "$n" -gt 1 ]; then
            prev=("${parts[@]:0:$((n - 1))}")
        else
            prev=()
        fi
    done < "$FILE_LIST"
}

log "$GREEN" "Starting project export..."
log "$YELLOW" "Project Path: $PROJECT_PATH"
log "$YELLOW" "Output File:  $OUTPUT_PATH"

RAW_LIST="$(mktemp)"
FILE_LIST="$(mktemp)"
TMP_OUTPUT=""
cleanup() { rm -f "$RAW_LIST" "$FILE_LIST" ${TMP_OUTPUT:+"$TMP_OUTPUT"}; }
trap cleanup EXIT

if in_git_repo; then
    log "$CYAN" "Listing files via git..."
    git ls-files -z --cached --others --exclude-standard > "$RAW_LIST"
else
    log "$YELLOW" "Not a git repo; falling back to find..."
    find . -type f -print0 > "$RAW_LIST"
fi

while IFS= read -r -d '' file; do
    rel="${file#./}"
    [ "$rel" = "$OUTPUT_REL" ] && continue
    is_excluded_path "$rel" && continue
    is_included_file "$rel" || continue
    [ -f "$rel" ] || continue
    is_text "$rel" || continue
    printf '%s\0' "$rel"
done < "$RAW_LIST" | LC_ALL=C sort -z -u > "$FILE_LIST"

FILE_COUNT=0
while IFS= read -r -d '' _; do FILE_COUNT=$((FILE_COUNT + 1)); done < "$FILE_LIST"
log "$GREEN" "Found $FILE_COUNT files to export"

GIT_COMMIT="n/a"; GIT_BRANCH="n/a"; GIT_DIRTY="n/a"
if in_git_repo; then
    commit="$(git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD 2>/dev/null || true)"
    [ -n "$commit" ] && GIT_COMMIT="$commit"
    branch="$(git branch --show-current 2>/dev/null || true)"
    [ -n "$branch" ] && GIT_BRANCH="$branch"

    status_args=(status --porcelain -- . ":(exclude)$OUTPUT_REL")
    if [ -n "$(git "${status_args[@]}" 2>/dev/null || true)" ]; then
        GIT_DIRTY="yes (uncommitted changes are included below)"
    else
        GIT_DIRTY="no"
    fi
fi
DOTNET_VERSION="$(dotnet --version 2>/dev/null || echo 'not installed')"

TMP_OUTPUT="$(mktemp "$OUTPUT_DIR/.export.XXXXXX")"

{
    echo "==============================================================================="
    echo "PROJECT EXPORT"
    echo "Generated (UTC): $(date -u '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')"
    echo "Project Path:    $PROJECT_PATH"
    echo "Git Commit:      $GIT_COMMIT"
    echo "Git Branch:      $GIT_BRANCH"
    echo "Git Dirty:       $GIT_DIRTY"
    echo ".NET SDK:        $DOTNET_VERSION"
    echo "Files Exported:  $FILE_COUNT"
    echo "==============================================================================="
    echo
    echo "DIRECTORY STRUCTURE (exported files only):"
    echo "=========================================="
    echo
    render_tree
    printf '\n\n'
    echo "FILE CONTENTS:"
    echo "=============="
    echo
} > "$TMP_OUTPUT"

TOTAL_BYTES=0
CURRENT=0
while IFS= read -r -d '' rel; do
    CURRENT=$((CURRENT + 1))
    full="$PROJECT_PATH/$rel"

    size="$(wc -c < "$full" | tr -d ' ')"
    lines="$(line_count_of "$full")"
    size_kb="$(awk "BEGIN {printf \"%.2f\", $size / 1024}")"
    hash="$(sha256_of "$full")"
    modified="$(mod_time_utc_of "$full")"
    TOTAL_BYTES=$((TOTAL_BYTES + size))

    log "$CYAN" "Processing ($CURRENT/$FILE_COUNT): $rel"

    {
        echo "================================================================================"
        echo "FILE:     $rel"
        echo "SIZE:     ${size_kb} KB (${size} bytes)"
        echo "LINES:    $lines"
        echo "SHA256:   $hash"
        echo "MODIFIED: $modified (UTC)"
        echo "================================================================================"
        echo
    } >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"

    if [ -s "$full" ]; then
        cat "$full" >> "$TMP_OUTPUT" 2>/dev/null || echo "[ERROR READING FILE]" >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"
    else
        echo "[EMPTY FILE]" >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"
    fi

    printf '\n\n' >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"
done < "$FILE_LIST"

TOTAL_MB="$(awk "BEGIN {printf \"%.2f\", $TOTAL_BYTES / 1048576}")"

{
    echo "==============================================================================="
    echo "EXPORT COMPLETED (UTC): $(date -u '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')"
    echo "Total Files Exported:   $FILE_COUNT"
    echo "Total Source Size:      ${TOTAL_MB} MB (${TOTAL_BYTES} bytes)"
    echo "Output File:            $OUTPUT_PATH"
    echo "==============================================================================="
} >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"

DUMP_HASH="$(sha256_of "$TMP_OUTPUT")"
echo "DUMP SHA256 (of all lines above this one): $DUMP_HASH" >> "$TMP_OUTPUT"

chmod 0644 "$TMP_OUTPUT"
mv -f "$TMP_OUTPUT" "$OUTPUT_PATH"
TMP_OUTPUT=""

log "$GREEN" ""
log "$GREEN" "Export completed successfully!"
log "$YELLOW" "Output file: $OUTPUT_PATH"
log "$CYAN" "Total source size: ${TOTAL_MB} MB across ${FILE_COUNT} files"

step() {
    printf '\n==> %s\n' "$*"
}

require() {
    local tool
    for tool in "$@"; do
        if ! command -v "$tool" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
            printf 'error: %s is required but was not found on PATH\n' "$tool" >&2
            exit 127
        fi
    done
}

playwright_version() {
    local version
    version="$(sed -n 's/.*Include="Microsoft.Playwright" Version="\([^"]*\)".*/\1/p' "$ROOT/Directory.Packages.props")"
    if [ -z "$version" ]; then
        printf 'error: no Microsoft.Playwright version in Directory.Packages.props\n' >&2
        exit 1
    fi
    printf '%s\n' "$version"
}

coverage_percent() {
    local rate
    rate="$(sed -n 's/.*<coverage[^>]* line-rate="\([0-9.]*\)".*/\1/p' "$1" | head -n 1)"
    if [ -n "$rate" ]; then
        awk -v rate="$rate" 'BEGIN { printf "%.1f%%\n", rate * 100 }'
    else
        printf 'unknown\n'
    fi
}

export const THEME_KEY = 'nest-theme';
export const MAX_RETRIES = 30;
export const RECONNECTED_EVENT = 'nest:reconnected';

export const VIEWS = Object.freeze({
    homes: './Pages/HomesPage.razor.js',
    listing: './Pages/ListingPage.razor.js',
});

export function storedTheme(storage) {
    try {
        const value = storage?.getItem(THEME_KEY);
        return value === 'light' || value === 'dark' ? value : null;
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

export function toggledTheme(current, prefersDark) {
    const effective = current ?? (prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light');
    return effective === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
}

export function reconnectDelay(attempt, random = Math.random) {
    if (attempt <= 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    const ceiling = Math.min(30000, 1000 * 2 ** (attempt - 1));
    return Math.round(ceiling / 2 + random() * ceiling / 2);
}

export function viewId(root) {
    return root ? `${root.dataset.view}|${root.dataset.key ?? ''}` : null;
}

export class ViewHost {
    constructor(win, load = path => import(path), shared = () => import('../js/sync.js')) {
        this.win = win;
        this.load = load;
        this.shared = shared;
        this.current = null;
        this.generation = 0;
    }

    async mount() {
        const root = this.win.document.querySelector('[data-view]');
        const id = viewId(root);
        if (this.current && this.current.id === id && this.current.root === root) {
            return;
        }
        this.generation += 1;
        const generation = this.generation;
        this.current?.view.dispose();
        this.current = null;
        const path = root ? VIEWS[root.dataset.view] : undefined;
        if (!path) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            const [{ nest }, module] = await Promise.all([this.shared(), this.load(path)]);
            if (generation !== this.generation) {
                return;
            }
            this.current = { id, root, view: module.mount(root, nest(this.win), this.win) };
        } catch {
        }
    }
}

export function boot(win) {
    const doc = win.document;
    const darkQuery = win.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');

    const applyTheme = theme => {
        if (theme) {
            doc.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
        } else {
            delete doc.documentElement.dataset.theme;
        }
        const dark = theme ? theme === 'dark' : darkQuery.matches;
        for (const button of doc.querySelectorAll('[data-theme-toggle]')) {
            button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(dark));
        }
    };

    const applyStoredTheme = () => applyTheme(storedTheme(win.localStorage));

    const toggleTheme = () => {
        const next = toggledTheme(doc.documentElement.dataset.theme ?? null, darkQuery.matches);
        try {
            win.localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, next);
        } catch {
        }
        applyTheme(next);
    };

    const retryWhenVisible = () => {
        if (doc.visibilityState === 'visible') {
            retry();
        }
    };

    const retry = async () => {
        doc.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', retryWhenVisible);
        try {
            if (await win.Blazor.reconnect()) {
                return;
            }
            if (await win.Blazor.resumeCircuit()) {
                return;
            }
            win.location.reload();
        } catch {
            doc.addEventListener('visibilitychange', retryWhenVisible);
        }
    };

    const resume = async () => {
        try {
            if (await win.Blazor.resumeCircuit()) {
                return;
            }
        } catch {
        }
        win.location.reload();
    };

    const clearLocal = async button => {
        const output = doc.getElementById(button.dataset.clearLocal);
        button.disabled = true;
        try {
            const [{ DB_NAME, deleteDatabase, draftKeys }, { current }, { MINE_KEY }] = await Promise.all([
                import('../js/store.js'),
                import('../js/sync.js'),
                import('./Pages/HomesPage.razor.js'),
            ]);
            await current()?.clear();
            const outcome = await deleteDatabase(win.indexedDB, DB_NAME);
            for (const key of [...draftKeys(win.localStorage), MINE_KEY]) {
                try {
                    win.localStorage.removeItem(key);
                } catch {
                }
            }
            if (output) {
                output.textContent = outcome === 'blocked'
                    ? 'Close any other Nest tabs to finish clearing.'
                    : 'This browser\'s copy is gone. Other browsers keep theirs.';
            }
        } catch {
            if (output) {
                output.textContent = 'Could not clear this browser\'s copy.';
            }
        } finally {
            button.disabled = false;
        }
    };

    const actions = {
        themeToggle: toggleTheme,
        reconnectRetry: retry,
        reconnectResume: resume,
        reconnectReload: () => win.location.reload(),
        clearLocal,
    };

    doc.addEventListener('click', event => {
        const button = event.target instanceof win.Element ? event.target.closest('button') : null;
        if (!button) {
            return;
        }
        for (const [name, action] of Object.entries(actions)) {
            if (name in button.dataset) {
                action(button);
                return;
            }
        }
    });

    doc.getElementById('components-reconnect-modal')?.addEventListener('components-reconnect-state-changed', event => {
        const state = event.detail?.state;
        if (state === 'rejected') {
            win.location.reload();
        } else if (state === 'hide') {
            win.dispatchEvent(new win.CustomEvent(RECONNECTED_EVENT));
        } else if (state === 'failed') {
            doc.addEventListener('visibilitychange', retryWhenVisible);
        }
    });

    darkQuery.addEventListener('change', applyStoredTheme);
    applyStoredTheme();

    const views = new ViewHost(win);
    views.mount();

    const afterNavigation = () => {
        applyStoredTheme();
        const announcer = doc.getElementById('announcer');
        if (announcer) {
            announcer.textContent = '';
        }
        views.mount();
    };

    return win.Blazor.start({
        circuit: {
            reconnectionOptions: {
                maxRetries: MAX_RETRIES,
                retryIntervalMilliseconds: attempt => reconnectDelay(attempt),
            },
        },
    }).then(() => win.Blazor.addEventListener('enhancedload', afterNavigation));
}

if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.Blazor) {
    boot(window);
}

@using System.Text.Json
@implements IAsyncDisposable
@inject NestService Service
@inject IJSRuntime JS
@inject ILogger<SyncIsland> Logger

@code {
    private readonly TaskCompletionSource _attached = new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
    private DotNetObjectReference<SyncIsland>? _self;
    private IJSObjectReference? _module;
    private IJSObjectReference? _client;
    private Outbox<NestUpdate>? _outbox;
    private Subscriber? _subscriber;
    private int _offered;

    [Parameter, EditorRequired]
    public string Client { get; set; } = ClientAddress.Unknown;

    protected override async Task OnAfterRenderAsync(bool firstRender)
    {
        if (!firstRender)
        {
            return;
        }

        _self = DotNetObjectReference.Create(this);
        _module = await JS.InvokeAsync<IJSObjectReference>("import", ModulePath.Of(Assets["Components/Islands/SyncIsland.razor.js"]));
        _client = await _module.InvokeAsync<IJSObjectReference>("attach", _self);
        _attached.TrySetResult();
    }

    [JSInvokable]
    public async Task Join()
    {
        await StopAsync();
        var outbox = new Outbox<NestUpdate>(64, 16, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(20), SendAsync, Logger);
        _outbox = outbox;
        _subscriber = new Subscriber(outbox);
        Service.Subscribe(_subscriber);
    }

    [JSInvokable]
    public bool Ping() => _subscriber is not null && _outbox is { IsFaulted: false } && Service.IsSubscribed(_subscriber);

    [JSInvokable]
    public JsonElement Fetch(string[]? ids)
    {
        var parsed = new List<Guid>();
        foreach (var id in (ids ?? []).Take(NestService.MaxFetch))
        {
            if (Guid.TryParseExact(id, "D", out var guid))
            {
                parsed.Add(guid);
            }
        }

        return Wire.Write(Service.Find(parsed));
    }

    [JSInvokable]
    public int Offer(JsonElement entries)
    {
        var wires = Wire.Read<EntryWire?[]>(entries);
        var budget = Math.Min(Service.Capacity - _offered, wires?.Length ?? 0);
        if (wires is null || budget <= 0)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        _offered += budget;
        var offered = new List<Entry>(budget);
        for (var i = 0; i < budget; i++)
        {
            if (wires[i]?.ToEntry() is { } entry)
            {
                offered.Add(entry);
            }
        }

        return Service.Offer(offered);
    }

    [JSInvokable]
    public JsonElement Post(JsonElement draft) =>
        Wire.Write(PostOutcome.From(Service.Submit(Wire.Read<EntryDraft>(draft), Client)));

    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        await StopAsync();
        await JsCleanup.DisposeAsync(_client, "dispose");
        await JsCleanup.DisposeAsync(_module, null);
        _self?.Dispose();
    }

    private async Task SendAsync(IReadOnlyList<NestUpdate> batch, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        await _attached.Task.WaitAsync(cancellationToken);
        await InvokeAsync(() => _client is null
            ? Task.FromException(new InvalidOperationException("The sync module is not attached."))
            : _client.InvokeVoidAsync("apply", cancellationToken, batch).AsTask());
    }

    private async Task StopAsync()
    {
        if (_subscriber is not null)
        {
            Service.Unsubscribe(_subscriber);
            _subscriber = null;
        }

        if (_outbox is not null)
        {
            await _outbox.DisposeAsync();
            _outbox = null;
        }
    }

    private sealed class Subscriber(Outbox<NestUpdate> outbox) : INestSubscriber
    {
        public bool Deliver(NestUpdate update) => outbox.TryPost(update);
    }
}

import { nest } from '../../js/sync.js';

export function attach(dotnet) {
    const shared = nest(window);
    shared.connect(dotnet);
    return {
        apply: updates => shared.apply(updates, dotnet),
        dispose: () => shared.disconnect(dotnet),
    };
}

@page "/about"

<PageTitle>About Nest</PageTitle>

<h1>About</h1>
<p class="lede">Nest is a shared notebook for house hunting. Paste links to homes, fill in what you know about them and talk about them. There are no accounts and the server keeps very little. Each browser keeps a copy of what it has seen and hands it back when the server has forgotten.</p>

<h2>Why it exists</h2>
<p>Looking for a home with other people means sending links back and forth across apps and tabs, and losing track of who said what about which house. Nest gives every home one page that anyone with the link to this site can add to.</p>
<p>It is also a demonstration: a plain Blazor Server app on a free hosting plan with a quarter of a CPU and 256 MB of memory, no database, no cookies and no third-party code. The HTML, CSS and JavaScript are all written by hand.</p>

<h2>What you can do</h2>
<ul>
    <li><strong>Add a home</strong> by pasting a link from Zillow, Redfin, Realtor.com or any other site, or by typing its street address and ZIP code. Text shared from another app works too; Nest finds the link inside it.</li>
    <li><strong>Fill in details</strong>: price, status, type, bedrooms, bathrooms, square feet, lot size, year built, HOA, taxes, parking, heating, MLS number and the address. Only what you change is sent.</li>
    <li><strong>Leave comments</strong> of up to 4,000 characters, emoji included. Unfinished comments are kept in this browser until you send them.</li>
    <li><strong>Add more links</strong> to the same home, from other sites.</li>
    <li><strong>Filter</strong> the list by street, city, ZIP code or ID, or show only the homes you added to.</li>
</ul>

<h2>How the same home is recognized</h2>
<p>Every home is known by one or more IDs:</p>
<ul>
    <li>A Zillow ID (<code>zpid</code>), Redfin ID or Realtor.com property ID, read from the link. Query strings, tracking codes and the address part of the link are ignored, so <code>zillow.com/homedetails/2893-State-Route-26-…/29747113_zpid/</code> and <code>zillow.com/homedetails/29747113_zpid/?utm_source=share</code> are the same home.</li>
    <li>The street address and ZIP code, in a standard form: <code>Street</code> and <code>St</code>, <code>Route</code> and <code>Rte</code>, <code>Apt 4</code> and <code>#4</code> all match.</li>
    <li>For other sites, the link itself without its query string.</li>
</ul>
<p>Everything anyone adds is filed under all the IDs its home had at that moment. Anything that shares an ID with anything else ends up on the same page. So if one person adds a Zillow link and its address, and later someone else adds a Redfin link with the same address, both appear on one page, with every detail and comment from both.</p>
<p>When two people set the same detail, the newest value wins and the one before it is shown next to it. Comments are never merged away; they are listed oldest first.</p>
<p>Merging cannot be undone. A wrong link or address joins two homes for everyone who has seen it.</p>

<h2>How it works</h2>
<ul>
    <li>Every addition gets a UUIDv7 from the server clock, so its ID also records when it happened. Anything that claims to come from the future is rejected.</li>
    <li>The server keeps the newest 1,000 additions in memory and forgets everything when it restarts, which can happen at any time.</li>
    <li>Your browser keeps up to 20,000 additions in IndexedDB, oldest removed first. When you open Nest, it asks the server what it has, downloads what it is missing and offers back what the server has forgotten. The server takes back older additions after a restart only if they are already in the standard form and fit among its newest 1,000.</li>
    <li>A solid border means the server still has it. A dotted border means only browsers have it now.</li>
    <li>Pages are rendered on the server and arrive as plain HTML. The home list and each home's page open one live connection, over a WebSocket, so changes appear for everyone at once.</li>
    <li>Nest never contacts Zillow, Redfin or any other site. It only reads the links you paste. Every detail comes from the people using it and is not checked.</li>
    <li>A strict Content Security Policy only allows scripts, styles and connections from this site, and there are no inline scripts.</li>
</ul>

<h2>Before you post</h2>
<p class="warning">Anything you add goes to every connected browser at once and is stored on their devices. It can't be edited, deleted or recalled. Nothing is moderated. Don't share anything private.</p>

<h2>Your data</h2>
<p>There are no accounts, no cookies and no tracking. Your theme, the homes filter and unfinished comments are kept in this browser's local storage; everything else in its IndexedDB. What you added is marked as yours only in this browser. Rate limits count by network address in the server's memory only; addresses are never saved or shown.</p>
<p class="clear">
    <button type="button" data-clear-local="clear-status">Delete this browser's copy</button>
    <output id="clear-status"></output>
</p>
<p>This deletes the homes, details, comments and drafts kept on this device. It does not delete anything from the server or from anyone else's browser. The next time you open Nest, this browser fills up again from the server.</p>

<h2>Source code</h2>
<p>The code is on <a href="https://github.com/kusl/nest">GitHub</a> under the GNU Affero General Public License, version 3 or later. If you run a modified copy for other people, you have to offer them its source too.</p>

FILE:     src/Nest.Web/Components/Pages/HomesPage.razor.js
SIZE:     9.94 KB (10175 bytes)
LINES:    294
SHA256:   ad0d09fb1dfdd6211a959d541e2618245b707e75faaa1f353fff5a9b58c806a2

import { addressKey, keyPath, parseLink, providerLabel } from '../../js/keys.js';
import { ADDRESS_FIELDS, normalizeField, placeOf, summaryOf } from '../../js/fields.js';
import { findListing, matches, timestampOf } from '../../js/merge.js';

export const PAGE_SIZE = 100;
export const MINE_KEY = 'nest-mine-only';

export const STATUS_TEXT = Object.freeze({
    local: 'Showing what this browser has. Connecting…',
    connecting: 'Connecting…',
    live: 'Live',
    offline: 'Offline. Showing what this browser has.',
});

export const RESULT_TEXT = Object.freeze({
    accepted: '',
    exists: '',
    empty: 'Fill in the street address and ZIP code.',
    invalid: 'That doesn\'t look like a listing link or an address.',
    tooFast: 'Slow down. Try again in a moment.',
    repeat: 'That was just added.',
    offline: 'Not connected to the server yet. Try again in a moment.',
});

export function timeLabel(id, format) {
    const at = new Date(timestampOf(id));
    try {
        return format ? format.format(at) : at.toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' ');
    } catch {
        return at.toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' ');
    }
}

export function isoOf(id) {
    return new Date(timestampOf(id)).toISOString();
}

export function previewText(parsed, local) {
    if (!parsed) {
        return '';
    }
    const what = parsed.provider === 'url' ? 'A link from another site' : `${providerLabel(parsed.provider)} home ${parsed.key.slice(parsed.key.indexOf(':') + 1).toUpperCase()}`;
    return local ? `${what}. Already here: ${local.title}.` : `${what}.`;
}

export function visibleListings(listings, query, mineOnly) {
    return listings.filter(listing => (!mineOnly || listing.mine) && matches(listing, query));
}

export function commentsLabel(count) {
    return count === 1 ? '1 comment' : `${count} comments`;
}

export function readMineOnly(storage) {
    try {
        return storage?.getItem(MINE_KEY) === 'on';
    } catch {
        return false;
    }
}

export function writeMineOnly(storage, on) {
    try {
        if (on) {
            storage?.setItem(MINE_KEY, 'on');
        } else {
            storage?.removeItem(MINE_KEY);
        }
    } catch {
    }
}

export function navigate(win, path) {
    if (typeof win.Blazor?.navigateTo === 'function') {
        win.Blazor.navigateTo(path);
    } else {
        win.location.assign(path);
    }
}

export function cardElement(doc, listing, live, format) {
    const item = doc.createElement('li');
    item.className = 'home';
    item.classList.toggle('archived', !live);
    item.classList.toggle('mine', listing.mine);
    item.dataset.key = listing.key;
    const link = doc.createElement('a');
    link.href = keyPath(listing.key);
    const title = doc.createElement('strong');
    title.className = 'title';
    title.textContent = listing.title;
    link.append(title);
    const place = placeOf(listing.fields);
    if (place && place !== listing.title) {
        const span = doc.createElement('span');
        span.className = 'place';
        span.textContent = place;
        link.append(span);
    }
    const summary = summaryOf(listing.fields);
    if (summary) {
        const span = doc.createElement('span');
        span.className = 'summary';
        span.textContent = summary;
        link.append(span);
    }
    const meta = doc.createElement('span');
    meta.className = 'meta';
    const sources = [...new Set(listing.links.map(entry => providerLabel(entry.provider)))];
    for (const text of [sources.join(' · '), commentsLabel(listing.comments.length)]) {
        if (text) {
            const span = doc.createElement('span');
            span.textContent = text;
            meta.append(span);
        }
    }
    const time = doc.createElement('time');
    time.dateTime = isoOf(listing.updated);
    time.textContent = timeLabel(listing.updated, format);
    meta.append(time);
    link.append(meta);
    item.append(link);
    return item;
}

class HomesView {
    constructor(root, shared, win) {
        this.root = root;
        this.nest = shared;
        this.win = win;
        this.doc = root.ownerDocument;
        this.format = new win.Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' });
        this.limit = PAGE_SIZE;
        this.find = id => this.doc.getElementById(id);
        this.linkForm = this.find('add-link');
        this.linkInput = this.find('link-input');
        this.linkPreview = this.find('link-preview');
        this.addressForm = this.find('add-address');
        this.addressStatus = this.find('address-status');
        this.filter = this.find('homes-filter');
        this.mineOnly = this.find('homes-mine');
        this.list = this.find('homes-list');
        this.empty = this.find('homes-empty');
        this.more = this.find('homes-more');
        this.status = this.find('sync-status');
        this.busy = false;
        this.handlers = [
            [this.linkForm, 'submit', event => this.submitLink(event)],
            [this.linkInput, 'input', () => this.preview()],
            [this.addressForm, 'submit', event => this.submitAddress(event)],
            [this.filter, 'input', () => this.renderList(true)],
            [this.mineOnly, 'change', () => this.toggleMine()],
            [this.more, 'click', () => this.showMore()],
        ];
    }

    start() {
        for (const [element, type, handler] of this.handlers) {
            element?.addEventListener(type, handler);
        }
        if (this.mineOnly) {
            this.mineOnly.checked = readMineOnly(this.win.localStorage);
        }
        this.unlisten = this.nest.listen(() => this.render());
        this.render();
    }

    dispose() {
        this.unlisten?.();
        for (const [element, type, handler] of this.handlers) {
            element?.removeEventListener(type, handler);
        }
    }

    render() {
        if (this.status) {
            this.status.dataset.status = this.nest.status;
            this.status.textContent = STATUS_TEXT[this.nest.status] ?? '';
        }
        for (const button of this.root.querySelectorAll('form button[type="submit"]')) {
            button.disabled = this.busy || !this.nest.live;
        }
        if (this.nest.ready && (this.nest.live || this.nest.entries.size > 0)) {
            this.renderList(false);
        }
        this.preview();
    }

    renderList(resetLimit) {
        if (!this.list) {
            return;
        }
        if (resetLimit) {
            this.limit = PAGE_SIZE;
        }
        const listings = visibleListings(this.nest.listings(), this.filter?.value ?? '', this.mineOnly?.checked ?? false);
        const fragment = this.doc.createDocumentFragment();
        for (const listing of listings.slice(0, this.limit)) {
            fragment.append(cardElement(this.doc, listing, listing.ids.some(id => this.nest.isLive(id)), this.format));
        }
        this.list.replaceChildren(fragment);
        if (this.more) {
            this.more.hidden = listings.length <= this.limit;
        }
        if (this.empty) {
            this.empty.hidden = listings.length > 0;
            this.empty.textContent = this.nest.listings().length > 0 ? 'No homes match.' : 'No homes yet. Paste a link to start.';
        }
    }

    showMore() {
        this.limit += PAGE_SIZE;
        this.renderList(false);
    }

    toggleMine() {
        writeMineOnly(this.win.localStorage, this.mineOnly.checked);
        this.renderList(true);
    }

    preview() {
        if (!this.linkPreview || !this.linkInput) {
            return;
        }
        const value = this.linkInput.value.trim();
        const parsed = parseLink(value);
        const local = parsed ? findListing(this.nest.listings(), parsed.key) : null;
        this.linkPreview.textContent = value && !parsed ? 'Paste a full link that starts with https://' : previewText(parsed, local);
    }

    async submitLink(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        const parsed = parseLink(this.linkInput?.value ?? '');
        if (!parsed) {
            this.linkPreview.textContent = RESULT_TEXT.invalid;
            return;
        }
        const local = findListing(this.nest.listings(), parsed.key);
        if (local) {
            navigate(this.win, keyPath(local.key));
            return;
        }
        await this.send({ kind: 'link', anchors: [], url: parsed.url }, this.linkPreview);
    }

    async submitAddress(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        const form = this.addressForm;
        const values = {};
        for (const key of ADDRESS_FIELDS) {
            const result = normalizeField(key, form.elements.namedItem(key)?.value ?? '');
            if (!result.ok) {
                this.addressStatus.textContent = result.error;
                return;
            }
            values[key] = result.value;
        }
        const key = addressKey(values.street, values.unit, values.zip);
        if (!key) {
            this.addressStatus.textContent = RESULT_TEXT.empty;
            return;
        }
        const local = findListing(this.nest.listings(), key);
        if (local) {
            navigate(this.win, keyPath(local.key));
            return;
        }
        const fields = ADDRESS_FIELDS.filter(name => values[name]).map(name => ({ key: name, value: values[name] }));
        await this.send({ kind: 'fact', anchors: [], fields }, this.addressStatus);
    }

    async send(draft, output) {
        this.busy = true;
        this.render();
        try {
            const outcome = await this.nest.post(draft);
            if ((outcome.result === 'accepted' || outcome.result === 'exists') && outcome.key) {
                output.textContent = '';
                navigate(this.win, keyPath(outcome.key));
                return;
            }
            output.textContent = RESULT_TEXT[outcome.result] ?? RESULT_TEXT.invalid;
        } finally {
            this.busy = false;
            this.render();
        }
    }
}

export function mount(root, shared, win) {
    const view = new HomesView(root, shared, win);
    view.start();
    return { dispose: () => view.dispose() };
}

                {

                }

@code {
    private static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> NoFields = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    private string? _key;
    private Listing? _listing;
    private IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> _fields = NoFields;
    private string _title = "";
    private string _place = "";
    private string _summary = "";
    private string _client = ClientAddress.Unknown;

    [Parameter]
    public string? Provider { get; set; }

    [Parameter]
    public string? Token { get; set; }

    [CascadingParameter]
    private HttpContext? HttpContext { get; set; }

    private string CountLabel => _listing is { Comments.Count: > 0 } listing
        ? string.Concat("(", listing.Comments.Count.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), ")")
        : "";

    protected override void OnParametersSet()
    {
        _key = Provider is null ? null : ListingKey.Of(Provider, Token);
        _client = ClientAddress.Key(HttpContext?.Connection.RemoteIpAddress);
        if (_key is null)
        {
            if (HttpContext is { Response.HasStarted: false } context)
            {
                context.Response.StatusCode = StatusCodes.Status404NotFound;
            }

            return;
        }

        _listing = Service.FindListing(_key);
        _fields = _listing?.Fields ?? NoFields;
        _title = _listing?.Title ?? ListingKey.Label(_key);
        _place = _listing is null || _listing.Place == _title ? "" : _listing.Place;
        _summary = _listing?.Summary ?? "";
    }

    private static string KeyText(string key)
    {
        var label = ListingKey.Label(key);
        return ListingKey.ProviderOf(key) == ListingKey.Address ? label : label[(label.IndexOf(' ', StringComparison.Ordinal) + 1)..];
    }
}

import { compareKeys, guessAddress, keyLabel, keyUrl, parseLink, providerLabel, providerOf } from '../../js/keys.js';
import { ADDRESS_FIELDS, FIELDS, changedFields, displayValue, placeOf, summaryOf } from '../../js/fields.js';
import { findListing } from '../../js/merge.js';
import { readDraft, writeDraft } from '../../js/store.js';
import { RESULT_TEXT, STATUS_TEXT, isoOf, timeLabel } from './HomesPage.razor.js';

export const COMMENT_MAX = 4000;

export function anchorsOf(listing, key) {
    const keys = listing ? [...listing.keys] : [key];
    return keys.sort(compareKeys).slice(0, 8);
}

export function previousValue(history, key, current) {
    const values = history?.[key] ?? [];
    for (let index = values.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
        if (values[index].value !== current && values[index].value !== '') {
            return values[index].value;
        }
    }
    return null;
}

export function addressSuggestion(listing) {
    if (!listing || listing.fields.street || listing.fields.zip) {
        return null;
    }
    for (const link of listing.links) {
        const guess = guessAddress(link.url);
        if (guess && (guess.street || guess.zip)) {
            return guess;
        }
    }
    return null;
}

export function countText(length, max) {
    return `${length} / ${max}`;
}

class ListingView {
    constructor(root, shared, win) {
        this.root = root;
        this.nest = shared;
        this.win = win;
        this.doc = root.ownerDocument;
        this.key = root.dataset.key;
        this.format = new win.Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' });
        this.find = id => this.doc.getElementById(id);
        this.title = this.find('listing-title');
        this.place = this.find('listing-place');
        this.summary = this.find('listing-summary');
        this.status = this.find('sync-status');
        this.missing = this.find('listing-missing');
        this.missingText = this.find('listing-missing-text');
        this.claim = this.find('listing-claim');
        this.links = this.find('listing-links');
        this.linkForm = this.find('add-more-link');
        this.linkInput = this.find('more-link-input');
        this.linkStatus = this.find('more-link-status');
        this.facts = this.find('listing-facts');
        this.factsEmpty = this.find('facts-empty');
        this.factsForm = this.find('facts-form');
        this.factsStatus = this.find('facts-status');
        this.suggestion = this.find('facts-suggestion');
        this.comments = this.find('comments');
        this.commentsEmpty = this.find('comments-empty');
        this.commentsCount = this.find('comments-count');
        this.commentForm = this.find('comment-form');
        this.commentText = this.find('comment-text');
        this.commentCount = this.find('comment-count');
        this.commentStatus = this.find('comment-status');
        this.keys = this.find('listing-keys');
        this.listing = null;
        this.shown = new Map();
        this.dirty = false;
        this.busy = false;
        this.seen = null;
        this.handlers = [
            [this.linkForm, 'submit', event => this.submitLink(event)],
            [this.factsForm, 'submit', event => this.submitFacts(event)],
            [this.factsForm, 'input', () => {
                this.dirty = true;
            }],
            [this.commentForm, 'submit', event => this.submitComment(event)],
            [this.commentText, 'input', () => this.commentInput()],
            [this.commentText, 'keydown', event => this.commentKey(event)],
            [this.claim, 'click', () => this.claimHome()],
        ];
    }

    start() {
        for (const [element, type, handler] of this.handlers) {
            element?.addEventListener(type, handler);
        }
        if (this.commentText && !this.commentText.value) {
            this.commentText.value = readDraft(this.win.localStorage, this.key);
        }
        this.commentInput(false);
        this.unlisten = this.nest.listen(() => this.render());
        this.render();
    }

    dispose() {
        this.unlisten?.();
        for (const [element, type, handler] of this.handlers) {
            element?.removeEventListener(type, handler);
        }
    }

    render() {
        if (this.status) {
            this.status.dataset.status = this.nest.status;
            this.status.textContent = STATUS_TEXT[this.nest.status] ?? '';
        }
        for (const button of this.root.querySelectorAll('form button[type="submit"]')) {
            button.disabled = this.busy || !this.nest.live;
        }
        if (this.claim) {
            this.claim.disabled = this.busy || !this.nest.live;
        }
        const listing = findListing(this.nest.listings(), this.key);
        if (!listing) {
            if (this.nest.ready && this.nest.live) {
                this.renderMissing();
            }
            return;
        }
        this.listing = listing;
        if (this.missing) {
            this.missing.hidden = true;
        }
        if (this.claim) {
            this.claim.hidden = true;
        }
        this.doc.title = `${listing.title} · Nest`;
        this.title.textContent = listing.title;
        this.setText(this.place, placeOf(listing.fields) === listing.title ? '' : placeOf(listing.fields));
        this.setText(this.summary, summaryOf(listing.fields));
        this.renderLinks(listing);
        this.renderFacts(listing);
        this.renderForm(listing);
        this.renderComments(listing);
        this.renderKeys(listing);
    }

    setText(element, text) {
        if (element) {
            element.textContent = text;
            element.hidden = text.length === 0;
        }
    }

    renderMissing() {
        if (this.missing) {
            this.missing.hidden = false;
            this.missingText.textContent = 'Nobody connected has this home right now, and this browser has never seen it.';
        }
        if (this.claim) {
            this.claim.hidden = keyUrl(this.key) === null;
        }
    }

    renderLinks(listing) {
        if (!this.links) {
            return;
        }
        const fragment = this.doc.createDocumentFragment();
        for (const link of listing.links) {
            const item = this.doc.createElement('li');
            const anchor = this.doc.createElement('a');
            anchor.href = link.url;
            anchor.target = '_blank';
            anchor.rel = 'noopener noreferrer';
            anchor.textContent = `Open on ${providerLabel(link.provider)}`;
            const where = this.doc.createElement('span');
            where.className = 'url';
            where.textContent = link.url.replace(/^https?:\/\//, '');
            item.append(anchor, where);
            fragment.append(item);
        }
        this.links.replaceChildren(fragment);
    }

    renderFacts(listing) {
        if (!this.facts) {
            return;
        }
        const fragment = this.doc.createDocumentFragment();
        for (const field of FIELDS) {
            const value = listing.fields[field.key];
            if (!value) {
                continue;
            }
            const row = this.doc.createElement('div');
            const term = this.doc.createElement('dt');
            term.textContent = field.label;
            const detail = this.doc.createElement('dd');
            detail.textContent = displayValue(field.key, value);
            const previous = previousValue(listing.history, field.key, value);
            if (previous !== null) {
                const was = this.doc.createElement('small');
                was.className = 'was';
                was.textContent = `was ${displayValue(field.key, previous)}`;
                detail.append(' ', was);
            }
            row.append(term, detail);
            fragment.append(row);
        }
        this.facts.replaceChildren(fragment);
        this.facts.hidden = this.facts.childElementCount === 0;
        if (this.factsEmpty) {
            this.factsEmpty.hidden = this.facts.childElementCount > 0;
        }
    }

    renderForm(listing) {
        if (!this.factsForm || this.dirty) {
            return;
        }
        const suggestion = addressSuggestion(listing);
        for (const field of FIELDS) {
            const input = this.factsForm.elements.namedItem(field.key);
            if (!input) {
                continue;
            }
            const suggested = suggestion && ADDRESS_FIELDS.includes(field.key) ? suggestion[field.key] ?? '' : '';
            input.value = listing.fields[field.key] ?? suggested;
        }
        if (this.suggestion) {
            this.suggestion.hidden = !suggestion;
            this.suggestion.textContent = suggestion
                ? suggestion.mixed
                    ? 'The address below comes from the link, which mixes the street and the city. Fix it, then save.'
                    : 'The address below comes from the link. Check it, then save.'
                : '';
        }
    }

    commentElement(comment, fresh) {
        const item = this.doc.createElement('li');
        item.className = 'comment';
        item.dataset.id = comment.id;
        item.classList.toggle('fresh', fresh);
        const head = this.doc.createElement('div');
        head.className = 'head';
        const time = this.doc.createElement('time');
        time.dateTime = isoOf(comment.id);
        time.textContent = timeLabel(comment.id, this.format);
        head.append(time);
        if (comment.mine) {
            const yours = this.doc.createElement('span');
            yours.className = 'yours';
            yours.textContent = 'You';
            head.append(yours);
        }
        const text = this.doc.createElement('p');
        text.className = 'text';
        text.textContent = comment.text;
        item.append(head, text);
        return item;
    }

    renderComments(listing) {
        if (!this.comments) {
            return;
        }
        const first = this.seen === null;
        const seen = this.seen ?? new Set();
        const next = new Map();
        const arrived = [];
        for (const comment of listing.comments) {
            let item = this.shown.get(comment.id);
            const mine = comment.mine === true;
            if (!item || item.classList.contains('mine') !== mine) {
                item = this.commentElement(comment, !first && !seen.has(comment.id));
                item.classList.toggle('mine', mine);
            }
            if (!first && !seen.has(comment.id) && !mine) {
                arrived.push(comment);
            }
            item.classList.toggle('archived', !this.nest.isLive(comment.id));
            next.set(comment.id, item);
            seen.add(comment.id);
        }
        this.comments.replaceChildren(...next.values());
        this.shown = next;
        this.seen = seen;
        if (this.commentsEmpty) {
            this.commentsEmpty.hidden = listing.comments.length > 0;
        }
        if (this.commentsCount) {
            this.commentsCount.textContent = listing.comments.length > 0 ? `(${listing.comments.length})` : '';
        }
        if (arrived.length > 0) {
            const announcer = this.doc.getElementById('announcer');
            if (announcer) {
                announcer.textContent = `New comment: ${arrived.at(-1).text.slice(0, 140)}`;
            }
        }
    }

    renderKeys(listing) {
        if (!this.keys) {
            return;
        }
        const fragment = this.doc.createDocumentFragment();
        for (const key of listing.keys) {
            const item = this.doc.createElement('li');
            const provider = this.doc.createElement('span');
            provider.className = 'provider';
            provider.textContent = providerLabel(providerOf(key));
            const value = this.doc.createElement('code');
            const label = keyLabel(key);
            value.textContent = providerOf(key) === 'address' ? label : label.slice(label.indexOf(' ') + 1);
            item.append(provider, ' ', value);
            fragment.append(item);
        }
        this.keys.replaceChildren(fragment);
    }

    commentInput(save = true) {
        if (!this.commentText) {
            return;
        }
        const length = this.commentText.value.length;
        if (this.commentCount) {
            this.commentCount.textContent = countText(length, COMMENT_MAX);
            this.commentCount.classList.toggle('full', length >= COMMENT_MAX);
        }
        if (save) {
            writeDraft(this.win.localStorage, this.key, this.commentText.value);
        }
    }

    commentKey(event) {
        if (event.key === 'Enter' && (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey)) {
            event.preventDefault();
            this.commentForm?.requestSubmit();
        }
    }

    async send(draft, output) {
        this.busy = true;
        this.render();
        try {
            const outcome = await this.nest.post(draft);
            const ok = outcome.result === 'accepted' || outcome.result === 'exists';
            if (output) {
                output.textContent = ok ? '' : RESULT_TEXT[outcome.result] ?? RESULT_TEXT.invalid;
            }
            return ok ? outcome : null;
        } finally {
            this.busy = false;
            this.render();
        }
    }

    anchors() {
        return anchorsOf(this.listing, this.key);
    }

    async submitComment(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        const text = this.commentText?.value ?? '';
        if (text.trim().length === 0) {
            this.commentStatus.textContent = 'Write something first.';
            return;
        }
        const outcome = await this.send({ kind: 'comment', anchors: this.anchors(), text }, this.commentStatus);
        if (outcome) {
            this.commentText.value = '';
            this.commentInput();
        }
    }

    async submitFacts(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        const inputs = {};
        for (const field of FIELDS) {
            const input = this.factsForm.elements.namedItem(field.key);
            if (input) {
                inputs[field.key] = input.value;
            }
        }
        for (const element of this.factsForm.querySelectorAll('.error')) {
            element.textContent = '';
        }
        const { changes, errors } = changedFields(this.listing?.fields ?? {}, inputs);
        if (errors.length > 0) {
            for (const { key, error } of errors) {
                const output = this.find(`fact-${key}-error`);
                if (output) {
                    output.textContent = error;
                }
            }
            this.factsStatus.textContent = 'Fix the highlighted values.';
            this.factsForm.elements.namedItem(errors[0].key)?.focus();
            return;
        }
        if (changes.length === 0) {
            this.factsStatus.textContent = 'Nothing changed.';
            return;
        }
        const outcome = await this.send({ kind: 'fact', anchors: this.anchors(), fields: changes }, this.factsStatus);
        if (outcome) {
            this.dirty = false;
            this.factsStatus.textContent = 'Saved.';
            this.render();
        }
    }

    async submitLink(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        const parsed = parseLink(this.linkInput?.value ?? '');
        if (!parsed) {
            this.linkStatus.textContent = 'Paste a full link that starts with https://';
            return;
        }
        if (this.listing?.keys.includes(parsed.key)) {
            this.linkStatus.textContent = 'This home already has that link.';
            return;
        }
        const outcome = await this.send({ kind: 'link', anchors: this.anchors(), url: parsed.url }, this.linkStatus);
        if (outcome) {
            this.linkInput.value = '';
            this.linkStatus.textContent = 'Link added.';
        }
    }

    async claimHome() {
        const url = keyUrl(this.key);
        if (url) {
            await this.send({ kind: 'link', anchors: [], url }, this.missingText);
        }
    }
}

export function mount(root, shared, win) {
    const view = new ListingView(root, shared, win);
    view.start();
    return { dispose: () => view.dispose() };
}
