using System.Net;
using System.Net.Sockets;

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static class ClientAddress
{
    public const string Unknown = "unknown";

    public static string Key(IPAddress? address)
    {
        if (address is null)
        {
            return Unknown;
        }

        if (address.IsIPv4MappedToIPv6)
        {
            address = address.MapToIPv4();
        }

        if (address.AddressFamily != AddressFamily.InterNetworkV6)
        {
            return address.ToString();
        }

        Span<byte> bytes = stackalloc byte[16];
        address.TryWriteBytes(bytes, out _);
        bytes[8..].Clear();
        return new IPAddress(bytes) + "/64";
    }
}

LINES:    93
SHA256:   7551d2f9e9ff7df788be2b6fefa38ecea5b3ff26f1402a927e20e20765962e9b

using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal enum ConnectionAdmission
{
    Admitted,
    ClientFull,
    ServerFull,
}

internal sealed class ConnectionTracker(IOptions<NestOptions> options)
{
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<string, int> _perClient = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    private int _total;

    public int Total
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _total;
            }
        }
    }

    public int CountFor(string client)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            return _perClient.GetValueOrDefault(client);
        }
    }

    public ConnectionAdmission TryAdmit(string client, out IDisposable? lease)
    {
        var limits = options.Value;
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (_total >= limits.MaxConnections)
            {
                lease = null;
                return ConnectionAdmission.ServerFull;
            }

            var count = _perClient.GetValueOrDefault(client);
            if (count >= limits.MaxConnectionsPerClient)
            {
                lease = null;
                return ConnectionAdmission.ClientFull;
            }

            _perClient[client] = count + 1;
            _total++;
        }

        lease = new Lease(this, client);
        return ConnectionAdmission.Admitted;
    }

    private void Release(string client)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            var count = _perClient.GetValueOrDefault(client) - 1;
            if (count > 0)
            {
                _perClient[client] = count;
            }
            else
            {
                _perClient.Remove(client);
            }

            _total--;
        }
    }

    private sealed class Lease(ConnectionTracker owner, string client) : IDisposable
    {
        private int _released;

        public void Dispose()
        {
            if (Interlocked.Exchange(ref _released, 1) == 0)
            {
                owner.Release(client);
            }
        }
    }
}

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Antiforgery;

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed class CookielessAntiforgery : IAntiforgery
{
    private static readonly AntiforgeryTokenSet NoTokens = new(null, null, "__RequestVerificationToken", null);

    public AntiforgeryTokenSet GetAndStoreTokens(HttpContext httpContext) => NoTokens;

    public AntiforgeryTokenSet GetTokens(HttpContext httpContext) => NoTokens;

    public Task<bool> IsRequestValidAsync(HttpContext httpContext)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(httpContext);
        return Task.FromResult(IsSafe(httpContext.Request.Method));
    }

    public Task ValidateRequestAsync(HttpContext httpContext)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(httpContext);
        return IsSafe(httpContext.Request.Method)
            ? Task.CompletedTask
            : Task.FromException(new AntiforgeryValidationException("This site accepts no form posts."));
    }

    public void SetCookieTokenAndHeader(HttpContext httpContext)
    {
    }

    private static bool IsSafe(string method) =>
        HttpMethods.IsGet(method) || HttpMethods.IsHead(method) || HttpMethods.IsOptions(method) || HttpMethods.IsTrace(method);
}

using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static partial class HubGuard
{
    public static readonly PathString HubPath = new("/_blazor");

    public static IApplicationBuilder UseHubGuard(this IApplicationBuilder app)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(app);
        var tracker = app.ApplicationServices.GetRequiredService<ConnectionTracker>();
        var options = app.ApplicationServices.GetRequiredService<IOptions<NestOptions>>();
        var logger = app.ApplicationServices.GetRequiredService<ILoggerFactory>().CreateLogger("Nest.Web.HubGuard");

        return app.Use(async (context, next) =>
        {
            if (!context.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments(HubPath, out var remainder))
            {
                await next(context);
                return;
            }

            if (!OriginPolicy.IsAllowed(context.Request.Headers.Origin, context.Request.Host.Value, options.Value.AllowedOrigins))
            {
                await RejectAsync(context, StatusCodes.Status403Forbidden, "Origin not allowed.");
                return;
            }

            if (remainder.HasValue)
            {
                await next(context);
                return;
            }

            if (!context.WebSockets.IsWebSocketRequest)
            {
                await RejectAsync(context, StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest, "Only WebSocket connections are accepted.");
                return;
            }

            var admission = tracker.TryAdmit(ClientAddress.Key(context.Connection.RemoteIpAddress), out var lease);
            if (admission is not ConnectionAdmission.Admitted)
            {
                if (admission is ConnectionAdmission.ServerFull)
                {
                    LogServerFull(logger, tracker.Total);
                }

                context.Response.Headers.RetryAfter = "10";
                var status = admission is ConnectionAdmission.ServerFull
                    ? StatusCodes.Status503ServiceUnavailable
                    : StatusCodes.Status429TooManyRequests;
                await RejectAsync(context, status, "Too many connections.");
                return;
            }

            using (lease)
            {
                await next(context);
            }
        });
    }

    private static Task RejectAsync(HttpContext context, int status, string message)
    {
        context.Response.StatusCode = status;
        context.Response.ContentType = "text/plain; charset=utf-8";
        return context.Response.WriteAsync(message, context.RequestAborted);
    }

    [LoggerMessage(Level = LogLevel.Warning, Message = "Refused a connection while holding {Connections} connections")]
    private static partial void LogServerFull(ILogger logger, int connections);
}

using Microsoft.JSInterop;

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static class JsCleanup
{
    public static async ValueTask DisposeAsync(IJSObjectReference? reference, string? method)
    {
        if (reference is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        try
        {
            if (method is not null)
            {
                await reference.InvokeVoidAsync(method).ConfigureAwait(false);
            }

            await reference.DisposeAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
        }
        catch (JSDisconnectedException)
        {
        }
        catch (JSException)
        {
        }
        catch (TaskCanceledException)
        {
        }
    }

    public static async ValueTask InvokeAsync(IJSObjectReference? reference, string method)
    {
        if (reference is null)
        {
            return;
        }

        try
        {
            await reference.InvokeVoidAsync(method).ConfigureAwait(false);
        }
        catch (JSDisconnectedException)
        {
        }
        catch (JSException)
        {
        }
        catch (TaskCanceledException)
        {
        }
    }
}

FILE:     src/Nest.Web/Infrastructure/KeyedTokenBuckets.cs
SIZE:     1.17 KB (1202 bytes)

SHA256:   c7d045e3c5bec878e0f3f271d1e2cae64e74431be52681e5a226cd83fee10d30

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed class KeyedTokenBuckets(double capacity, double perSecond, TimeProvider time, int maxKeys = 10_000)
{
    private readonly Dictionary<string, TokenBucket> _buckets = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();

    public int Count
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _buckets.Count;
            }
        }
    }

    public bool TryTake(string key, double count = 1)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (!_buckets.TryGetValue(key, out var bucket))
            {
                if (_buckets.Count >= maxKeys)
                {
                    Sweep();
                }

                bucket = new TokenBucket(capacity, perSecond, time);
                _buckets[key] = bucket;
            }

            return bucket.TryTake(count);
        }
    }

    private void Sweep()
    {
        foreach (var (key, bucket) in _buckets)
        {
            if (bucket.IsFull)
            {
                _buckets.Remove(key);
            }
        }

        if (_buckets.Count >= maxKeys)
        {
            _buckets.Clear();
        }
    }
}

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static class OriginPolicy
{
    public static bool IsAllowed(string? origin, string? host, IEnumerable<string> allowed)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(allowed);

        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(origin))
        {
            return true;
        }

        if (!Uri.TryCreate(origin, UriKind.Absolute, out var uri)
            || (uri.Scheme != Uri.UriSchemeHttps && uri.Scheme != Uri.UriSchemeHttp))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(host) && string.Equals(uri.Authority, host, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            return true;
        }

        var normalized = uri.GetLeftPart(UriPartial.Authority);
        return allowed.Any(entry => string.Equals(entry.TrimEnd('/'), normalized, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }
}

using System.Threading.Channels;

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed class Outbox<T> : IAsyncDisposable
{
    private readonly Channel<T> _channel;
    private readonly CancellationTokenSource _stop = new();
    private readonly Func<IReadOnlyList<T>, CancellationToken, Task> _send;
    private readonly int _maxBatch;
    private readonly TimeSpan _timeout;
    private readonly ILogger _logger;
    private readonly Task _pump;
    private volatile bool _faulted;

    public Outbox(int capacity, int maxBatch, TimeSpan timeout, Func<IReadOnlyList<T>, CancellationToken, Task> send, ILogger logger)
    {
        _channel = Channel.CreateBounded<T>(new BoundedChannelOptions(capacity)
        {
            SingleReader = true,
            FullMode = BoundedChannelFullMode.Wait,
        });
        _maxBatch = maxBatch;
        _timeout = timeout;
        _send = send;
        _logger = logger;
        _pump = Task.Run(PumpAsync);
    }

    public bool IsFaulted => _faulted;

    public bool TryPost(T item) => !_faulted && _channel.Writer.TryWrite(item);

    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        _channel.Writer.TryComplete();
        await _stop.CancelAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
        await _pump.ConfigureAwait(false);
        _stop.Dispose();
    }

    private async Task PumpAsync()
    {
        var batch = new List<T>(_maxBatch);
        try
        {
            while (await _channel.Reader.WaitToReadAsync(_stop.Token).ConfigureAwait(false))
            {
                while (batch.Count < _maxBatch && _channel.Reader.TryRead(out var item))
                {
                    batch.Add(item);
                }

                using var timeout = CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(_stop.Token);
                timeout.CancelAfter(_timeout);
                await _send([.. batch], timeout.Token).ConfigureAwait(false);
                batch.Clear();
            }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException) when (_stop.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            _faulted = true;
            _channel.Writer.TryComplete();
            OutboxLog.SendFailed(_logger, exception);
        }
    }
}

internal static partial class OutboxLog
{
    [LoggerMessage(Level = LogLevel.Debug, Message = "Realtime delivery stopped")]
    public static partial void SendFailed(ILogger logger, Exception exception);
}

using System.Threading.RateLimiting;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.RateLimiting;

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static class RequestLimits
{
    public static readonly PathString HealthPath = new("/health");

    public static void Configure(RateLimiterOptions limiter, NestOptions limits)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(limiter);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(limits);

        limiter.RejectionStatusCode = StatusCodes.Status429TooManyRequests;
        limiter.OnRejected = static async (context, cancellationToken) =>
        {
            context.HttpContext.Response.Headers.RetryAfter = "1";
            context.HttpContext.Response.ContentType = "text/plain; charset=utf-8";
            await context.HttpContext.Response.WriteAsync("Too many requests.", cancellationToken);
        };
        limiter.GlobalLimiter = PartitionedRateLimiter.Create<HttpContext, string>(context =>
            context.Request.Path.StartsWithSegments(HealthPath)
                ? RateLimitPartition.GetNoLimiter("health")
                : RateLimitPartition.GetTokenBucketLimiter(
                    ClientAddress.Key(context.Connection.RemoteIpAddress),
                    _ => new TokenBucketRateLimiterOptions
                    {
                        TokenLimit = limits.RequestBurst,
                        TokensPerPeriod = limits.RequestsPerSecond,
                        ReplenishmentPeriod = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1),
                        QueueLimit = 0,
                        AutoReplenishment = true,
                    }));
    }
}

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal static class SecurityHeaders
{
    public const string ContentSecurityPolicy =
        "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self'; font-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'self'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'";

    public const string PermissionsPolicy = "camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(), payment=(), usb=()";

    public static IApplicationBuilder UseSecurityHeaders(this IApplicationBuilder app) =>
        app.Use((context, next) =>
        {
            context.Response.OnStarting(Apply, context);
            return next(context);
        });

    private static Task Apply(object state)
    {
        var headers = ((HttpContext)state).Response.Headers;
        headers.ContentSecurityPolicy = ContentSecurityPolicy;
        headers.XContentTypeOptions = "nosniff";
        headers["Referrer-Policy"] = "no-referrer";
        headers["Permissions-Policy"] = PermissionsPolicy;
        headers["Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy"] = "same-origin";
        headers["Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy"] = "same-origin";
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
}

FILE:     src/Nest.Web/Infrastructure/TokenBucket.cs

SHA256:   bfb4de864a20442fb8e603467a28c3ec7055c148ac2c1058ffdbcaf40fdaff0a

namespace Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

internal sealed class TokenBucket
{
    private readonly double _capacity;
    private readonly double _perSecond;
    private readonly TimeProvider _time;
    private double _tokens;
    private long _stamp;

    public TokenBucket(double capacity, double perSecond, TimeProvider time)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(time);
        _capacity = capacity;
        _perSecond = perSecond;
        _time = time;
        _tokens = capacity;
        _stamp = time.GetTimestamp();
    }

    public bool IsFull
    {
        get
        {
            Refill();
            return _tokens >= _capacity;
        }
    }

    public bool TryTake(double count = 1)
    {
        Refill();
        if (_tokens < count)
        {
            return false;
        }

        _tokens -= count;
        return true;
    }

    private void Refill()
    {
        var now = _time.GetTimestamp();
        var elapsed = _time.GetElapsedTime(_stamp, now).TotalSeconds;
        _stamp = now;
        _tokens = Math.Min(_capacity, _tokens + (elapsed * _perSecond));
    }
}

using System.Globalization;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal static partial class CommentText
{
    public const int MaxLength = 4000;

    public static string Normalize(string? input, int maxLength = MaxLength)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input))
        {
            return "";
        }

        var text = string.Concat(input.ReplaceLineEndings("\n").Where(c => c == '\n' || !char.IsControl(c)));
        text = ExtraBlankLines().Replace(text, "\n\n").Trim();

        if (text.Length > maxLength)
        {
            var cut = 0;
            while (cut < text.Length)
            {
                var next = StringInfo.GetNextTextElementLength(text, cut);
                if (cut + next > maxLength)
                {
                    break;
                }

                cut += next;
            }

            text = text[..cut].TrimEnd();
        }

        return text.Any(IsVisible) ? text : "";
    }

    public static bool IsNormalized(string? text, int maxLength = MaxLength) =>
        !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && string.Equals(Normalize(text, maxLength), text, StringComparison.Ordinal);

    private static bool IsVisible(char c) =>
        !char.IsWhiteSpace(c) && char.GetUnicodeCategory(c) != UnicodeCategory.Format;

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\n{3,}")]
    private static partial Regex ExtraBlankLines();
}

LINES:    153
SHA256:   69718b44dfaaafe1cae8868df9ae093082f0494d9958c62de6fcebdd3b840be8

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal static class EntryRules
{
    public static Entry? Build(Guid id, string? kind, IEnumerable<string?>? anchors, string? url, IEnumerable<FieldWire?>? fields, string? text) => kind switch
    {
        EntryKinds.Link => BuildLink(id, anchors, url),
        EntryKinds.Fact => BuildFact(id, anchors, fields),
        EntryKinds.Comment => BuildComment(id, anchors, text),
        _ => null,
    };

    public static Entry? Build(Guid id, EntryDraft draft)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(draft);
        return Build(id, draft.Kind, draft.Anchors, draft.Url, draft.Fields, draft.Text);
    }

    public static string? DerivedKey(Entry entry)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(entry);
        return entry.Kind switch
        {
            EntryKinds.Link => ListingLink.Parse(entry.Url)?.Key,
            EntryKinds.Fact => AddressKey(entry.Fields),
            _ => null,
        };
    }

    public static bool IsNormalized(Entry? entry)
    {
        if (entry?.Keys is null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var fields = entry.Fields?.Select(field => new FieldWire(field.Key, field.Value)).ToArray();
        var rebuilt = Build(entry.Id, entry.Kind, entry.Keys, entry.Url, fields, entry.Text);
        return rebuilt is not null && Same(rebuilt, entry);
    }

    public static bool Same(Entry a, Entry b)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(a);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(b);
        return a.Id == b.Id
            && string.Equals(a.Kind, b.Kind, StringComparison.Ordinal)
            && a.Keys.SequenceEqual(b.Keys, StringComparer.Ordinal)
            && string.Equals(a.Url, b.Url, StringComparison.Ordinal)
            && string.Equals(a.Text, b.Text, StringComparison.Ordinal)
            && (a.Fields is null ? b.Fields is null : b.Fields is not null && a.Fields.SequenceEqual(b.Fields));
    }

    private static Entry? BuildLink(Guid id, IEnumerable<string?>? anchors, string? url)
    {
        var parsed = ListingLink.Parse(url);
        if (parsed is null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        return new Entry(id, EntryKinds.Link, ListingKey.Normalize(new[] { parsed.Key }, anchors), Url: parsed.Url);
    }

    private static Entry? BuildFact(Guid id, IEnumerable<string?>? anchors, IEnumerable<FieldWire?>? fields)
    {
        if (fields is null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var values = new string?[ListingFields.All.Count];
        foreach (var field in fields.Take(ListingFields.All.Count * 2))
        {
            if (field?.Key is not { } key)
            {
                return null;
            }

            var index = ListingFields.OrderOf(key);
            if (index < 0 || values[index] is not null)
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (!ListingFields.TryNormalize(key, field.Value, out var value))
            {
                return null;
            }

            values[index] = value;
        }

        var normalized = new List<FieldValue>(values.Length);
        for (var i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
        {
            if (values[i] is { } value)
            {
                normalized.Add(new FieldValue(ListingFields.All[i].Key, value));
            }
        }

        if (normalized.Count == 0)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var derived = AddressKey(normalized);
        var keys = ListingKey.Normalize(derived is null ? Array.Empty<string>() : new[] { derived }, anchors);
        return keys.Length == 0 ? null : new Entry(id, EntryKinds.Fact, keys, Fields: normalized);
    }

    private static Entry? BuildComment(Guid id, IEnumerable<string?>? anchors, string? text)
    {
        var normalized = CommentText.Normalize(text);
        if (normalized.Length == 0)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var keys = ListingKey.Normalize(Array.Empty<string>(), anchors);
        return keys.Length == 0 ? null : new Entry(id, EntryKinds.Comment, keys, Text: normalized);
    }

    private static string? AddressKey(IReadOnlyList<FieldValue>? fields)
    {
        if (fields is null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        string? street = null;
        string? unit = null;
        string? zip = null;
        foreach (var field in fields)
        {
            switch (field.Key)
            {
                case ListingFields.Street:
                    street = field.Value;
                    break;
                case ListingFields.Unit:
                    unit = field.Value;
                    break;
                case ListingFields.Zip:
                    zip = field.Value;
                    break;
            }
        }

        return string.IsNullOrEmpty(street) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(zip) ? null : ListingKey.AddressOf(street, unit, zip);
    }
}

SHA256:   9c7545a63a8b02be078332f0e7b21e50d77e75b0ce8d93d29c16e93eb6615c7c

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal sealed record WindowChange(Entry[] Next, Entry[] Added, Guid[] Removed);

internal static class EntryWindow
{
    public static WindowChange? Merge(Entry[] current, IEnumerable<Entry> candidates, int capacity)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(current);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(candidates);
        var known = new HashSet<Guid>(current.Length);
        foreach (var entry in current)
        {
            known.Add(entry.Id);
        }

        var next = current
            .Concat(candidates.Where(entry => !known.Contains(entry.Id)).DistinctBy(entry => entry.Id))
            .OrderByDescending(entry => entry.Id)
            .Take(capacity)
            .ToArray();
        var added = next.Where(entry => !known.Contains(entry.Id)).ToArray();
        if (added.Length == 0)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var kept = next.Select(entry => entry.Id).ToHashSet();
        var removed = current.Where(entry => !kept.Contains(entry.Id)).Select(entry => entry.Id).ToArray();
        return new WindowChange(next, added, removed);
    }

    public static bool IsRestorable(Guid id, DateTimeOffset now, DateTimeOffset startedAt)
    {
        if (!Uuid7.IsValid(id))
        {
            return false;
        }

        var at = Uuid7.Timestamp(id);
        return at <= now && at < startedAt;
    }
}

using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal enum FieldKind
{
    Money,
    Count,
    Decimal,
    Integer,
    Choice,
    State,
    Zip,
    Text,
}

internal sealed record ListingField(string Key, string Label, FieldKind Kind, int MaxLength = 64, long Min = 0, long Max = 0, IReadOnlyList<string>? Options = null, string? Hint = null);

internal static partial class ListingFields
{
    public const string Price = "price";
    public const string Status = "status";
    public const string Beds = "beds";
    public const string Baths = "baths";
    public const string Sqft = "sqft";
    public const string Year = "year";
    public const string Street = "street";
    public const string Unit = "unit";
    public const string City = "city";
    public const string State = "state";
    public const string Zip = "zip";

    public static IReadOnlyList<string> StatusOptions { get; } = ["For sale", "Coming soon", "Pending", "Sold", "For rent", "Off market"];

    public static IReadOnlyList<string> TypeOptions { get; } = ["House", "Condo", "Townhouse", "Multi-family", "Manufactured", "Land", "Other"];

    public static IReadOnlyList<string> AddressKeys { get; } = [Street, Unit, City, State, Zip];

    public static IReadOnlyList<ListingField> All { get; } =
    [
        new(Price, "Price", FieldKind.Money, Hint: "450000 or 450k"),
        new(Status, "Status", FieldKind.Choice, Options: StatusOptions),
        new("type", "Type", FieldKind.Choice, Options: TypeOptions),
        new(Beds, "Bedrooms", FieldKind.Count),
        new(Baths, "Bathrooms", FieldKind.Decimal, Hint: "2.5"),
        new(Sqft, "Square feet", FieldKind.Integer, Min: 1, Max: 10_000_000),
        new("lot", "Lot size", FieldKind.Text, 40, Hint: "0.25 acres"),
        new(Year, "Year built", FieldKind.Integer, Min: 1600, Max: 2100),
        new("hoa", "HOA per month", FieldKind.Money),
        new("taxes", "Taxes per year", FieldKind.Money),
        new("parking", "Parking", FieldKind.Text, 60),
        new("heating", "Heating and cooling", FieldKind.Text, 60),
        new("mls", "MLS number", FieldKind.Text, 40),
        new(Street, "Street address", FieldKind.Text, 120),
        new(Unit, "Unit", FieldKind.Text, 20),
        new(City, "City", FieldKind.Text, 60),
        new(State, "State", FieldKind.State),
        new(Zip, "ZIP code", FieldKind.Zip),
    ];

    private static readonly Dictionary<string, int> Order = All
        .Select((field, index) => (field.Key, index))
        .ToDictionary(pair => pair.Key, pair => pair.index, StringComparer.Ordinal);

    public static ListingField? Find(string? key) =>
        key is not null && Order.TryGetValue(key, out var index) ? All[index] : null;

    public static int OrderOf(string key) => Order.TryGetValue(key, out var index) ? index : -1;

    public static string CleanText(string? input, int max)
    {
        var builder = new StringBuilder(Math.Min(input?.Length ?? 0, max + 1));
        foreach (var c in input ?? "")
        {
            if (IsSpace(c))
            {
                if (builder.Length > 0 && builder[^1] != ' ')
                {
                    builder.Append(' ');
                }
            }
            else if (c > '\u001f' && (c < '\u007f' || c > '\u009f'))
            {
                builder.Append(c);
            }
        }

        if (builder.Length > 0 && builder[^1] == ' ')
        {
            builder.Length--;
        }

        if (builder.Length > max)
        {
            var cut = max;
            if (char.IsHighSurrogate(builder[cut - 1]))
            {
                cut--;
            }

            builder.Length = cut;
            if (builder.Length > 0 && builder[^1] == ' ')
            {
                builder.Length--;
            }
        }

        return builder.ToString();
    }

    public static bool TryNormalize(string? key, string? input, out string value)
    {
        value = "";
        var field = Find(key);
        if (field is null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var text = CleanText(input, field.Kind == FieldKind.Text ? field.MaxLength : 64);
        if (text.Length == 0)
        {
            return true;
        }

        var normalized = field.Kind switch
        {
            FieldKind.Money => Money(text),
            FieldKind.Count => CountPattern().IsMatch(text) ? StripZeros(text) : null,
            FieldKind.Decimal => DecimalValue(text),
            FieldKind.Integer => IntegerValue(text, field),
            FieldKind.Choice => field.Options?.FirstOrDefault(option => string.Equals(ListingKey.LowerAscii(option), ListingKey.LowerAscii(text), StringComparison.Ordinal)),
            FieldKind.State => StatePattern().IsMatch(text) ? ListingKey.UpperAscii(text) : null,
            FieldKind.Zip => ZipValue(text),
            _ => text,
        };

        if (normalized is null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        value = normalized;
        return true;
    }

    public static string Display(string key, string value)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(value);
        if (value.Length == 0)
        {
            return "";
        }

        return Find(key)?.Kind switch
        {
            FieldKind.Money => string.Concat("$", Grouped(value)),
            FieldKind.Integer when !string.Equals(key, Year, StringComparison.Ordinal) => Grouped(value),
            _ => value,
        };
    }

    public static string Summary(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> fields)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(fields);
        var parts = new List<string>(5);
        if (fields.TryGetValue(Price, out var price))
        {
            parts.Add(Display(Price, price));
        }

        if (fields.TryGetValue(Beds, out var beds))
        {
            parts.Add(string.Concat(beds, " bd"));
        }

        if (fields.TryGetValue(Baths, out var baths))
        {
            parts.Add(string.Concat(baths, " ba"));
        }

        if (fields.TryGetValue(Sqft, out var sqft))
        {
            parts.Add(string.Concat(Display(Sqft, sqft), " sq ft"));
        }

        if (fields.TryGetValue(Status, out var status))
        {
            parts.Add(status);
        }

        return string.Join(" · ", parts);
    }

    public static string Place(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> fields)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(fields);
        var city = string.Join(", ", new[] { fields.GetValueOrDefault(City), fields.GetValueOrDefault(State) }.Where(part => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(part)));
        return string.Join(' ', new[] { city, fields.GetValueOrDefault(Zip) }.Where(part => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(part)));
    }

    public static string StreetLine(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> fields)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(fields);
        if (!fields.TryGetValue(Street, out var street))
        {
            return "";
        }

        return fields.TryGetValue(Unit, out var unit)
            ? string.Concat(street, ", unit ", UnitPrefix().Replace(unit, ""))
            : street;
    }

    private static bool IsSpace(char c) =>
        c is >= '\u0009' and <= '\u000d' or ' ' or ' ' or ' ' or >= ' ' and <= ' ' or ' ' or ' ' or '　';

    private static string StripZeros(string digits)
    {
        var trimmed = digits.TrimStart('0');
        return trimmed.Length > 0 ? trimmed : "0";
    }

    private static string? Money(string text)
    {
        var match = MoneyPattern().Match(MoneyNoise().Replace(text, ""));
        if (!match.Success)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var suffix = match.Groups[3].Value;
        var places = suffix.Length == 0 ? 0 : suffix is "k" or "K" ? 3 : 6;
        var fraction = match.Groups[2].Value;
        if (places == 0 && fraction.AsSpan().ContainsAnyExcept('0'))
        {
            return null;
        }

        if (places > 0 && fraction.Length > places)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var value = StripZeros(places > 0 ? string.Concat(match.Groups[1].Value, fraction.PadRight(places, '0')) : match.Groups[1].Value);
        return value.Length <= 11 ? value : null;
    }

    private static string? DecimalValue(string text)
    {
        var match = DecimalPattern().Match(text);
        if (!match.Success)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var whole = StripZeros(match.Groups[1].Value);
        var fraction = match.Groups[2].Value.TrimEnd('0');
        return fraction.Length > 0 ? string.Concat(whole, ".", fraction) : whole;
    }

    private static string? IntegerValue(string text, ListingField field)
    {
        var cleaned = IntegerNoise().Replace(text, "");
        if (!IntegerPattern().IsMatch(cleaned))
        {
            return null;
        }

        var value = StripZeros(cleaned);
        var number = long.Parse(value, NumberStyles.None, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        return number >= field.Min && number <= field.Max ? value : null;
    }

    private static string? ZipValue(string text)
    {
        var match = ZipPattern().Match(text);
        return match.Success ? match.Groups[1].Value : null;
    }

    private static string Grouped(string digits)
    {
        if (!long.TryParse(digits, NumberStyles.None, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var number))
        {
            return digits;
        }

        return number.ToString("#,0", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
    }

    [GeneratedRegex(@"[ ,$_]")]
    private static partial Regex MoneyNoise();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"[ ,_]")]
    private static partial Regex IntegerNoise();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^([0-9]+)(?:\.([0-9]+))?([kKmM])?\z")]
    private static partial Regex MoneyPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^[0-9]{1,2}\z")]
    private static partial Regex CountPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^([0-9]{1,2})(?:\.([0-9]{1,2}))?\z")]
    private static partial Regex DecimalPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^[0-9]{1,8}\z")]
    private static partial Regex IntegerPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^[A-Za-z]{2}\z")]
    private static partial Regex StatePattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^([0-9]{5})(?:-?[0-9]{4})?\z")]
    private static partial Regex ZipPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^(?:unit|apt|apartment|suite|ste|#)\s*", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase | RegexOptions.CultureInvariant)]
    private static partial Regex UnitPrefix();
}

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal static class ListingIndex
{
    public static IReadOnlyList<Listing> Build(IEnumerable<Entry> entries)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(entries);
        var parents = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        var usable = new List<Entry>();
        foreach (var entry in entries)
        {
            if (entry?.Keys is not { Count: > 0 } keys)
            {
                continue;
            }

            usable.Add(entry);
            for (var i = 1; i < keys.Count; i++)
            {
                Union(parents, keys[0], keys[i]);
            }
        }

        var groups = new Dictionary<string, List<Entry>>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        foreach (var entry in usable)
        {
            var root = Root(parents, entry.Keys[0]);
            if (!groups.TryGetValue(root, out var group))
            {
                group = [];
                groups[root] = group;
            }

            group.Add(entry);
        }

        var listings = groups.Values.Select(group => Resolve(group)).ToList();
        listings.Sort(static (a, b) =>
        {
            var updated = b.Updated.CompareTo(a.Updated);
            return updated != 0 ? updated : ListingKey.Compare(a.Key, b.Key);
        });
        return listings;
    }

    public static Listing? Find(IEnumerable<Listing> listings, string key)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(listings);
        return listings.FirstOrDefault(listing => listing.Keys.Contains(key, StringComparer.Ordinal));
    }

    public static Listing Resolve(IReadOnlyList<Entry> entries)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(entries);
        var sorted = entries.OrderBy(entry => entry.Id).ToArray();
        var keys = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        var links = new Dictionary<string, ListingSource>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        var fields = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        var comments = new List<Entry>();
        foreach (var entry in sorted)
        {
            keys.UnionWith(entry.Keys);
            switch (entry.Kind)
            {
                case EntryKinds.Link when ListingLink.Parse(entry.Url) is { } parsed:
                    links[parsed.Key] = new ListingSource(parsed.Key, parsed.Provider, entry.Url!, entry.Id);
                    break;
                case EntryKinds.Fact when entry.Fields is { } values:
                    foreach (var field in values)
                    {
                        fields[field.Key] = field.Value;
                    }

                    break;
                case EntryKinds.Comment:
                    comments.Add(entry);
                    break;
            }
        }

        foreach (var key in fields.Where(pair => pair.Value.Length == 0).Select(pair => pair.Key).ToList())
        {
            fields.Remove(key);
        }

        var orderedKeys = keys.Order(ListingKey.Comparer).ToArray();
        var primary = orderedKeys[0];
        var title = ListingFields.StreetLine(fields);
        if (title.Length == 0)
        {
            title = ListingFields.Place(fields);
        }

        if (title.Length == 0)
        {
            title = ListingKey.Label(primary);
        }

        return new Listing(
            primary,
            orderedKeys,
            links.Values.OrderBy(link => link.Key, ListingKey.Comparer).ToArray(),
            fields,
            comments,
            sorted.Select(entry => entry.Id).ToArray(),
            sorted[^1].Id,
            title);
    }

    private static string Root(Dictionary<string, string> parents, string key)
    {
        var root = key;
        while (parents.TryGetValue(root, out var parent) && !string.Equals(parent, root, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            root = parent;
        }

        var node = key;
        while (!string.Equals(node, root, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            var next = parents[node];
            parents[node] = root;
            node = next;
        }

        parents.TryAdd(root, root);
        return root;
    }

    private static void Union(Dictionary<string, string> parents, string a, string b)
    {
        var rootA = Root(parents, a);
        var rootB = Root(parents, b);
        if (string.Equals(rootA, rootB, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            return;
        }

        if (ListingKey.Compare(rootA, rootB) <= 0)
        {
            parents[rootB] = rootA;
        }
        else
        {
            parents[rootA] = rootB;
        }
    }
}

using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal static partial class ListingKey
{
    public const int MaxKeys = 8;
    public const int MaxToken = 100;
    public const string Zillow = "zillow";
    public const string Redfin = "redfin";
    public const string Realtor = "realtor";
    public const string Address = "address";
    public const string Url = "url";

    private static readonly string[] Order = [Zillow, Redfin, Realtor, Address, Url];

    private static readonly string[] UnitWords = ["apt", "apartment", "unit", "suite", "ste"];

    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string> StreetWords = new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
    {
        ["alley"] = "aly",
        ["avenue"] = "ave",
        ["av"] = "ave",
        ["boulevard"] = "blvd",
        ["circle"] = "cir",
        ["court"] = "ct",
        ["cove"] = "cv",
        ["crescent"] = "cres",
        ["creek"] = "crk",
        ["crossing"] = "xing",
        ["drive"] = "dr",
        ["expressway"] = "expy",
        ["freeway"] = "fwy",
        ["glen"] = "gln",
        ["heights"] = "hts",
        ["highway"] = "hwy",
        ["hill"] = "hl",
        ["hollow"] = "holw",
        ["junction"] = "jct",
        ["lane"] = "ln",
        ["loop"] = "loop",
        ["manor"] = "mnr",
        ["mount"] = "mt",
        ["mountain"] = "mtn",
        ["parkway"] = "pkwy",
        ["place"] = "pl",
        ["plaza"] = "plz",
        ["point"] = "pt",
        ["ridge"] = "rdg",
        ["road"] = "rd",
        ["route"] = "rte",
        ["rt"] = "rte",
        ["square"] = "sq",
        ["street"] = "st",
        ["terrace"] = "ter",
        ["trail"] = "trl",
        ["turnpike"] = "tpke",
        ["valley"] = "vly",
        ["view"] = "vw",
        ["village"] = "vlg",
        ["north"] = "n",
        ["south"] = "s",
        ["east"] = "e",
        ["west"] = "w",
        ["northeast"] = "ne",
        ["northwest"] = "nw",
        ["southeast"] = "se",
        ["southwest"] = "sw",
    };

    public static IReadOnlyList<string> Providers => Order;

    public static IComparer<string> Comparer { get; } = Comparer<string>.Create(Compare);

    public static string? ProviderOf(string? key)
    {
        var colon = key?.IndexOf(':', StringComparison.Ordinal) ?? -1;
        return colon > 0 ? key![..colon] : null;
    }

    public static string? TokenOf(string? key)
    {
        var colon = key?.IndexOf(':', StringComparison.Ordinal) ?? -1;
        return colon > 0 ? key![(colon + 1)..] : null;
    }

    public static bool IsValid(string? provider, string? token)
    {
        if (provider is null || token is null || token.Length > MaxToken)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return provider switch
        {
            Zillow or Redfin => NumericToken().IsMatch(token),
            Realtor => RealtorToken().IsMatch(token),
            Address => AddressToken().IsMatch(token),
            Url => HashToken().IsMatch(token),
            _ => false,
        };
    }

    public static bool IsValid(string? key) => IsValid(ProviderOf(key), TokenOf(key));

    public static string? Of(string provider, string? token) =>
        IsValid(provider, token) ? string.Concat(provider, ":", token) : null;

    public static int RankOf(string? key)
    {
        var provider = ProviderOf(key);
        var rank = provider is null ? -1 : Array.IndexOf(Order, provider);
        return rank < 0 ? Order.Length : rank;
    }

    public static int Compare(string? a, string? b)
    {
        var rank = RankOf(a) - RankOf(b);
        return rank != 0 ? rank : string.CompareOrdinal(a, b);
    }

    public static string ProviderLabel(string? provider) => provider switch
    {
        Zillow => "Zillow",
        Redfin => "Redfin",
        Realtor => "Realtor.com",
        Address => "Address",
        _ => "Link",
    };

    public static string Label(string key)
    {
        var provider = ProviderOf(key);
        var token = TokenOf(key) ?? "";
        return provider switch
        {
            Zillow or Redfin => string.Concat(ProviderLabel(provider), " ", token),
            Realtor => string.Concat("Realtor.com ", UpperAscii(token)),
            Address when token.Length > 6 => string.Concat(token[6..].Replace('-', ' '), ", ", token[..5]),
            _ => string.Concat("Link ", token.Length > 6 ? token[..6] : token),
        };
    }

    public static string Path(string key) => string.Concat("homes/", ProviderOf(key), "/", TokenOf(key));

    public static string[] Normalize(IEnumerable<string> derived, IEnumerable<string?>? anchors)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(derived);
        var keys = new List<string>(MaxKeys);
        foreach (var key in derived)
        {
            AddKey(keys, key);
        }

        foreach (var key in anchors ?? [])
        {
            AddKey(keys, key);
        }

        keys.Sort(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        return [.. keys];
    }

    public static string? AddressOf(string? street, string? unit, string? zip)
    {
        if (zip is null || !ZipToken().IsMatch(zip))
        {
            return null;
        }

        var tokens = Words(UnitMarked(street));
        var split = -1;
        for (var i = 1; i < tokens.Count; i++)
        {
            if (UnitWords.Contains(tokens[i], StringComparer.Ordinal))
            {
                split = i;
                break;
            }
        }

        var streetTokens = (split > 0 ? tokens.GetRange(0, split) : tokens)
            .Select(token => StreetWords.TryGetValue(token, out var shortened) ? shortened : token)
            .ToList();
        if (streetTokens.Count == 0)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var unitInput = Words(UnitMarked(unit));
        var unitSource = unitInput.Count > 0 ? unitInput : split > 0 ? tokens.GetRange(split, tokens.Count - split) : new List<string>();
        var unitTokens = unitSource.Where(token => !UnitWords.Contains(token, StringComparer.Ordinal)).ToList();

        var parts = new List<string>(streetTokens.Count + unitTokens.Count + 2) { zip };
        parts.AddRange(streetTokens);
        if (unitTokens.Count > 0)
        {
            parts.Add("unit");
            parts.AddRange(unitTokens);
        }

        return Of(Address, string.Join('-', parts));
    }

    public static List<string> Words(string? text)
    {
        var words = new List<string>();
        var current = new StringBuilder();
        foreach (var c in text ?? "")
        {
            if (c is >= 'A' and <= 'Z')
            {
                current.Append((char)(c + 32));
            }
            else if (c is >= 'a' and <= 'z' or >= '0' and <= '9')
            {
                current.Append(c);
            }
            else if (current.Length > 0)
            {
                words.Add(current.ToString());
                current.Clear();
            }
        }

        if (current.Length > 0)
        {
            words.Add(current.ToString());
        }

        return words;
    }

    public static string LowerAscii(string text)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(text);
        return string.Create(text.Length, text, static (span, source) =>
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < source.Length; i++)
            {
                var c = source[i];
                span[i] = c is >= 'A' and <= 'Z' ? (char)(c + 32) : c;
            }
        });
    }

    public static string UpperAscii(string text)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(text);
        return string.Create(text.Length, text, static (span, source) =>
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < source.Length; i++)
            {
                var c = source[i];
                span[i] = c is >= 'a' and <= 'z' ? (char)(c - 32) : c;
            }
        });
    }

    public static string Fnv1a64(string text)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(text);
        var hash = 0xcbf29ce484222325UL;
        foreach (var b in Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(text))
        {
            hash ^= b;
            hash = unchecked(hash * 0x100000001b3UL);
        }

        return hash.ToString("x16", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
    }

    private static void AddKey(List<string> keys, string? key)
    {
        if (keys.Count < MaxKeys && key is not null && IsValid(key) && !keys.Contains(key, StringComparer.Ordinal))
        {
            keys.Add(key);
        }
    }

    private static string UnitMarked(string? text) => text?.Replace("#", " unit ", StringComparison.Ordinal) ?? "";

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^[1-9][0-9]{0,11}\z")]
    private static partial Regex NumericToken();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^m[0-9]{1,12}-[0-9]{1,12}\z")]
    private static partial Regex RealtorToken();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^[0-9]{5}(?:-[a-z0-9]+)+\z")]
    private static partial Regex AddressToken();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^[0-9a-f]{16}\z")]
    private static partial Regex HashToken();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^[0-9]{5}\z")]
    private static partial Regex ZipToken();
}

using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal sealed record ParsedLink(string Key, string Provider, string Url);

internal static partial class ListingLink
{
    public const int MaxUrl = 2048;
    private const string Trailing = ".,;:!?)]}";

    public static string? ExtractUrl(string? text)
    {
        if (text is null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var match = UrlPattern().Match(text);
        if (!match.Success)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var url = match.Value;
        var end = url.Length;
        while (end > 0 && Trailing.Contains(url[end - 1], StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            end--;
        }

        return end > 0 && end <= MaxUrl ? url[..end] : null;
    }

    public static ParsedLink? Parse(string? text)
    {
        var raw = ExtractUrl(text);
        var parts = raw is null ? null : PartsPattern().Match(raw);
        if (parts is null || !parts.Success)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var scheme = parts.Groups[1].Value.Length == 5 ? "https" : "http";
        var host = ListingKey.LowerAscii(parts.Groups[2].Value);
        var path = parts.Groups[3].Value.Length > 0 ? parts.Groups[3].Value : "/";
        if (!HostPattern().IsMatch(host))
        {
            return null;
        }

        var site = PortPattern().Replace(host, "");
        if (site.StartsWith("www.", StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            site = site[4..];
        }

        if (!site.Contains('.', StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            return null;
        }

        string? key = null;
        if (IsSite(site, "zillow.com"))
        {
            var match = ZillowPattern().Match(path);
            key = match.Success ? ListingKey.Of(ListingKey.Zillow, StripZeros(match.Groups[1].Value)) : null;
        }
        else if (IsSite(site, "redfin.com"))
        {
            var match = RedfinPattern().Match(path);
            key = match.Success ? ListingKey.Of(ListingKey.Redfin, StripZeros(match.Groups[1].Value)) : null;
        }
        else if (IsSite(site, "realtor.com"))
        {
            var match = RealtorPattern().Match(path);
            key = match.Success ? ListingKey.Of(ListingKey.Realtor, string.Concat("m", match.Groups[1].Value, "-", match.Groups[2].Value)) : null;
        }

        if (key is not null)
        {
            return new ParsedLink(key, ListingKey.ProviderOf(key)!, string.Concat("https://", host, path));
        }

        var canonical = string.Concat(site, path.TrimEnd('/'));
        return new ParsedLink(string.Concat(ListingKey.Url, ":", ListingKey.Fnv1a64(canonical)), ListingKey.Url, string.Concat(scheme, "://", host, path));
    }

    private static bool IsSite(string site, string domain) =>
        string.Equals(site, domain, StringComparison.Ordinal) || site.EndsWith(string.Concat(".", domain), StringComparison.Ordinal);

    private static string StripZeros(string digits)
    {
        var trimmed = digits.TrimStart('0');
        return trimmed.Length > 0 ? trimmed : "0";
    }

    [GeneratedRegex(@"[hH][tT][tT][pP][sS]?://[\x21\x23-\x26\x28-\x3B\x3D\x3F-\x7E]+")]
    private static partial Regex UrlPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^([hH][tT][tT][pP][sS]?)://([^/?#]+)([^?#]*)")]
    private static partial Regex PartsPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"^[a-z0-9.-]+(?::[0-9]{1,5})?\z")]
    private static partial Regex HostPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@":[0-9]+\z")]
    private static partial Regex PortPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"([0-9]+)_[zZ][pP][iI][dD]")]
    private static partial Regex ZillowPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"/home/([0-9]+)")]
    private static partial Regex RedfinPattern();

    [GeneratedRegex(@"_[mM]([0-9]+)-([0-9]+)")]
    private static partial Regex RealtorPattern();
}

FILE:     src/Nest.Web/Listings/NestService.cs

using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal sealed class NestService
{
    public const int MaxFetch = 100;
    public const int RepeatWindow = 20;

    private readonly TimeProvider _time;
    private readonly KeyedTokenBuckets _posts;
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();
    private readonly HashSet<INestSubscriber> _subscribers = [];
    private Entry[] _entries = [];
    private Dictionary<Guid, Entry> _byId = new();
    private ListingCache? _listings;
    private long _version;

    public NestService(TimeProvider time, IOptions<NestOptions> options)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(time);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(options);
        var limits = options.Value;
        _time = time;
        Capacity = limits.Capacity;
        _posts = new KeyedTokenBuckets(limits.PostBurst, limits.PostsPerSecond, time);
        StartedAt = time.GetUtcNow();
    }

    public int Capacity { get; }

    public DateTimeOffset StartedAt { get; }

    public IReadOnlyList<Entry> Entries => Volatile.Read(ref _entries);

    public long Version
    {
        get
        {
            lock (_gate)
            {
                return _version;
            }
        }
    }

    public IReadOnlyList<Listing> Listings
    {
        get
        {
            var entries = Volatile.Read(ref _entries);
            var cache = Volatile.Read(ref _listings);
            if (cache is not null && ReferenceEquals(cache.Source, entries))
            {
                return cache.Listings;
            }

            var listings = ListingIndex.Build(entries);
            Volatile.Write(ref _listings, new ListingCache(entries, listings));
            return listings;
        }
    }

    public Listing? FindListing(string key) => ListingIndex.Find(Listings, key);

    public SubmitReply Submit(EntryDraft? draft, string client)
    {
        var candidate = draft is null ? null : EntryRules.Build(Guid.Empty, draft);
        if (candidate is null)
        {
            return new SubmitReply(SubmitResult.Invalid);
        }

        var key = KeyOf(candidate);
        if (!_posts.TryTake(client))
        {
            return new SubmitReply(SubmitResult.TooFast);
        }

        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (Exists(candidate))
            {
                return new SubmitReply(SubmitResult.Exists, Key: key);
            }

            if (IsRepeat(candidate))
            {
                return new SubmitReply(SubmitResult.Repeat);
            }

            var newest = _entries.Length > 0 ? _entries[0].Id : Guid.Empty;
            var entry = candidate with { Id = Uuid7.After(newest, _time.GetUtcNow()) };
            Merge([entry]);
            return new SubmitReply(SubmitResult.Accepted, entry, key);
        }
    }

    public int Offer(IEnumerable<Entry> offered)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(offered);
        var now = _time.GetUtcNow();
        var valid = new List<Entry>();
        foreach (var entry in offered.Take(Capacity))
        {
            if (IsAcceptable(entry, now))
            {
                valid.Add(entry);
            }
        }

        if (valid.Count == 0)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        lock (_gate)
        {
            return Merge(valid);
        }
    }

    public IReadOnlyList<Entry> Find(IEnumerable<Guid> ids)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(ids);
        var byId = Volatile.Read(ref _byId);
        var found = new List<Entry>();
        foreach (var id in ids.Take(MaxFetch))
        {
            if (byId.TryGetValue(id, out var entry))
            {
                found.Add(entry);
            }
        }

        return found;
    }

    public void Subscribe(INestSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(subscriber);
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (subscriber.Deliver(NestUpdate.Snapshot(_version, Capacity, _entries)))
            {
                _subscribers.Add(subscriber);
            }
        }
    }

    public void Unsubscribe(INestSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            _subscribers.Remove(subscriber);
        }
    }

    public bool IsSubscribed(INestSubscriber subscriber)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            return _subscribers.Contains(subscriber);
        }
    }

    private static string KeyOf(Entry entry) =>
        EntryRules.DerivedKey(entry) ?? entry.Keys.Order(ListingKey.Comparer).First();

    private bool IsAcceptable(Entry? entry, DateTimeOffset now) =>
        entry is not null
        && EntryWindow.IsRestorable(entry.Id, now, StartedAt)
        && EntryRules.IsNormalized(entry);

    private bool Exists(Entry candidate)
    {
        if (!string.Equals(candidate.Kind, EntryKinds.Link, StringComparison.Ordinal))
        {
            return false;
        }

        foreach (var entry in _entries)
        {
            if (string.Equals(entry.Kind, EntryKinds.Link, StringComparison.Ordinal)
                && candidate.Keys.All(key => entry.Keys.Contains(key, StringComparer.Ordinal)))
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private bool IsRepeat(Entry candidate)
    {
        var window = Math.Min(RepeatWindow, _entries.Length);
        for (var i = 0; i < window; i++)
        {
            var entry = _entries[i];
            if (string.Equals(entry.Kind, candidate.Kind, StringComparison.Ordinal)
                && string.Equals(entry.Text, candidate.Text, StringComparison.Ordinal)
                && entry.Keys.SequenceEqual(candidate.Keys, StringComparer.Ordinal)
                && FieldsEqual(entry.Fields, candidate.Fields))
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static bool FieldsEqual(IReadOnlyList<FieldValue>? a, IReadOnlyList<FieldValue>? b) =>
        a is null ? b is null : b is not null && a.SequenceEqual(b);

    private int Merge(IReadOnlyCollection<Entry> candidates)
    {
        var change = EntryWindow.Merge(_entries, candidates, Capacity);
        if (change is null)
        {
            return 0;
        }

        var previous = _version;
        _version++;
        Volatile.Write(ref _byId, change.Next.ToDictionary(entry => entry.Id));
        Volatile.Write(ref _entries, change.Next);
        Broadcast(NestUpdate.Delta(_version, previous, change.Added, change.Removed));
        return change.Added.Length;
    }

    private void Broadcast(NestUpdate update)
    {
        List<INestSubscriber>? dropped = null;
        foreach (var subscriber in _subscribers)
        {
            if (!subscriber.Deliver(update))
            {
                (dropped ??= []).Add(subscriber);
            }
        }

        if (dropped is not null)
        {
            _subscribers.ExceptWith(dropped);
        }
    }

    private sealed record ListingCache(Entry[] Source, IReadOnlyList<Listing> Listings);
}

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal sealed record NestUpdate(string Kind, long Version, long Previous, int Capacity, IReadOnlyList<Guid> Ids, IReadOnlyList<Entry> Added, IReadOnlyList<Guid> Removed)
{
    public static NestUpdate Snapshot(long version, int capacity, IReadOnlyList<Entry> entries)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(entries);
        return new("snapshot", version, version, capacity, entries.Select(entry => entry.Id).ToArray(), [], []);
    }

    public static NestUpdate Delta(long version, long previous, IReadOnlyList<Entry> added, IReadOnlyList<Guid> removed) =>
        new("delta", version, previous, 0, [], added, removed);
}

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal static class Uuid7
{
    public static bool IsValid(Guid id) => id.Version == 7 && (id.Variant & 0b1100) == 0b1000;

    public static long UnixMilliseconds(Guid id)
    {
        Span<byte> bytes = stackalloc byte[16];
        id.TryWriteBytes(bytes, bigEndian: true, out _);
        long milliseconds = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        {
            milliseconds = (milliseconds << 8) | bytes[i];
        }

        return milliseconds;
    }

    public static DateTimeOffset Timestamp(Guid id) => DateTimeOffset.FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(UnixMilliseconds(id));

    public static Guid After(Guid previous, DateTimeOffset now)
    {
        var candidate = Guid.CreateVersion7(now);
        if (candidate.CompareTo(previous) > 0 || !IsValid(previous))
        {
            return candidate;
        }

        Span<byte> bytes = stackalloc byte[16];
        previous.TryWriteBytes(bytes, bigEndian: true, out _);
        for (var i = 15; i >= 9; i--)
        {
            if (++bytes[i] != 0)
            {
                return new Guid(bytes, bigEndian: true);
            }
        }

        if ((bytes[8] & 0b0011_1111) != 0b0011_1111)
        {
            bytes[8]++;
            return new Guid(bytes, bigEndian: true);
        }

        return candidate;
    }
}

using System.Text.Json;

namespace Nest.Web.Listings;

internal sealed record FieldWire(string? Key, string? Value);

internal sealed record EntryWire(string? Id, string? Kind, IReadOnlyList<string?>? Keys, string? Url, IReadOnlyList<FieldWire?>? Fields, string? Text)
{
    public Entry? ToEntry()
    {
        if (!Guid.TryParseExact(Id, "D", out var id) || Kind is null || Keys is null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        var keys = new List<string>(Keys.Count);
        foreach (var key in Keys)
        {
            if (key is null)
            {
                return null;
            }

            keys.Add(key);
        }

        List<FieldValue>? fields = null;
        if (Fields is not null)
        {
            fields = new List<FieldValue>(Fields.Count);
            foreach (var field in Fields)
            {
                if (field?.Key is null || field.Value is null)
                {
                    return null;
                }

                fields.Add(new FieldValue(field.Key, field.Value));
            }
        }

        return new Entry(id, Kind, keys, Url, fields, Text);
    }
}

internal sealed record EntryDraft(string? Kind, IReadOnlyList<string?>? Anchors, string? Url, IReadOnlyList<FieldWire?>? Fields, string? Text);

internal sealed record PostOutcome(string Result, Entry? Entry, string? Key)
{
    public static PostOutcome From(SubmitReply reply)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(reply);
        return new PostOutcome(NameOf(reply.Result), reply.Entry, reply.Key);
    }

    public static string NameOf(SubmitResult result) => result switch
    {
        SubmitResult.Accepted => "accepted",
        SubmitResult.Exists => "exists",
        SubmitResult.TooFast => "tooFast",
        SubmitResult.Repeat => "repeat",
        _ => "invalid",
    };
}

internal static class Wire
{
    public static JsonSerializerOptions Options { get; } = new(JsonSerializerDefaults.Web);

    public static T? Read<T>(JsonElement element)
        where T : class
    {
        try
        {
            return element.Deserialize<T>(Options);
        }
        catch (JsonException)
        {
            return null;
        }
        catch (NotSupportedException)
        {
            return null;
        }
        catch (InvalidOperationException)
        {
            return null;
        }
    }

    public static JsonElement Write<T>(T value) => JsonSerializer.SerializeToElement(value, Options);
}

using System.IO.Compression;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Antiforgery;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.DataProtection;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.HttpOverrides;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.RateLimiting;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.ResponseCompression;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using Nest.Web.Components;
using Nest.Web.Infrastructure;
using Nest.Web.Listings;

var publishedWebRoot = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "wwwroot");
var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(new WebApplicationOptions
{
    Args = args,
    WebRootPath = Directory.Exists(publishedWebRoot) ? publishedWebRoot : null,
});

builder.WebHost.ConfigureKestrel(kestrel =>
{
    kestrel.AddServerHeader = false;
    if (int.TryParse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PORT"), out var port))
    {
        kestrel.ListenAnyIP(port);
    }
});

builder.Services.Configure<HostOptions>(host => host.ShutdownTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10));
builder.Services.Configure<ForwardedHeadersOptions>(forwarded =>
{
    forwarded.ForwardedHeaders = ForwardedHeaders.XForwardedFor | ForwardedHeaders.XForwardedProto;
    forwarded.KnownIPNetworks.Clear();
    forwarded.KnownProxies.Clear();
});

builder.Services.AddOptions<NestOptions>()
    .Bind(builder.Configuration.GetSection(NestOptions.SectionName))
    .Validate(nest => nest.IsValid(), "Nest limits are out of range.")
    .ValidateOnStart();

builder.Services.AddDataProtection().UseEphemeralDataProtectionProvider();
builder.Services.AddSingleton<IAntiforgery>(new CookielessAntiforgery());
builder.Services.AddSingleton(TimeProvider.System);
builder.Services.AddSingleton<NestService>();
builder.Services.AddSingleton<ConnectionTracker>();
builder.Services.AddHealthChecks();

builder.Services.AddResponseCompression(compression =>
{
    compression.EnableForHttps = true;
    compression.Providers.Add<BrotliCompressionProvider>();
    compression.Providers.Add<GzipCompressionProvider>();
});
builder.Services.Configure<BrotliCompressionProviderOptions>(brotli => brotli.Level = CompressionLevel.Fastest);
builder.Services.Configure<GzipCompressionProviderOptions>(gzip => gzip.Level = CompressionLevel.Fastest);

builder.Services.AddRateLimiter(_ => { });
builder.Services.AddOptions<RateLimiterOptions>()
    .Configure<IOptions<NestOptions>>((limiter, nest) => RequestLimits.Configure(limiter, nest.Value));

builder.Services.AddRazorComponents()
    .AddInteractiveServerComponents(circuit =>
    {
        circuit.DisconnectedCircuitMaxRetained = 20;
        circuit.DisconnectedCircuitRetentionPeriod = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1);
        circuit.PersistedCircuitInMemoryMaxRetained = 20;
        circuit.PersistedCircuitInMemoryRetentionPeriod = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);
        circuit.MaxBufferedUnacknowledgedRenderBatches = 2;
        circuit.JSInteropDefaultCallTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30);
    })
    .AddHubOptions(hub => hub.MaximumReceiveMessageSize = 32 * 1024);

var app = builder.Build();

app.UseForwardedHeaders();
app.UseSecurityHeaders();

if (!app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseExceptionHandler("/Error", createScopeForErrors: true);
    app.UseHsts();
}

app.UseStatusCodePagesWithReExecute("/not-found", createScopeForStatusCodePages: true);
app.UseResponseCompression();
app.UseRateLimiter();
app.UseWebSockets();
app.UseHubGuard();
app.UseAntiforgery();

app.MapHealthChecks("/health");
app.MapStaticAssets();
app.MapRazorComponents<App>()
    .AddInteractiveServerRenderMode(server => server.DisableWebSocketCompression = true);

await app.RunAsync();

public partial class Program;

import { lowerAscii } from './keys.js';

export const STATUS_OPTIONS = Object.freeze(['For sale', 'Coming soon', 'Pending', 'Sold', 'For rent', 'Off market']);
export const TYPE_OPTIONS = Object.freeze(['House', 'Condo', 'Townhouse', 'Multi-family', 'Manufactured', 'Land', 'Other']);

export const FIELDS = Object.freeze([
    { key: 'price', label: 'Price', kind: 'money', hint: '450000 or 450k' },
    { key: 'status', label: 'Status', kind: 'choice', options: STATUS_OPTIONS },
    { key: 'type', label: 'Type', kind: 'choice', options: TYPE_OPTIONS },
    { key: 'beds', label: 'Bedrooms', kind: 'count' },
    { key: 'baths', label: 'Bathrooms', kind: 'decimal', hint: '2.5' },
    { key: 'sqft', label: 'Square feet', kind: 'int', min: 1, max: 10000000 },
    { key: 'lot', label: 'Lot size', kind: 'text', max: 40, hint: '0.25 acres' },
    { key: 'year', label: 'Year built', kind: 'int', min: 1600, max: 2100 },
    { key: 'hoa', label: 'HOA per month', kind: 'money' },
    { key: 'taxes', label: 'Taxes per year', kind: 'money' },
    { key: 'parking', label: 'Parking', kind: 'text', max: 60 },
    { key: 'heating', label: 'Heating and cooling', kind: 'text', max: 60 },
    { key: 'mls', label: 'MLS number', kind: 'text', max: 40 },
    { key: 'street', label: 'Street address', kind: 'text', max: 120 },
    { key: 'unit', label: 'Unit', kind: 'text', max: 20 },
    { key: 'city', label: 'City', kind: 'text', max: 60 },
    { key: 'state', label: 'State', kind: 'state' },
    { key: 'zip', label: 'ZIP code', kind: 'zip' },
]);

export const ADDRESS_FIELDS = Object.freeze(['street', 'unit', 'city', 'state', 'zip']);

const BY_KEY = new Map(FIELDS.map(field => [field.key, field]));
const ORDER = new Map(FIELDS.map((field, index) => [field.key, index]));

const ERRORS = Object.freeze({
    money: 'Use a whole dollar amount like 450000, 450k or 1.2m.',
    count: 'Use a whole number from 0 to 99.',
    decimal: 'Use a number from 0 to 99 like 2 or 2.5.',
    int: 'Use a whole number.',
    choice: 'Pick one of the listed options.',
    state: 'Use the two-letter state code, like NY.',
    zip: 'Use a five-digit ZIP code.',
});

export function fieldOf(key) {
    return BY_KEY.get(key) ?? null;
}

export function fieldOrder(key) {
    return ORDER.get(key) ?? -1;
}

export function cleanText(input, max) {
    let text = '';
    for (const char of typeof input === 'string' ? input : '') {
        const code = char.charCodeAt(0);
        const space = (code >= 0x09 && code <= 0x0d) || code === 0x20 || code === 0xa0 || code === 0x1680 || (code >= 0x2000 && code <= 0x200a)
            || code === 0x202f || code === 0x205f || code === 0x3000;
        if (space) {
            if (text.length > 0 && !text.endsWith(' ')) {
                text += ' ';
            }
        } else if (code > 0x1f && (code < 0x7f || code > 0x9f)) {
            text += char;
        }
    }
    if (text.endsWith(' ')) {
        text = text.slice(0, -1);
    }
    if (text.length > max) {
        let cut = max;
        const code = text.charCodeAt(cut - 1);
        if (code >= 0xd800 && code <= 0xdbff) {
            cut -= 1;
        }
        text = text.slice(0, cut);
        if (text.endsWith(' ')) {
            text = text.slice(0, -1);
        }
    }
    return text;
}

function stripZeros(digits) {
    const trimmed = digits.replace(/^0+/, '');
    return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : '0';
}

function money(text) {
    const match = /^([0-9]+)(?:\.([0-9]+))?([kKmM])?$/.exec(text.replace(/[ ,$_]/g, ''));
    if (!match) {
        return null;
    }
    const places = match[3] === undefined ? 0 : match[3].toLowerCase() === 'k' ? 3 : 6;
    const fraction = match[2] ?? '';
    if (places === 0 && /[1-9]/.test(fraction)) {
        return null;
    }
    if (places > 0 && fraction.length > places) {
        return null;
    }
    const value = stripZeros(match[1] + (places > 0 ? fraction.padEnd(places, '0') : ''));
    return value.length <= 11 ? value : null;
}

function count(text) {
    return /^[0-9]{1,2}$/.test(text) ? stripZeros(text) : null;
}

function decimal(text) {
    const match = /^([0-9]{1,2})(?:\.([0-9]{1,2}))?$/.exec(text);
    if (!match) {
        return null;
    }
    const whole = stripZeros(match[1]);
    const fraction = (match[2] ?? '').replace(/0+$/, '');
    return fraction.length > 0 ? `${whole}.${fraction}` : whole;
}

function integer(text, field) {
    const cleaned = text.replace(/[ ,_]/g, '');
    if (!/^[0-9]{1,8}$/.test(cleaned)) {
        return null;
    }
    const value = stripZeros(cleaned);
    const number = Number(value);
    return number >= field.min && number <= field.max ? value : null;
}

function choice(text, field) {
    const lower = lowerAscii(text);
    return field.options.find(option => lowerAscii(option) === lower) ?? null;
}

function state(text) {
    return /^[A-Za-z]{2}$/.test(text) ? text.toUpperCase() : null;
}

function zip(text) {
    const match = /^([0-9]{5})(?:-?[0-9]{4})?$/.exec(text);
    return match ? match[1] : null;
}

export function normalizeField(key, input) {
    const field = fieldOf(key);
    if (!field) {
        return { ok: false, error: 'Unknown field.' };
    }
    const text = cleanText(input, field.kind === 'text' ? field.max : 64);
    if (text.length === 0) {
        return { ok: true, value: '' };
    }
    let value;
    switch (field.kind) {
        case 'money':
            value = money(text);
            break;
        case 'count':
            value = count(text);
            break;
        case 'decimal':
            value = decimal(text);
            break;
        case 'int':
            value = integer(text, field);
            break;
        case 'choice':
            value = choice(text, field);
            break;
        case 'state':
            value = state(text);
            break;
        case 'zip':
            value = zip(text);
            break;
        default:
            value = text;
    }
    return value === null ? { ok: false, error: ERRORS[field.kind] ?? 'Check this value.' } : { ok: true, value };
}

function grouped(digits) {
    return digits.replace(/\B(?=([0-9]{3})+(?![0-9]))/g, ',');
}

export function displayValue(key, value) {
    if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length === 0) {
        return '';
    }
    switch (fieldOf(key)?.kind) {
        case 'money':
            return `$${grouped(value)}`;
        case 'int':
            return key === 'year' ? value : grouped(value);
        default:
            return value;
    }
}

export function summaryOf(fields) {
    const parts = [];
    if (fields.price) {
        parts.push(displayValue('price', fields.price));
    }
    if (fields.beds) {
        parts.push(`${fields.beds} bd`);
    }
    if (fields.baths) {
        parts.push(`${fields.baths} ba`);
    }
    if (fields.sqft) {
        parts.push(`${displayValue('sqft', fields.sqft)} sq ft`);
    }
    if (fields.status) {
        parts.push(fields.status);
    }
    return parts.join(' · ');
}

export function placeOf(fields) {
    const city = [fields.city, fields.state].filter(Boolean).join(', ');
    return [city, fields.zip].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
}

export function streetOf(fields) {
    if (!fields.street) {
        return '';
    }
    return fields.unit ? `${fields.street}, unit ${fields.unit.replace(/^(?:unit|apt|apartment|suite|ste|#)\s*/i, '')}` : fields.street;
}

export function changedFields(current, inputs) {
    const changes = [];
    const errors = [];
    for (const field of FIELDS) {
        if (!Object.hasOwn(inputs, field.key)) {
            continue;
        }
        const result = normalizeField(field.key, inputs[field.key]);
        if (!result.ok) {
            errors.push({ key: field.key, error: result.error });
        } else if (result.value !== (current[field.key] ?? '')) {
            changes.push({ key: field.key, value: result.value });
        }
    }
    if (changes.some(change => ADDRESS_FIELDS.includes(change.key))) {
        for (const key of ADDRESS_FIELDS) {
            if (!changes.some(change => change.key === key) && Object.hasOwn(inputs, key)) {
                const result = normalizeField(key, inputs[key]);
                if (result.ok) {
                    changes.push({ key, value: result.value });
                }
            }
        }
        changes.sort((a, b) => fieldOrder(a.key) - fieldOrder(b.key));
    }
    return { changes, errors };
}
