export const PROVIDERS = Object.freeze(['zillow', 'redfin', 'realtor', 'address', 'url']);
export const MAX_KEYS = 8;
export const MAX_URL = 2048;
export const MAX_ADDRESS_TOKEN = 100;

const LABELS = Object.freeze({
    zillow: 'Zillow',
    redfin: 'Redfin',
    realtor: 'Realtor.com',
    address: 'Address',
    url: 'Link',
});

const TOKEN_PATTERNS = Object.freeze({
    zillow: /^[1-9][0-9]{0,11}$/,
    redfin: /^[1-9][0-9]{0,11}$/,
    realtor: /^m[0-9]{1,12}-[0-9]{1,12}$/,
    address: /^[0-9]{5}(?:-[a-z0-9]+)+$/,
    url: /^[0-9a-f]{16}$/,
});

const URL_PATTERN = /[hH][tT][tT][pP][sS]?:\/\/[\x21\x23-\x26\x28-\x3B\x3D\x3F-\x7E]+/;
const PARTS_PATTERN = /^([hH][tT][tT][pP][sS]?):\/\/([^/?#]+)([^?#]*)/;
const HOST_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9.-]+(?::[0-9]{1,5})?$/;
const TRAILING = '.,;:!?)]}';

export const STREET_WORDS = Object.freeze({
    alley: 'aly',
    avenue: 'ave',
    av: 'ave',
    boulevard: 'blvd',
    circle: 'cir',
    court: 'ct',
    cove: 'cv',
    crescent: 'cres',
    creek: 'crk',
    crossing: 'xing',
    drive: 'dr',
    expressway: 'expy',
    freeway: 'fwy',
    glen: 'gln',
    heights: 'hts',
    highway: 'hwy',
    hill: 'hl',
    hollow: 'holw',
    junction: 'jct',
    lane: 'ln',
    loop: 'loop',
    manor: 'mnr',
    mount: 'mt',
    mountain: 'mtn',
    parkway: 'pkwy',
    place: 'pl',
    plaza: 'plz',
    point: 'pt',
    ridge: 'rdg',
    road: 'rd',
    route: 'rte',
    rt: 'rte',
    square: 'sq',
    street: 'st',
    terrace: 'ter',
    trail: 'trl',
    turnpike: 'tpke',
    valley: 'vly',
    view: 'vw',
    village: 'vlg',
    north: 'n',
    south: 's',
    east: 'e',
    west: 'w',
    northeast: 'ne',
    northwest: 'nw',
    southeast: 'se',
    southwest: 'sw',
});

export const UNIT_WORDS = Object.freeze(['apt', 'apartment', 'unit', 'suite', 'ste']);

export function providerOf(key) {
    const colon = typeof key === 'string' ? key.indexOf(':') : -1;
    return colon > 0 ? key.slice(0, colon) : null;
}

export function tokenOf(key) {
    const colon = typeof key === 'string' ? key.indexOf(':') : -1;
    return colon > 0 ? key.slice(colon + 1) : null;
}

export function isKeyParts(provider, token) {
    const pattern = Object.hasOwn(TOKEN_PATTERNS, provider) ? TOKEN_PATTERNS[provider] : null;
    return pattern !== null
        && typeof token === 'string'
        && token.length <= MAX_ADDRESS_TOKEN
        && pattern.test(token);
}

export function isKey(key) {
    return isKeyParts(providerOf(key), tokenOf(key));
}

export function keyOf(provider, token) {
    return isKeyParts(provider, token) ? `${provider}:${token}` : null;
}

export function rankOf(key) {
    const rank = PROVIDERS.indexOf(providerOf(key));
    return rank < 0 ? PROVIDERS.length : rank;
}

export function compareKeys(a, b) {
    const rank = rankOf(a) - rankOf(b);
    if (rank !== 0) {
        return rank;
    }
    return a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0;
}

export function providerLabel(provider) {
    return LABELS[provider] ?? 'Link';
}

export function keyLabel(key) {
    const provider = providerOf(key);
    const token = tokenOf(key) ?? '';
    switch (provider) {
        case 'zillow':
        case 'redfin':
            return `${providerLabel(provider)} ${token}`;
        case 'realtor':
            return `Realtor.com ${token.toUpperCase()}`;
        case 'address':
            return `${token.slice(6).replaceAll('-', ' ')}, ${token.slice(0, 5)}`;
        default:
            return `Link ${token.slice(0, 6)}`;
    }
}

export function keyPath(key) {
    return isKey(key) ? `homes/${providerOf(key)}/${tokenOf(key)}` : null;
}

export function keyUrl(key) {
    const token = tokenOf(key);
    switch (providerOf(key)) {
        case 'zillow':
            return `https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/${token}_zpid/`;
        case 'redfin':
            return `https://www.redfin.com/home/${token}`;
        default:
            return null;
    }
}

export function normalizeKeys(derived, anchors) {
    const keys = [];
    for (const key of [...(derived ?? []), ...(anchors ?? [])]) {
        if (keys.length >= MAX_KEYS) {
            break;
        }
        if (isKey(key) && !keys.includes(key)) {
            keys.push(key);
        }
    }
    return keys.sort((a, b) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0));
}

export function fnv1a64(text) {
    let hash = 0xcbf29ce484222325n;
    for (const byte of new TextEncoder().encode(text)) {
        hash ^= BigInt(byte);
        hash = (hash * 0x100000001b3n) & 0xffffffffffffffffn;
    }
    return hash.toString(16).padStart(16, '0');
}

export function extractUrl(text) {
    if (typeof text !== 'string') {
        return null;
    }
    const match = URL_PATTERN.exec(text);
    if (!match) {
        return null;
    }
    let url = match[0];
    while (url.length > 0 && TRAILING.includes(url.at(-1))) {
        url = url.slice(0, -1);
    }
    return url.length <= MAX_URL ? url : null;
}

export function lowerAscii(text) {
    return text.replace(/[A-Z]/g, char => String.fromCharCode(char.charCodeAt(0) + 32));
}

function isSite(site, domain) {
    return site === domain || site.endsWith(`.${domain}`);
}

function stripZeros(digits) {
    const trimmed = digits.replace(/^0+/, '');
    return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : '0';
}

export function parseLink(text) {
    const raw = extractUrl(text);
    const parts = raw ? PARTS_PATTERN.exec(raw) : null;
    if (!parts) {
        return null;
    }
    const scheme = parts[1].length === 5 ? 'https' : 'http';
    const host = lowerAscii(parts[2]);
    const path = parts[3].length > 0 ? parts[3] : '/';
    if (!HOST_PATTERN.test(host)) {
        return null;
    }
    const site = host.replace(/:[0-9]+$/, '').replace(/^www\./, '');
    if (!site.includes('.')) {
        return null;
    }

    let provider = null;
    let token = null;
    if (isSite(site, 'zillow.com')) {
        const match = /([0-9]+)_[zZ][pP][iI][dD]/.exec(path);
        provider = match ? 'zillow' : null;
        token = match ? stripZeros(match[1]) : null;
    } else if (isSite(site, 'redfin.com')) {
        const match = /\/home\/([0-9]+)/.exec(path);
        provider = match ? 'redfin' : null;
        token = match ? stripZeros(match[1]) : null;
    } else if (isSite(site, 'realtor.com')) {
        const match = /_[mM]([0-9]+)-([0-9]+)/.exec(path);
        provider = match ? 'realtor' : null;
        token = match ? `m${match[1]}-${match[2]}` : null;
    }

    const key = provider ? keyOf(provider, token) : null;
    if (key) {
        return { key, provider, url: `https://${host}${path}` };
    }
    const canonical = `${site}${path.replace(/\/+$/, '')}`;
    return { key: `url:${fnv1a64(canonical)}`, provider: 'url', url: `${scheme}://${host}${path}` };
}

export function words(text) {
    let current = '';
    const result = [];
    for (const char of typeof text === 'string' ? text : '') {
        const code = char.charCodeAt(0);
        if (code >= 65 && code <= 90) {
            current += String.fromCharCode(code + 32);
        } else if ((code >= 97 && code <= 122) || (code >= 48 && code <= 57)) {
            current += char;
        } else if (current.length > 0) {
            result.push(current);
            current = '';
        }
    }
    if (current.length > 0) {
        result.push(current);
    }
    return result;
}

function unitMarked(text) {
    return typeof text === 'string' ? text.replaceAll('#', ' unit ') : '';
}

export function addressKey(street, unit, zip) {
    if (typeof zip !== 'string' || !/^[0-9]{5}$/.test(zip)) {
        return null;
    }
    const tokens = words(unitMarked(street));
    const split = tokens.findIndex((token, index) => index > 0 && UNIT_WORDS.includes(token));
    const streetTokens = (split > 0 ? tokens.slice(0, split) : tokens).map(token => STREET_WORDS[token] ?? token);
    if (streetTokens.length === 0) {
        return null;
    }
    const unitInput = words(unitMarked(unit));
    const unitSource = unitInput.length > 0 ? unitInput : split > 0 ? tokens.slice(split) : [];
    const unitTokens = unitSource.filter(token => !UNIT_WORDS.includes(token));
    const parts = [zip, ...streetTokens];
    if (unitTokens.length > 0) {
        parts.push('unit', ...unitTokens);
    }
    return keyOf('address', parts.join('-'));
}

function decode(text) {
    try {
        return decodeURIComponent(text);
    } catch {
        return text;
    }
}

function spaced(text) {
    return decode(text).replaceAll('-', ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
}

function splitZip(tokens) {
    const zip = tokens.length > 1 && /^[0-9]{5}$/.test(tokens.at(-1)) ? tokens.at(-1) : '';
    return { zip, rest: zip ? tokens.slice(0, -1) : tokens };
}

export function guessAddress(url) {
    const parts = typeof url === 'string' ? PARTS_PATTERN.exec(url) : null;
    if (!parts) {
        return null;
    }
    const host = parts[2].toLowerCase();
    const segments = parts[3].split('/').filter(segment => segment.length > 0);

    if (host.endsWith('zillow.com')) {
        const index = segments.indexOf('homedetails');
        if (index < 0 || index + 2 >= segments.length) {
            return null;
        }
        const { zip, rest } = splitZip(decode(segments[index + 1]).split('-').filter(Boolean));
        const state = zip && rest.length > 1 && /^[A-Za-z]{2}$/.test(rest.at(-1)) ? rest.at(-1).toUpperCase() : '';
        const street = (state ? rest.slice(0, -1) : rest).join(' ');
        return street || zip ? { street, unit: '', city: '', state, zip, mixed: true } : null;
    }

    if (host.endsWith('redfin.com')) {
        const index = segments.indexOf('home');
        if (index < 3) {
            return null;
        }
        const { zip, rest } = splitZip(decode(segments[2]).split('-').filter(Boolean));
        const unitSegment = index > 3 ? segments[3] : '';
        const unit = /^unit-/i.test(unitSegment) ? spaced(unitSegment.slice(5)) : '';
        const state = /^[A-Za-z]{2}$/.test(segments[0]) ? segments[0].toUpperCase() : '';
        return { street: rest.join(' '), unit, city: spaced(segments[1]), state, zip, mixed: false };
    }

    if (host.endsWith('realtor.com')) {
        const detail = segments.find(segment => /_M[0-9]+-[0-9]+/i.test(segment));
        const fields = detail ? detail.split('_') : [];
        if (fields.length < 5) {
            return null;
        }
        const zip = /^[0-9]{5}$/.test(fields[3]) ? fields[3] : '';
        const state = /^[A-Za-z]{2}$/.test(fields[2]) ? fields[2].toUpperCase() : '';
        return { street: spaced(fields[0]), unit: '', city: spaced(fields[1]), state, zip, mixed: false };
    }

    return null;
}

import { compareKeys, keyLabel, parseLink } from './keys.js';
import { placeOf, streetOf } from './fields.js';

export function newestFirst(a, b) {
    return a.id === b.id ? 0 : a.id < b.id ? 1 : -1;
}

export function oldestFirst(a, b) {
    return -newestFirst(a, b);
}

export function timestampOf(id) {
    return Number.parseInt(id.slice(0, 8) + id.slice(9, 13), 16);
}

class KeySets {
    constructor() {
        this.parent = new Map();
    }

    find(key) {
        let root = key;
        while (this.parent.has(root) && this.parent.get(root) !== root) {
            root = this.parent.get(root);
        }
        let node = key;
        while (node !== root) {
            const next = this.parent.get(node);
            this.parent.set(node, root);
            node = next;
        }
        if (!this.parent.has(root)) {
            this.parent.set(root, root);
        }
        return root;
    }

    union(a, b) {
        const rootA = this.find(a);
        const rootB = this.find(b);
        if (rootA === rootB) {
            return;
        }
        if (compareKeys(rootA, rootB) <= 0) {
            this.parent.set(rootB, rootA);
        } else {
            this.parent.set(rootA, rootB);
        }
    }
}

export function titleOf(listing) {
    return streetOf(listing.fields) || placeOf(listing.fields) || keyLabel(listing.key);
}

export function resolve(entries) {
    const sorted = [...entries].sort(oldestFirst);
    const keys = new Set();
    const links = new Map();
    const fields = {};
    const history = {};
    const comments = [];
    for (const entry of sorted) {
        for (const key of entry.keys) {
            keys.add(key);
        }
        if (entry.kind === 'link' && typeof entry.url === 'string') {
            const parsed = parseLink(entry.url);
            if (parsed) {
                links.set(parsed.key, { key: parsed.key, provider: parsed.provider, url: entry.url, id: entry.id });
            }
        } else if (entry.kind === 'fact' && Array.isArray(entry.fields)) {
            for (const { key, value } of entry.fields) {
                (history[key] ??= []).push({ value, id: entry.id });
                fields[key] = value;
            }
        } else if (entry.kind === 'comment') {
            comments.push(entry);
        }
    }
    for (const key of Object.keys(fields)) {
        if (fields[key] === '') {
            delete fields[key];
        }
    }
    const sortedKeys = [...keys].sort(compareKeys);
    const listing = {
        key: sortedKeys[0],
        keys: sortedKeys,
        links: [...links.values()].sort((a, b) => compareKeys(a.key, b.key)),
        fields,
        history,
        comments,
        ids: sorted.map(entry => entry.id),
        created: sorted[0].id,
        updated: sorted.at(-1).id,
        mine: sorted.some(entry => entry.mine === true),
    };
    listing.title = titleOf(listing);
    return listing;
}

export function buildListings(entries) {
    const sets = new KeySets();
    const usable = [];
    for (const entry of entries) {
        if (!entry || !Array.isArray(entry.keys) || entry.keys.length === 0) {
            continue;
        }
        usable.push(entry);
        for (const key of entry.keys.slice(1)) {
            sets.union(entry.keys[0], key);
        }
    }
    const groups = new Map();
    for (const entry of usable) {
        const root = sets.find(entry.keys[0]);
        const group = groups.get(root);
        if (group) {
            group.push(entry);
        } else {
            groups.set(root, [entry]);
        }
    }
    return [...groups.values()]
        .map(resolve)
        .sort((a, b) => (a.updated === b.updated ? compareKeys(a.key, b.key) : a.updated < b.updated ? 1 : -1));
}

export function findListing(listings, key) {
    return listings.find(listing => listing.keys.includes(key)) ?? null;
}

export function matches(listing, query) {
    const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (needle.length === 0) {
        return true;
    }
    const haystack = [listing.title, placeOf(listing.fields), listing.fields.mls ?? '', ...listing.keys, ...listing.links.map(link => link.url)]
        .join(' ')
        .toLowerCase();
    return needle.split(/\s+/).every(part => haystack.includes(part));
}

export const DB_NAME = 'nest';
export const DB_VERSION = 1;
export const STORE = 'entries';
export const LOCAL_CAP = 20000;
export const DRAFT_PREFIX = 'nest-draft:';

export const migrations = [
    db => db.createObjectStore(STORE, { keyPath: 'id' }),
];

export const SCHEMA = Object.freeze({ name: DB_NAME, version: DB_VERSION, store: STORE, migrations, cap: LOCAL_CAP });

export function upgrade(db, oldVersion, newVersion = DB_VERSION, steps = migrations) {
    for (let version = oldVersion; version < newVersion; version += 1) {
        steps[version](db);
    }
}

export function excessOf(count, cap = LOCAL_CAP) {
    return Math.max(0, count - cap);
}

function settle(request) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
        request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
    });
}

function finished(transaction) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
        transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
        transaction.onabort = () => reject(transaction.error);
    });
}

export function deleteDatabase(factory, name = DB_NAME) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        if (!factory) {
            resolve('deleted');
            return;
        }
        const request = factory.deleteDatabase(name);
        request.onsuccess = () => resolve('deleted');
        request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
        request.onblocked = () => resolve('blocked');
    });
}

export function openDatabase(factory, schema = SCHEMA, onClose = () => {}, allowReset = true) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const request = factory.open(schema.name, schema.version);
        request.onupgradeneeded = event => upgrade(request.result, event.oldVersion, event.newVersion ?? schema.version, schema.migrations);
        request.onsuccess = () => {
            const db = request.result;
            db.onversionchange = () => {
                db.close();
                onClose();
            };
            db.onclose = onClose;
            resolve(db);
        };
        request.onerror = event => {
            if (allowReset && request.error?.name === 'VersionError') {
                event.preventDefault();
                deleteDatabase(factory, schema.name)
                    .then(() => openDatabase(factory, schema, onClose, false))
                    .then(resolve, reject);
                return;
            }
            reject(request.error);
        };
    });
}

export function draftKeys(storage) {
    const keys = [];
    try {
        for (let index = 0; index < storage.length; index += 1) {
            const key = storage.key(index);
            if (key?.startsWith(DRAFT_PREFIX)) {
                keys.push(key);
            }
        }
    } catch {
    }
    return keys;
}

export function readDraft(storage, key) {
    try {
        return storage?.getItem(DRAFT_PREFIX + key) ?? '';
    } catch {
        return '';
    }
}

export function writeDraft(storage, key, text) {
    try {
        if (text) {
            storage?.setItem(DRAFT_PREFIX + key, text);
        } else {
            storage?.removeItem(DRAFT_PREFIX + key);
        }
    } catch {
    }
}

export class EntryStore {
    constructor(factory, schema = SCHEMA) {
        this.factory = factory ?? null;
        this.schema = schema;
        this.db = null;
        this.opening = null;
    }

    database() {
        if (this.db) {
            return Promise.resolve(this.db);
        }
        if (!this.factory) {
            return Promise.resolve(null);
        }
        this.opening ??= openDatabase(this.factory, this.schema, () => {
            this.db = null;
        })
            .then(db => {
                this.db = db;
                return db;
            }, () => {
                this.factory = null;
                return null;
            })
            .finally(() => {
                this.opening = null;
            });
        return this.opening;
    }

    async all() {
        const db = await this.database();
        if (!db) {
            return [];
        }
        return settle(db.transaction(this.schema.store, 'readonly').objectStore(this.schema.store).getAll());
    }

    async save(records) {
        const db = await this.database();
        if (!db || records.length === 0) {
            return [];
        }
        const transaction = db.transaction(this.schema.store, 'readwrite');
        const done = finished(transaction);
        const store = transaction.objectStore(this.schema.store);
        for (const record of records) {
            store.put(record);
        }
        let evicted = [];
        const excess = excessOf(await settle(store.count()), this.schema.cap);
        if (excess > 0) {
            evicted = await settle(store.getAllKeys(null, excess));
            store.delete(IDBKeyRange.upperBound(evicted.at(-1)));
        }
        await done;
        return evicted;
    }
}

import { EntryStore } from './store.js';
import { buildListings, newestFirst } from './merge.js';

export const OFFER_BYTES = 24000;
export const FETCH_CHUNK = 50;
export const PING_MS = 25000;
export const FALLBACK_MS = 3000;
export const RECONNECTED_EVENT = 'nest:reconnected';

export function needsResync(version, update) {
    return update.previous !== version;
}

export function wire(entry) {
    const result = { id: entry.id, kind: entry.kind, keys: entry.keys };
    if (typeof entry.url === 'string') {
        result.url = entry.url;
    }
    if (Array.isArray(entry.fields)) {
        result.fields = entry.fields;
    }
    if (typeof entry.text === 'string') {
        result.text = entry.text;
    }
    return result;
}

export function missingIds(ids, have) {
    return ids.filter(id => !have.has(id));
}

export function offerCandidates(local, serverIds, capacity) {
    let oldest = null;
    if (serverIds.size >= capacity) {
        for (const id of serverIds) {
            if (oldest === null || id < oldest) {
                oldest = id;
            }
        }
    }
    return local
        .filter(entry => !serverIds.has(entry.id) && (oldest === null || entry.id > oldest))
        .sort(newestFirst)
        .slice(0, capacity)
        .map(wire);
}

export function chunkBySize(items, maxBytes = OFFER_BYTES) {
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const chunks = [];
    let chunk = [];
    let size = 0;
    for (const item of items) {
        const bytes = encoder.encode(JSON.stringify(item)).length + 1;
        if (bytes > maxBytes) {
            continue;
        }
        if (chunk.length > 0 && size + bytes > maxBytes) {
            chunks.push(chunk);
            chunk = [];
            size = 0;
        }
        chunk.push(item);
        size += bytes;
    }
    if (chunk.length > 0) {
        chunks.push(chunk);
    }
    return chunks;
}

export function chunk(items, size) {
    const chunks = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < items.length; index += size) {
        chunks.push(items.slice(index, index + size));
    }
    return chunks;
}

export class Nest {
    constructor(win, store = new EntryStore(win.indexedDB)) {
        this.win = win;
        this.store = store;
        this.entries = new Map();
        this.server = new Set();
        this.capacity = 0;
        this.version = -1;
        this.dotnet = null;
        this.status = 'local';
        this.ready = false;
        this.revision = 0;
        this.cached = null;
        this.cachedRevision = -1;
        this.listeners = new Set();
        this.joining = null;
        this.reconciling = null;
        this.timer = 0;
        this.fallback = win.setTimeout(() => this.markReady(), FALLBACK_MS);
        this.emitting = false;
        this.onReconnected = () => this.join();
        win.addEventListener(RECONNECTED_EVENT, this.onReconnected);
        this.loaded = this.load();
    }

    async load() {
        try {
            for (const record of await this.store.all()) {
                this.entries.set(record.id, record);
            }
        } catch {
        }
        this.changed();
    }

    listen(listener) {
        this.listeners.add(listener);
        return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
    }

    changed() {
        this.revision += 1;
        if (this.emitting) {
            return;
        }
        this.emitting = true;
        queueMicrotask(() => {
            this.emitting = false;
            for (const listener of [...this.listeners]) {
                try {
                    listener(this);
                } catch {
                }
            }
        });
    }

    markReady() {
        this.win.clearTimeout(this.fallback);
        if (!this.ready) {
            this.ready = true;
            this.changed();
        }
    }

    setStatus(status) {
        if (this.status !== status) {
            this.status = status;
            this.changed();
        }
    }

    listings() {
        if (this.cachedRevision !== this.revision) {
            this.cached = buildListings(this.entries.values());
            this.cachedRevision = this.revision;
        }
        return this.cached;
    }

    isLive(id) {
        return this.server.has(id);
    }

    get live() {
        return this.status === 'live' && this.dotnet !== null;
    }

    async connect(dotnet) {
        this.dotnet = dotnet;
        this.setStatus('connecting');
        this.win.clearInterval(this.timer);
        this.timer = this.win.setInterval(() => this.ping(), PING_MS);
        await this.loaded;
        await this.join();
    }

    disconnect(dotnet) {
        if (this.dotnet !== dotnet) {
            return;
        }
        this.dotnet = null;
        this.version = -1;
        this.win.clearInterval(this.timer);
        this.setStatus('local');
    }

    join() {
        const dotnet = this.dotnet;
        if (!dotnet) {
            return Promise.resolve();
        }
        this.version = -1;
        this.joining ??= dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Join')
            .catch(() => this.setStatus('offline'))
            .finally(() => {
                this.joining = null;
            });
        return this.joining;
    }

    async ping() {
        const dotnet = this.dotnet;
        if (!dotnet || this.joining) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            if (!(await dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Ping'))) {
                await this.join();
            }
        } catch {
            this.setStatus('offline');
        }
    }

    async apply(updates, dotnet) {
        if (dotnet !== this.dotnet) {
            return;
        }
        for (const update of updates) {
            if (update.kind === 'snapshot') {
                this.snapshot(update);
            } else if (update.kind === 'delta' && this.version >= 0) {
                if (needsResync(this.version, update)) {
                    this.join();
                    return;
                }
                await this.delta(update);
            }
        }
    }

    snapshot(update) {
        this.version = update.version;
        this.capacity = update.capacity;
        this.server = new Set(update.ids);
        this.setStatus('live');
        this.changed();
        this.reconciling = this.reconcile(this.dotnet).finally(() => {
            this.reconciling = null;
        });
    }

    async reconcile(dotnet) {
        try {
            for (const ids of chunk(missingIds([...this.server], this.entries), FETCH_CHUNK)) {
                if (dotnet !== this.dotnet) {
                    return;
                }
                const fetched = await dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Fetch', ids);
                await this.add(fetched ?? []);
            }
            const offers = offerCandidates([...this.entries.values()], this.server, this.capacity);
            for (const offered of chunkBySize(offers)) {
                if (dotnet !== this.dotnet) {
                    return;
                }
                await dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Offer', offered);
            }
        } catch {
        } finally {
            this.markReady();
        }
    }

    async delta(update) {
        this.version = update.version;
        for (const id of update.removed) {
            this.server.delete(id);
        }
        for (const entry of update.added) {
            this.server.add(entry.id);
        }
        await this.add(update.added);
        this.changed();
    }

    async add(entries, mine = false) {
        const records = [];
        for (const entry of entries) {
            if (!entry || typeof entry.id !== 'string') {
                continue;
            }
            const known = this.entries.get(entry.id);
            if (known && !(mine && !known.mine)) {
                continue;
            }
            const record = wire(entry);
            if (mine || known?.mine) {
                record.mine = true;
            }
            this.entries.set(record.id, record);
            records.push(record);
        }
        if (records.length === 0) {
            return;
        }
        this.changed();
        try {
            for (const id of await this.store.save(records)) {
                this.entries.delete(id);
            }
        } catch {
        }
    }

    async post(draft) {
        const dotnet = this.dotnet;
        if (!dotnet || this.status !== 'live') {
            return { result: 'offline', entry: null, key: null };
        }
        try {
            const outcome = await dotnet.invokeMethodAsync('Post', draft);
            if (outcome?.entry) {
                this.server.add(outcome.entry.id);
                await this.add([outcome.entry], true);
            }
            return outcome ?? { result: 'invalid', entry: null, key: null };
        } catch {
            return { result: 'offline', entry: null, key: null };
        }
    }

    async clear() {
        this.entries.clear();
        this.server.clear();
        this.changed();
    }
}

let instance = null;

export function nest(win) {
    instance ??= new Nest(win);
    return instance;
}

export function current() {
    return instance;
}

using Microsoft.Playwright;
using Xunit.Sdk;
using Xunit.v3;

[assembly: Parallelization(Mode = ParallelMode.None)]

namespace Nest.E2E;

public sealed class BrowserFixture : IAsyncLifetime
{
    private IPlaywright? _playwright;
    private IBrowser? _browser;

    public static string BaseUrl { get; } = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("NEST_BASE_URL") ?? "http://127.0.0.1:5080/";

    public async ValueTask InitializeAsync()
    {
        _playwright = await Playwright.CreateAsync();
        _browser = await _playwright.Chromium.LaunchAsync();
    }

    public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()
    {
        if (_browser is not null)
        {
            await _browser.DisposeAsync();
        }

        _playwright?.Dispose();
    }

    public Task<IBrowserContext> NewContextAsync(int width = 1280, int height = 800)
    {
        var browser = _browser ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("The browser is not running.");
        return browser.NewContextAsync(new BrowserNewContextOptions
        {
            BaseURL = BaseUrl,
            ColorScheme = ColorScheme.Light,
            ViewportSize = new ViewportSize { Width = width, Height = height },
        });
    }
}

using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using Microsoft.Playwright;
using static Microsoft.Playwright.Assertions;

namespace Nest.E2E;

public sealed partial class HomesTests(BrowserFixture browser) : IClassFixture<BrowserFixture>
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task APastedLinkOpensItsHomeForEveryone()
    {
        await using var first = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await using var second = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var watcher = await Pages.OpenHomesAsync(second);
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();

        var page = await Pages.AddLinkAsync(first, $"Look at this one https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/2893-State-Route-26-Glen-Aubrey-NY-13777/{number}_zpid/?utm_source=share.");

        await Expect(page).ToHaveURLAsync(new Regex($"/homes/zillow/{number}$"));
        await Expect(page.Locator("#listing-title")).ToHaveTextAsync($"Zillow {number}");
        await Expect(page.Locator("#listing-links a")).ToHaveAttributeAsync("href", $"https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/2893-State-Route-26-Glen-Aubrey-NY-13777/{number}_zpid/");
        await Expect(watcher.Locator($"#homes-list .home[data-key=\"zillow:{number}\"]")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task TheSameLinkWithOtherParametersOpensTheSameHome()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();
        await Pages.AddLinkAsync(context, $"https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/{number}_zpid/");

        var page = await Pages.OpenHomesAsync(context);
        await page.Locator("#link-input").FillAsync($"https://zillow.com/homedetails/Some-Street-City-ST-12345/{number}_zpid/?mmlb=g,0");
        await Expect(page.Locator("#link-preview")).ToContainTextAsync("Already here");
        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Open" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page).ToHaveURLAsync(new Regex($"/homes/zillow/{number}$"));
        await Expect(page.Locator("#listing-links li")).ToHaveCountAsync(1);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task DetailsAreSharedLiveAndSuggestedFromTheLink()
    {
        await using var editorContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await using var readerContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();
        var editor = await Pages.AddLinkAsync(editorContext, $"https://www.realtor.com/realestateandhomes-detail/{number}-Oak-Ave_Glen-Aubrey_NY_13777_M{number}-11111");
        var reader = await readerContext.NewPageAsync();
        await reader.GotoAsync($"/homes/realtor/m{number}-11111");
        await Pages.WaitLiveAsync(reader);

        await editor.Locator("#facts-editor summary").ClickAsync();
        await Expect(editor.Locator("#facts-suggestion")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(editor.Locator("#fact-street")).ToHaveValueAsync($"{number} Oak Ave");
        await Expect(editor.Locator("#fact-zip")).ToHaveValueAsync("13777");

        await Pages.SaveDetailsAsync(editor, ("price", "450k"), ("beds", "3"), ("baths", "2.50"), ("status", "For sale"));

        await Expect(editor.Locator("#listing-title")).ToHaveTextAsync($"{number} Oak Ave");
        await Expect(reader.Locator("#listing-title")).ToHaveTextAsync($"{number} Oak Ave");
        await Expect(reader.Locator("#listing-summary")).ToHaveTextAsync("$450,000 · 3 bd · 2.5 ba · For sale");
        await Expect(reader.Locator("#listing-place")).ToHaveTextAsync("Glen Aubrey, NY 13777");

        await Pages.SaveDetailsAsync(reader, ("price", "435000"));
        await Expect(editor.Locator("#listing-facts")).ToContainTextAsync("$435,000 was $450,000");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task BadValuesAreExplainedBeforeSending()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await Pages.AddLinkAsync(context, $"https://www.redfin.com/home/{Pages.UniqueNumber()}");
        await page.Locator("#facts-editor summary").ClickAsync();
        await page.Locator("#fact-zip").FillAsync("123");
        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Save details" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#fact-zip-error")).ToHaveTextAsync("Use a five-digit ZIP code.");
        await Expect(page.Locator("#fact-zip")).ToBeFocusedAsync();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task CommentsReachOtherBrowsersAndTheirStorage()
    {
        await using var writerContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await using var readerContext = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();
        var writer = await Pages.AddLinkAsync(writerContext, $"https://www.redfin.com/NY/Glen-Aubrey/1-Elm-St-13777/home/{number}");
        var reader = await readerContext.NewPageAsync();
        await reader.GotoAsync($"/homes/redfin/{number}");
        await Pages.WaitLiveAsync(reader);
        var text = $"Big yard 🌳 {Guid.NewGuid():N}";

        await Pages.CommentAsync(writer, text);

        await Expect(writer.Locator("#comments .comment.mine .yours")).ToHaveTextAsync("You");
        var item = reader.Locator("#comments .comment", new() { HasText = text });
        await Expect(item).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await Expect(item).Not.ToHaveClassAsync(Archived());
        await Pages.EventuallyAsync(async () => (await reader.EvaluateAsync<string[]>(Pages.StoredTexts)).Contains(text));
        await Expect(writer.Locator("#comment-text")).ToHaveValueAsync("");
        await Expect(writer.Locator("#comment-count")).ToHaveTextAsync("0 / 4000");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task UnsentCommentsSurviveAReload()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await Pages.AddLinkAsync(context, $"https://www.redfin.com/home/{Pages.UniqueNumber()}");
        await page.Locator("#comment-text").PressSequentiallyAsync("half a thought");
        await page.ReloadAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#comment-text")).ToHaveValueAsync("half a thought");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task HomesWithTheSameAddressMergeIntoOnePage()
    {
        await using var first = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await using var second = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();
        var zillow = await Pages.AddLinkAsync(first, $"https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/{number}_zpid/");
        await Pages.SaveDetailsAsync(zillow, ("street", $"{number} Maple Street"), ("zip", "13777"), ("beds", "3"));
        await Pages.CommentAsync(zillow, "Seen on Zillow");

        var redfin = await Pages.AddLinkAsync(second, $"https://www.redfin.com/home/{number}");
        await Pages.SaveDetailsAsync(redfin, ("street", $"{number} MAPLE ST"), ("zip", "13777"), ("baths", "2"));
        await Pages.CommentAsync(redfin, "Seen on Redfin");

        await Expect(zillow.Locator("#listing-links")).ToContainTextAsync("Open on Redfin");
        await Expect(zillow.Locator("#comments")).ToContainTextAsync("Seen on Redfin");
        await Expect(zillow.Locator("#listing-summary")).ToHaveTextAsync("3 bd · 2 ba");
        await Expect(redfin.Locator("#comments")).ToContainTextAsync("Seen on Zillow");

        var home = await Pages.OpenHomesAsync(first);
        await Expect(home.Locator("#homes-list .home", new() { HasText = $"{number} MAPLE ST" })).ToHaveCountAsync(1);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task AHomeCanBeAddedByAddress()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await Pages.OpenHomesAsync(context);
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();
        await page.Locator("details.by-address summary").ClickAsync();
        await page.Locator("#add-street").FillAsync($"{number} Birch Lane Apt 4");
        await page.Locator("#add-zip").FillAsync("10019");
        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Add home" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page).ToHaveURLAsync(new Regex($"/homes/address/10019-{number}-birch-ln-unit-4$"));
        await Expect(page.Locator("#listing-title")).ToHaveTextAsync($"{number} Birch Lane Apt 4");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task BrowsersRestoreWhatTheServerForgot()
    {
        await using var keeper = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        await using var stranger = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();
        var seeder = await keeper.NewPageAsync();
        await seeder.GotoAsync("/about");
        var old = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddDays(-30).ToUnixTimeMilliseconds();
        var linkId = await seeder.EvaluateAsync<string>(Pages.IdAt, old);
        var commentId = await seeder.EvaluateAsync<string>(Pages.IdAt, old + 1000);
        await seeder.EvaluateAsync(Pages.Seed, new object[]
        {
            new { id = linkId, kind = "link", keys = new[] { $"zillow:{number}" }, url = $"https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/{number}_zpid/" },
            new { id = commentId, kind = "comment", keys = new[] { $"zillow:{number}" }, text = "Kept by one browser" },
        });

        await seeder.GotoAsync($"/homes/zillow/{number}");
        await Expect(seeder.Locator("#comments .comment", new() { HasText = "Kept by one browser" })).Not.ToHaveClassAsync(Archived());

        var fresh = await stranger.NewPageAsync();
        await fresh.GotoAsync($"/homes/zillow/{number}");
        await Expect(fresh.Locator("#comments .comment", new() { HasText = "Kept by one browser" })).ToBeVisibleAsync();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task EntriesTheServerRefusedHaveADottedBorder()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        await page.GotoAsync("/about");
        var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds();
        var id = await page.EvaluateAsync<string>(Pages.IdAt, now);
        await page.EvaluateAsync(Pages.Seed, new object[]
        {
            new { id, kind = "comment", keys = new[] { $"redfin:{number}" }, text = "Only in this browser" },
        });

        await page.GotoAsync($"/homes/redfin/{number}");
        await Pages.WaitLiveAsync(page);
        var item = page.Locator("#comments .comment", new() { HasText = "Only in this browser" });
        await Expect(item).ToHaveClassAsync(Archived());
        await Expect(item).ToHaveCSSAsync("border-top-style", "dotted");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ClearingThisBrowserKeepsTheServerCopy()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();
        var page = await Pages.AddLinkAsync(context, $"https://www.redfin.com/home/{number}");
        await Pages.CommentAsync(page, $"Remember me {number}");

        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Navigation).GetByRole(AriaRole.Link, new() { Name = "About" }).ClickAsync();
        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Delete this browser's copy" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#clear-status")).ToHaveTextAsync("This browser's copy is gone. Other browsers keep theirs.");
        Assert.Empty(await page.EvaluateAsync<string[]>(Pages.StoredTexts));

        await page.GotoAsync($"/homes/redfin/{number}");
        await Expect(page.Locator("#comments")).ToContainTextAsync($"Remember me {number}");
        await Pages.EventuallyAsync(async () => (await page.EvaluateAsync<string[]>(Pages.StoredTexts)).Contains($"Remember me {number}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task TheListCanBeFiltered()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();
        var listing = await Pages.AddLinkAsync(context, $"https://www.redfin.com/home/{number}");
        await Pages.SaveDetailsAsync(listing, ("city", $"Filterville{number}"));
        var page = await Pages.OpenHomesAsync(context);

        await page.Locator("#homes-filter").FillAsync($"filterville{number}");
        await Expect(page.Locator("#homes-list .home")).ToHaveCountAsync(1);
        await page.Locator("#homes-filter").FillAsync($"nowhere{number}");
        await Expect(page.Locator("#homes-list .home")).ToHaveCountAsync(0);
        await Expect(page.Locator("#homes-empty")).ToHaveTextAsync("No homes match.");
    }

    [GeneratedRegex(@"\barchived\b")]
    private static partial Regex Archived();
}

using Microsoft.Playwright;
using static Microsoft.Playwright.Assertions;

namespace Nest.E2E;

internal static class Pages
{
    public const string StoredTexts = """
        () => new Promise(resolve => {
            const request = indexedDB.open('nest');
            request.onupgradeneeded = () => request.transaction.abort();
            request.onsuccess = () => {
                const db = request.result;
                const all = db.transaction('entries', 'readonly').objectStore('entries').getAll();
                all.onsuccess = () => { db.close(); resolve(all.result.filter(e => e.text).map(e => e.text)); };
                all.onerror = () => { db.close(); resolve([]); };
            };
            request.onerror = () => resolve([]);
        })
        """;

    public const string Seed = """
        records => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const request = indexedDB.open('nest', 1);
            request.onupgradeneeded = () => request.result.createObjectStore('entries', { keyPath: 'id' });
            request.onsuccess = () => {
                const db = request.result;
                const transaction = db.transaction('entries', 'readwrite');
                for (const record of records) { transaction.objectStore('entries').put(record); }
                transaction.oncomplete = () => { db.close(); resolve(); };
                transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
            };
            request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
        })
        """;

    public const string IdAt = """
        ms => {
            const hex = ms.toString(16).padStart(12, '0');
            const random = Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(10)), b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
            return `${hex.slice(0, 8)}-${hex.slice(8, 12)}-7${random.slice(0, 3)}-8${random.slice(3, 6)}-${random.slice(6, 18)}`;
        }
        """;

    public static int UniqueNumber() => Random.Shared.Next(10_000_000, 99_999_999);

    public static async Task<IPage> OpenHomesAsync(IBrowserContext context)
    {
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        await page.GotoAsync("/");
        await WaitLiveAsync(page);
        return page;
    }

    public static async Task WaitLiveAsync(IPage page)
    {
        await Expect(page.Locator("#sync-status")).ToHaveAttributeAsync("data-status", "live");
    }

    public static async Task<IPage> AddLinkAsync(IBrowserContext context, string text)
    {
        var page = await OpenHomesAsync(context);
        await page.Locator("#link-input").FillAsync(text);
        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Open" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#listing-title")).ToBeVisibleAsync();
        await WaitLiveAsync(page);
        return page;
    }

    public static async Task SaveDetailsAsync(IPage page, params (string Key, string Value)[] values)
    {
        var editor = page.Locator("#facts-editor");
        if (await editor.GetAttributeAsync("open") is null)
        {
            await editor.Locator("summary").ClickAsync();
        }

        foreach (var (key, value) in values)
        {
            var field = page.Locator($"#fact-{key}");
            if (await field.EvaluateAsync<string>("element => element.tagName") == "SELECT")
            {
                await field.SelectOptionAsync(value);
            }
            else
            {
                await field.FillAsync(value);
            }
        }

        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Save details" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page.Locator("#facts-status")).ToHaveTextAsync("Saved.");
    }

    public static async Task CommentAsync(IPage page, string text)
    {
        await page.Locator("#comment-text").FillAsync(text);
        await page.Locator("#comment-text").PressAsync("Control+Enter");
        await Expect(page.Locator("#comments .comment", new() { HasText = text })).ToBeVisibleAsync();
    }

    public static async Task EventuallyAsync(Func<Task<bool>> condition)
    {
        for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 50; attempt++)
        {
            if (await condition())
            {
                return;
            }

            await Task.Delay(100, TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);
        }

        Assert.True(await condition());
    }
}

using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using Microsoft.Playwright;
using static Microsoft.Playwright.Assertions;

namespace Nest.E2E;

public sealed partial class SiteTests(BrowserFixture browser) : IClassFixture<BrowserFixture>
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task PagesLoadWithoutErrorsOrCookies()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        var errors = new List<string>();
        page.Console += (_, message) =>
        {
            if (message.Type == "error")
            {
                errors.Add(message.Text);
            }
        };
        page.PageError += (_, error) => errors.Add(error);

        foreach (var path in new[] { "/about", "/homes/zillow/123", "/" })
        {
            await page.GotoAsync(path);
        }

        await Pages.WaitLiveAsync(page);
        Assert.Empty(errors);
        Assert.Empty(await context.CookiesAsync());
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task OnlyTheHomePagesOpenAWebSocket()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        var sockets = new List<string>();
        page.WebSocket += (_, socket) => sockets.Add(socket.Url);

        await page.GotoAsync("/about");
        await page.WaitForTimeoutAsync(1000);
        Assert.Empty(sockets);

        await page.GotoAsync("/");
        await Pages.WaitLiveAsync(page);
        Assert.NotEmpty(sockets);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(320, 640)]
    [InlineData(390, 844)]
    public async Task NoPageScrollsSidewaysOnAPhone(int width, int height)
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync(width, height);
        var number = Pages.UniqueNumber();
        var page = await Pages.AddLinkAsync(context, $"https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/{new string('x', 120)}/{number}_zpid/");
        await Pages.CommentAsync(page, $"{Guid.NewGuid():N}{new string('w', 300)}");

        foreach (var path in new[] { "/", $"/homes/zillow/{number}", "/about", "/no-such-page" })
        {
            await page.GotoAsync(path);
            var nav = page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Navigation, new() { Name = "Main" });
            await Expect(nav.GetByRole(AriaRole.Link, new() { Name = "Homes", Exact = true })).ToBeInViewportAsync();
            var widths = await page.EvaluateAsync<int[]>("() => [document.documentElement.scrollWidth, document.documentElement.clientWidth]");
            Assert.True(widths[0] <= widths[1], $"{path} is {widths[0]} px wide in a {widths[1]} px viewport.");
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ThemeChoiceSurvivesReloadAndNavigation()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();
        var root = page.Locator("html");

        await page.GotoAsync("/about");
        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Button, new() { Name = "Dark theme" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(root).ToHaveAttributeAsync("data-theme", "dark");

        await page.ReloadAsync();
        await Expect(root).ToHaveAttributeAsync("data-theme", "dark");

        await page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Navigation).GetByRole(AriaRole.Link, new() { Name = "Homes" }).ClickAsync();
        await Expect(page).ToHaveURLAsync(HomeUrl());
        await Expect(root).ToHaveAttributeAsync("data-theme", "dark");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ResponsesCarryAStrictPolicyAndACustomNotFoundPage()
    {
        await using var context = await browser.NewContextAsync();
        var page = await context.NewPageAsync();

        var home = await page.GotoAsync("/") ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No response.");
        Assert.StartsWith("default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'", home.Headers["content-security-policy"], StringComparison.Ordinal);

        var missing = await page.GotoAsync("/no-such-page") ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No response.");
        Assert.Equal(404, missing.Status);
        await Expect(page.GetByRole(AriaRole.Heading, new() { Name = "Nothing here" })).ToBeVisibleAsync();
    }

    [GeneratedRegex("/$")]
    private static partial Regex HomeUrl();
}

using System.Text.Json;
using Nest.Web.Listings;

namespace Nest.Web.Tests;

public sealed class EntryRulesTests
{
    private static readonly Guid Id = Guid.Parse("01950000-0001-7000-8000-000000000001");

    [Fact]
    public void LinksAreKeyedByTheirListingAndStripped()
    {
        var entry = EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Link("See https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/29747113_zpid/?utm=1 now", "address:13777-main", "bad"));
        Assert.NotNull(entry);
        Assert.Equal(EntryKinds.Link, entry.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(["address:13777-main", "zillow:29747113"], entry.Keys);
        Assert.Equal("https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/29747113_zpid/", entry.Url);
        Assert.Null(entry.Fields);
        Assert.Null(entry.Text);
        Assert.Equal("zillow:29747113", EntryRules.DerivedKey(entry));
        Assert.Null(EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Link("no link here")));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FactsAreNormalizedOrderedAndKeyedByAddress()
    {
        var entry = EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Fact([], ("zip", "13777-1234"), ("price", "450k"), ("street", "2893 State Route 26"), ("price", "1"), ("pool", "yes")));
        Assert.NotNull(entry);
        Assert.Equal(["address:13777-2893-state-rte-26"], entry.Keys);
        Assert.Equal([new FieldValue("price", "450000"), new FieldValue("street", "2893 State Route 26"), new FieldValue("zip", "13777")], entry.Fields);
        Assert.Equal("address:13777-2893-state-rte-26", EntryRules.DerivedKey(entry));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FactsNeedAKeyAndValidValues()
    {
        Assert.Null(EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Fact([], ("beds", "3"))));
        Assert.Null(EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Fact(["zillow:1"], ("beds", "lots"))));
        Assert.Null(EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Fact(["zillow:1"])));
        Assert.Null(EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Fact(["zillow:1"], ("pool", "yes"))));
        var cleared = EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Fact(["zillow:1"], ("beds", " ")));
        Assert.Equal([new FieldValue("beds", "")], cleared?.Fields);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CommentsNeedTextAndAKey()
    {
        var entry = EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Comment("  Roof looks new 🏠\n\n\n\nGood  ", "zillow:1"));
        Assert.Equal("Roof looks new 🏠\n\nGood", entry?.Text);
        Assert.Null(EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Comment("   ", "zillow:1")));
        Assert.Null(EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Comment("hello")));
        Assert.Null(EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Comment("hello", "bad")));
        Assert.Null(EntryRules.Build(Id, new EntryDraft("poll", ["zillow:1"], null, null, "x")));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RecognizesNormalizedEntries()
    {
        var link = EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Link("https://www.redfin.com/home/5"))!;
        var fact = EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Fact(["zillow:1"], ("beds", "3")))!;
        var comment = EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Comment("hi", "zillow:1"))!;
        Assert.True(EntryRules.IsNormalized(link));
        Assert.True(EntryRules.IsNormalized(fact));
        Assert.True(EntryRules.IsNormalized(comment));
        Assert.False(EntryRules.IsNormalized(null));
        Assert.False(EntryRules.IsNormalized(link with { Url = "https://www.redfin.com/home/5?x=1" }));
        Assert.False(EntryRules.IsNormalized(link with { Keys = ["zillow:9"] }));
        Assert.False(EntryRules.IsNormalized(link with { Text = "extra" }));
        Assert.False(EntryRules.IsNormalized(fact with { Fields = [new FieldValue("beds", "03")] }));
        Assert.False(EntryRules.IsNormalized(comment with { Text = " hi " }));
        Assert.False(EntryRules.IsNormalized(comment with { Keys = ["zillow:1", "redfin:2", "zillow:1"] }));
        Assert.True(EntryRules.IsNormalized(comment with { Keys = ["redfin:2", "zillow:1"] }));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ComparesEntriesByValue()
    {
        var a = EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Fact(["zillow:1"], ("beds", "3")))!;
        var b = EntryRules.Build(Id, Drafts.Fact(["zillow:1"], ("beds", "3")))!;
        Assert.True(EntryRules.Same(a, b));
        Assert.False(EntryRules.Same(a, b with { Fields = null }));
    }
}

public sealed class WireTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void ReadsEntriesFromTheBrowser()
    {
        using var json = JsonDocument.Parse("""[{"id":"01950000-0001-7000-8000-000000000001","kind":"comment","keys":["zillow:1"],"text":"hi"},{"id":"nope","kind":"comment","keys":[]},null]""");
        var wires = Wire.Read<EntryWire?[]>(json.RootElement);
        Assert.NotNull(wires);
        Assert.Equal(3, wires.Length);
        var entry = wires[0]?.ToEntry();
        Assert.Equal("hi", entry?.Text);
        Assert.Equal(["zillow:1"], entry?.Keys);
        Assert.Null(wires[1]?.ToEntry());
        Assert.Null(wires[2]);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RejectsMalformedPayloads()
    {
        using var json = JsonDocument.Parse("""{"kind":42}""");
        Assert.Null(Wire.Read<EntryDraft>(json.RootElement));
        Assert.Null(new EntryWire("01950000-0001-7000-8000-000000000001", "fact", ["zillow:1"], null, [new FieldWire(null, "x")], null).ToEntry());
        Assert.Null(new EntryWire("01950000-0001-7000-8000-000000000001", "fact", ["zillow:1", null], null, null, null).ToEntry());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WritesCamelCaseWithoutNulls()
    {
        var entry = new Entry(Guid.Parse("01950000-0001-7000-8000-000000000001"), EntryKinds.Comment, ["zillow:1"], Text: "hi");
        var json = Wire.Write(PostOutcome.From(new SubmitReply(SubmitResult.Accepted, entry, "zillow:1"))).GetRawText();
        Assert.Equal("""{"result":"accepted","entry":{"id":"01950000-0001-7000-8000-000000000001","kind":"comment","keys":["zillow:1"],"text":"hi"},"key":"zillow:1"}""", json);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NamesResultsForTheBrowser()
    {
        Assert.Equal("accepted", PostOutcome.NameOf(SubmitResult.Accepted));
        Assert.Equal("exists", PostOutcome.NameOf(SubmitResult.Exists));
        Assert.Equal("invalid", PostOutcome.NameOf(SubmitResult.Invalid));
        Assert.Equal("tooFast", PostOutcome.NameOf(SubmitResult.TooFast));
        Assert.Equal("repeat", PostOutcome.NameOf(SubmitResult.Repeat));
    }
}

using System.Net;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Hosting;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Testing;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Nest.Web.Infrastructure;
using Nest.Web.Listings;

namespace Nest.Web.Tests;

public sealed class NestFactory : WebApplicationFactory<Program>
{
    public const string AllowedOrigin = "https://nest.example";

    protected override void ConfigureWebHost(IWebHostBuilder builder)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(builder);
        builder.UseSetting("Nest:AllowedOrigins:0", AllowedOrigin);
        builder.UseSetting("Nest:PostBurst", "1000");
    }
}

public sealed class HttpPipelineTests(NestFactory factory) : IClassFixture<NestFactory>
{
    private static CancellationToken Cancellation => TestContext.Current.CancellationToken;

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("/", "Nest")]
    [InlineData("/about", "About Nest")]
    [InlineData("/homes/zillow/123", "Zillow 123")]
    public async Task PagesRenderWithoutSettingCookies(string path, string title)
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync(path, Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.False(response.Headers.Contains("Set-Cookie"));
        Assert.Contains($"<title>{title}", await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(Cancellation), StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task HomePageRendersServerListings()
    {
        var nest = factory.Services.GetRequiredService<NestService>();
        var id = Random.Shared.Next(1_000_000, 9_999_999);
        var reply = nest.Submit(Drafts.Link($"https://www.redfin.com/home/{id}"), "integration");
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Accepted, reply.Result);
        nest.Submit(Drafts.Fact([$"redfin:{id}"], ("street", $"{id} Integration Way"), ("zip", "13777"), ("beds", "3")), "integration");

        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync("/", Cancellation);
        Assert.Contains($"href=\"homes/redfin/{id}\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains($"{id} Integration Way", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("3 bd", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("data-view=\"homes\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ListingPageRendersMergedDetailsAndComments()
    {
        var nest = factory.Services.GetRequiredService<NestService>();
        var id = Random.Shared.Next(1_000_000, 9_999_999);
        nest.Submit(Drafts.Link($"https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/{id}_zpid/"), "integration");
        nest.Submit(Drafts.Fact([$"zillow:{id}"], ("street", $"{id} Merge Road"), ("zip", "10019"), ("price", "450k")), "integration");
        nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("Lovely porch 🏡", $"address:10019-{id}-merge-rd"), "integration");

        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync($"/homes/address/10019-{id}-merge-rd", Cancellation);
        Assert.Contains("<h1 id=\"listing-title\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains($"{id} Merge Road", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("$450,000", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("Open on Zillow", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("Lovely porch", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains($"data-key=\"address:10019-{id}-merge-rd\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("id=\"facts-form\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task UnknownHomesStillRenderSoTheBrowserCanLook()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();

        Assert.Contains("id=\"listing-missing\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("data-key=\"redfin:42\"", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [InlineData("/homes/zillow/abc")]
    [InlineData("/homes/mars/1")]
    [InlineData("/homes/address/1-main")]
    public async Task InvalidHomeAddressesAreNotFound(string path)
    {

        Assert.Contains("Nothing here", await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(Cancellation), StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ResponsesCarrySecurityHeaders()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync("/", Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(SecurityHeaders.ContentSecurityPolicy, response.Headers.GetValues("Content-Security-Policy").Single());
        Assert.StartsWith("default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'", SecurityHeaders.ContentSecurityPolicy, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Equal("nosniff", response.Headers.GetValues("X-Content-Type-Options").Single());
        Assert.Equal("no-referrer", response.Headers.GetValues("Referrer-Policy").Single());
        Assert.Equal(SecurityHeaders.PermissionsPolicy, response.Headers.GetValues("Permissions-Policy").Single());
        Assert.False(response.Headers.Contains("Server"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task HealthEndpointReportsHealthy()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        Assert.Equal("Healthy", await client.GetStringAsync("/health", Cancellation));
    }

    public async Task UnknownPathsGetTheNotFoundPage()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync("/no-such-page", Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NotFound, response.StatusCode);
        Assert.Contains("Nothing here", await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync(Cancellation), StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("https://evil.example", HttpStatusCode.Forbidden)]
    [InlineData(NestFactory.AllowedOrigin, HttpStatusCode.OK)]
    public async Task HubNegotiationChecksTheOrigin(string origin, HttpStatusCode expected)
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, "/_blazor/negotiate?negotiateVersion=1");
        request.Headers.Add("Origin", origin);
        using var response = await client.SendAsync(request, Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(expected, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task HubRefusesTransportsOtherThanWebSockets()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync("/_blazor?id=abc", Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task PagesAreCompressedWhenAsked()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Get, "/about");
        request.Headers.AcceptEncoding.ParseAdd("br");
        using var response = await client.SendAsync(request, Cancellation);
        Assert.Contains("br", response.Content.Headers.ContentEncoding);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task FormPostsAreRejected()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var content = new FormUrlEncodedContent([new KeyValuePair<string, string>("link", "https://example.com")]);
        using var response = await client.PostAsync("/", content, Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("/app.css")]
    [InlineData("/theme-init.js")]
    [InlineData("/favicon.svg")]
    [InlineData("/js/keys.js")]
    [InlineData("/js/sync.js")]
    public async Task StaticAssetsAreServed(string path)
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        using var response = await client.GetAsync(path, Cancellation);
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, response.StatusCode);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task TheAboutPageLinksToTheSourceAndWarnsAboutSharing()
    {
        using var client = CreateClient();
        var html = await client.GetStringAsync("/about", Cancellation);
        Assert.Contains("https://github.com/kusl/nest", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("can't be edited, deleted or recalled", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("data-clear-local", html, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    private HttpClient CreateClient() =>
        factory.CreateClient(new WebApplicationFactoryClientOptions { AllowAutoRedirect = false, HandleCookies = false });

using System.Net;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Antiforgery;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http;
using Nest.Web.Infrastructure;

namespace Nest.Web.Tests;

public sealed class TokenBucketTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void TakesUntilEmptyThenRefillsOverTime()
    {
        var time = new ManualTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch);
        var bucket = new TokenBucket(2, 1, time);
        Assert.True(bucket.TryTake());
        Assert.True(bucket.TryTake());
        Assert.False(bucket.TryTake());
        time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
        Assert.True(bucket.TryTake());
        Assert.False(bucket.TryTake());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NeverHoldsMoreThanItsCapacity()
    {
        var time = new ManualTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch);
        var bucket = new TokenBucket(2, 1, time);
        time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromHours(1));
        Assert.True(bucket.TryTake(2));
        Assert.False(bucket.TryTake());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeyedBucketsAreIndependentAndBounded()
    {
        var time = new ManualTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset.UnixEpoch);
        var buckets = new KeyedTokenBuckets(1, 1, time, maxKeys: 3);
        Assert.True(buckets.TryTake("a"));
        Assert.False(buckets.TryTake("a"));
        Assert.True(buckets.TryTake("b"));
        for (var i = 0; i < 10; i++)
        {
            buckets.TryTake($"k{i}");
        }

        Assert.InRange(buckets.Count, 1, 3);
    }
}

public sealed class ConnectionTrackerTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void LimitsConnectionsPerClientAndOverall()
    {
        var tracker = new ConnectionTracker(Limits.Create(o =>
        {
            o.MaxConnectionsPerClient = 2;
            o.MaxConnections = 3;
        }));

        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.Admitted, tracker.TryAdmit("a", out var first));
        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.Admitted, tracker.TryAdmit("a", out var second));
        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.ClientFull, tracker.TryAdmit("a", out _));
        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.Admitted, tracker.TryAdmit("b", out var third));
        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.ServerFull, tracker.TryAdmit("c", out _));

        first?.Dispose();
        first?.Dispose();
        Assert.Equal(2, tracker.Total);
        Assert.Equal(1, tracker.CountFor("a"));
        Assert.Equal(ConnectionAdmission.Admitted, tracker.TryAdmit("c", out var fourth));

        second?.Dispose();
        third?.Dispose();
        fourth?.Dispose();
        Assert.Equal(0, tracker.Total);
    }
}

    }

public sealed class CookielessAntiforgeryTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void NeverIssuesTokens()
    {
        var tokens = new CookielessAntiforgery().GetAndStoreTokens(new DefaultHttpContext());
        Assert.Null(tokens.RequestToken);
        Assert.Null(tokens.CookieToken);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task RejectsUnsafeRequests()
    {
        var antiforgery = new CookielessAntiforgery();
        var get = new DefaultHttpContext();
        get.Request.Method = HttpMethods.Get;
        var post = new DefaultHttpContext();
        post.Request.Method = HttpMethods.Post;

        Assert.True(await antiforgery.IsRequestValidAsync(get));
        Assert.False(await antiforgery.IsRequestValidAsync(post));
        await antiforgery.ValidateRequestAsync(get);
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<AntiforgeryValidationException>(() => antiforgery.ValidateRequestAsync(post));
    }
}

{

    [InlineData("/Components/App.razor.js", "/Components/App.razor.js")]
    public void MakesModulePathsRelative(string asset, string expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, ModulePath.Of(asset));
    }

    {
        Assert.True(new NestOptions().IsValid());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RejectsImpossibleLimits()
    {
        Assert.False(new NestOptions { MaxConnections = 1, MaxConnectionsPerClient = 2 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new NestOptions { Capacity = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new NestOptions { Capacity = 20_001 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new NestOptions { PostBurst = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new NestOptions { PostsPerSecond = 0 }.IsValid());
        Assert.False(new NestOptions { RequestBurst = 0 }.IsValid());
    }
}

using Nest.Web.Listings;

namespace Nest.Web.Tests;

public sealed class ListingFieldsTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void FieldsMatchTheSharedFixture()
    {
        foreach (var vector in Fixtures.Load<FieldVector[]>("fields"))
        {
            var ok = ListingFields.TryNormalize(vector.Key, vector.Input, out var value);
            Assert.Equal((vector.Key, vector.Input, vector.Ok, vector.Ok ? vector.Value : ""), (vector.Key, vector.Input, ok, value));
            if (ok)
            {
                Assert.True(ListingFields.TryNormalize(vector.Key, value, out var again));
                Assert.Equal(value, again);
            }
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeysAreUniqueAndOrdered()
    {
        Assert.Equal(ListingFields.All.Count, ListingFields.All.Select(field => field.Key).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count());
        Assert.Equal(0, ListingFields.OrderOf("price"));
        Assert.Equal(-1, ListingFields.OrderOf("pool"));
        Assert.Null(ListingFields.Find(null));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CleansText()
    {
        Assert.Equal("a b", ListingFields.CleanText(" a \n\n b ", 10));
        Assert.Equal("abc", ListingFields.CleanText("abcdef", 3));
        Assert.Equal("ab", ListingFields.CleanText("ab cd", 3));
        Assert.Equal("", ListingFields.CleanText(null, 3));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FormatsValues()
    {
        Assert.Equal("$1,250,000", ListingFields.Display("price", "1250000"));
        Assert.Equal("1,800", ListingFields.Display("sqft", "1800"));
        Assert.Equal("1925", ListingFields.Display("year", "1925"));
        Assert.Equal("3", ListingFields.Display("beds", "3"));
        Assert.Equal("", ListingFields.Display("beds", ""));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SummarizesAHome()
    {
        var fields = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal)
        {
            ["price"] = "450000",
            ["beds"] = "3",
            ["baths"] = "2.5",
            ["sqft"] = "1800",
            ["status"] = "Pending",
            ["street"] = "1 Main St",
            ["unit"] = "Apt 4",
            ["city"] = "Glen Aubrey",
            ["state"] = "NY",
            ["zip"] = "13777",
        };
        Assert.Equal("$450,000 · 3 bd · 2.5 ba · 1,800 sq ft · Pending", ListingFields.Summary(fields));
        Assert.Equal("Glen Aubrey, NY 13777", ListingFields.Place(fields));
        Assert.Equal("1 Main St, unit 4", ListingFields.StreetLine(fields));
        var empty = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        Assert.Equal("", ListingFields.Summary(empty));
        Assert.Equal("", ListingFields.Place(empty));
        Assert.Equal("", ListingFields.StreetLine(empty));
    }
}

using Nest.Web.Listings;

namespace Nest.Web.Tests;

public sealed class ListingIndexTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void MergesEntriesThatShareAnyKey()
    {
        var expected = Fixtures.Listings().Listings;
        AssertMatches(expected, ListingIndex.Build(Fixtures.ListingEntries()));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DoesNotDependOnEntryOrder()
    {
        var expected = Fixtures.Listings().Listings;
        var entries = Fixtures.ListingEntries();
        AssertMatches(expected, ListingIndex.Build(entries.AsEnumerable().Reverse()));
        AssertMatches(expected, ListingIndex.Build(entries.OrderBy(entry => entry.Text ?? entry.Kind, StringComparer.Ordinal)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FindsAListingByAnyOfItsKeys()
    {
        var listings = ListingIndex.Build(Fixtures.ListingEntries());
        Assert.Equal("zillow:29747113", ListingIndex.Find(listings, "address:13777-2893-state-rte-26")?.Key);
        Assert.Null(ListingIndex.Find(listings, "zillow:1"));
        Assert.Equal("Zillow · Redfin", listings[0].Sources);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void IgnoresEntriesWithoutKeys()
    {
        Assert.Empty(ListingIndex.Build([new Entry(Guid.CreateVersion7(), EntryKinds.Comment, [], Text: "lost")]));
    }

    private static void AssertMatches(IReadOnlyList<ListingVector> expected, IReadOnlyList<Listing> actual)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected.Count, actual.Count);
        for (var i = 0; i < expected.Count; i++)
        {
            var want = expected[i];
            var have = actual[i];
            Assert.Equal(want.Key, have.Key);
            Assert.Equal(want.Keys, have.Keys);
            Assert.Equal(want.Links, have.Links.Select(link => link.Url));
            Assert.Equal(want.Fields.OrderBy(pair => pair.Key, StringComparer.Ordinal), have.Fields.OrderBy(pair => pair.Key, StringComparer.Ordinal));
            Assert.Equal(want.Comments, have.Comments.Select(comment => comment.Id.ToString("D")));
            Assert.Equal(want.Updated, have.Updated.ToString("D"));
            Assert.Equal(want.Title, have.Title);
        }
    }
}

using Nest.Web.Listings;

namespace Nest.Web.Tests;

public sealed class ListingKeyTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void LinksMatchTheSharedFixture()
    {
        foreach (var vector in Fixtures.Load<LinkVector[]>("links"))
        {
            var parsed = ListingLink.Parse(vector.Input);
            Assert.Equal((vector.Input, vector.Key, vector.Url), (vector.Input, parsed?.Key, parsed?.Url));
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddressesMatchTheSharedFixture()
    {
        foreach (var vector in Fixtures.Load<AddressVector[]>("addresses"))
        {
            Assert.Equal((vector.Street, vector.Unit, vector.Key), (vector.Street, vector.Unit, ListingKey.AddressOf(vector.Street, vector.Unit, vector.Zip)));
        }
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("zillow:29747113", true)]
    [InlineData("zillow:029747113", false)]
    [InlineData("zillow:29747113\n", false)]
    [InlineData("redfin:1", true)]
    [InlineData("realtor:m1-2", true)]
    [InlineData("realtor:M1-2", false)]
    [InlineData("address:13777-main", true)]
    [InlineData("address:1377-main", false)]
    [InlineData("address:13777-Main", false)]
    [InlineData("url:0123456789abcdef", true)]
    [InlineData("url:0123", false)]
    [InlineData("other:1", false)]
    [InlineData(":1", false)]
    [InlineData(null, false)]
    public void ValidatesKeys(string? key, bool expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, ListingKey.IsValid(key));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RanksProvidersBeforeAddressesAndLinks()
    {
        string[] keys = ["url:0123456789abcdef", "address:13777-main", "redfin:2", "zillow:9", "realtor:m1-2"];
        Assert.Equal(["zillow:9", "redfin:2", "realtor:m1-2", "address:13777-main", "url:0123456789abcdef"], keys.Order(ListingKey.Comparer));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NormalizesKeyLists()
    {
        Assert.Equal(["redfin:2", "zillow:1"], ListingKey.Normalize(["zillow:1"], ["zillow:1", "bad", null, "redfin:2"]));
        var many = Enumerable.Range(1, 12).Select(i => $"redfin:{i}").ToArray();
        var keys = ListingKey.Normalize(["zillow:1"], many);
        Assert.Equal(ListingKey.MaxKeys, keys.Length);
        Assert.Contains("zillow:1", keys);
        Assert.Empty(ListingKey.Normalize([], null));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("zillow:29747113", "Zillow 29747113")]
    [InlineData("realtor:m1-2", "Realtor.com M1-2")]
    [InlineData("address:13777-2893-state-rte-26", "2893 state rte 26, 13777")]
    [InlineData("url:0123456789abcdef", "Link 012345")]
    public void LabelsKeys(string key, string expected)
    {
        Assert.Equal(expected, ListingKey.Label(key));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void BuildsPaths()
    {
        Assert.Equal("homes/zillow/1", ListingKey.Path("zillow:1"));
        Assert.Equal("zillow", ListingKey.ProviderOf("zillow:1"));
        Assert.Equal("1", ListingKey.TokenOf("zillow:1"));
        Assert.Null(ListingKey.ProviderOf("nothing"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void HashesWithFnv1a()
    {
        Assert.Equal("cbf29ce484222325", ListingKey.Fnv1a64(""));
        Assert.Equal("af63dc4c8601ec8c", ListingKey.Fnv1a64("a"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ExtractsTheUrlFromSharedText()
    {
        Assert.Equal("https://a.example/x?y=1", ListingLink.ExtractUrl("look: https://a.example/x?y=1, nice"));
        Assert.Null(ListingLink.ExtractUrl("none"));
        Assert.Null(ListingLink.ExtractUrl(null));
        Assert.Null(ListingLink.ExtractUrl("https://a.example/" + new string('x', 3000)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SplitsWordsOnAnythingButAsciiLettersAndDigits()
    {
        Assert.Equal(["100", "w", "57th", "st"], ListingKey.Words("100 W. 57th-St"));
        Assert.Empty(ListingKey.Words(null));
    }
}
