using Nest.Web.Infrastructure;
using Nest.Web.Listings;

namespace Nest.Web.Tests;

public sealed class NestServiceTests
{
    private const string Zillow = "https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/29747113_zpid/";
    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Start = new(2026, 11, 5, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
    private readonly ManualTimeProvider _time = new(Start);

    [Fact]
    public void StampsEntriesWithTheServerClockInOrder()
    {
        var nest = Create();
        var first = nest.Submit(Drafts.Link(Zillow), "a");
        var second = nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("hi", "zillow:29747113"), "a");
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Accepted, first.Result);
        Assert.Equal("zillow:29747113", first.Key);
        Assert.Equal(Start, Uuid7.Timestamp(first.Entry!.Id));
        Assert.True(second.Entry!.Id.CompareTo(first.Entry.Id) > 0);
        Assert.Equal([second.Entry.Id, first.Entry.Id], nest.Entries.Select(entry => entry.Id));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RejectsInvalidDrafts()
    {
        var nest = Create();
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Invalid, nest.Submit(null, "a").Result);
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Invalid, nest.Submit(Drafts.Link("nothing"), "a").Result);
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Invalid, nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("orphan"), "a").Result);
        Assert.Empty(nest.Entries);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ReturnsTheExistingHomeForAKnownLink()
    {
        var nest = Create();
        nest.Submit(Drafts.Link(Zillow), "a");
        var again = nest.Submit(Drafts.Link(Zillow + "?utm_source=share"), "b");
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Exists, again.Result);
        Assert.Equal("zillow:29747113", again.Key);
        Assert.Null(again.Entry);
        var joined = nest.Submit(Drafts.Link(Zillow, "redfin:5"), "b");
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Accepted, joined.Result);
        Assert.Single(nest.Listings);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeysAnAddressFactByItsAddress()
    {
        var reply = Create().Submit(Drafts.Fact([], ("street", "1 Main Street"), ("zip", "13777")), "a");
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Accepted, reply.Result);
        Assert.Equal("address:13777-1-main-st", reply.Key);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RefusesRecentRepeats()
    {
        var nest = Create();
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Accepted, nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("same", "zillow:1"), "a").Result);
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Repeat, nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("same", "zillow:1"), "b").Result);
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Accepted, nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("same", "zillow:2"), "b").Result);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RateLimitsEachClient()
    {
        var nest = Create(o =>
        {
            o.PostBurst = 2;
            o.PostsPerSecond = 0.5;
        });
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Accepted, nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("one", "zillow:1"), "a").Result);
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Accepted, nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("two", "zillow:1"), "a").Result);
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.TooFast, nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("three", "zillow:1"), "a").Result);
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Accepted, nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("four", "zillow:1"), "b").Result);
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2));
        Assert.Equal(SubmitResult.Accepted, nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("five", "zillow:1"), "a").Result);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeepsOnlyTheNewestEntries()
    {
        var nest = Create(o => o.Capacity = 3);
        for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++)
        {
            _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
            nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment($"c{i}", "zillow:1"), $"client {i}");
        }

        Assert.Equal(["c4", "c3", "c2"], nest.Entries.Select(entry => entry.Text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AcceptsOffersFromBeforeItStarted()
    {
        var nest = Create();
        var old = Comment(Start.AddDays(-3), "kept by a browser");
        Assert.Equal(1, nest.Offer([old]));
        Assert.Equal(0, nest.Offer([old]));
        Assert.Equal(old.Id, Assert.Single(nest.Entries).Id);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RejectsOffersFromTheFutureOrAfterItStarted()
    {
        var nest = Create();
        Assert.Equal(0, nest.Offer([Comment(Start.AddMinutes(5), "from the future")]));
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(10));
        Assert.Equal(0, nest.Offer([Comment(Start.AddMinutes(1), "forged")]));
        Assert.Empty(nest.Entries);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RejectsOffersThatAreNotNormalized()
    {
        var nest = Create();
        var at = Start.AddDays(-1);
        Assert.Equal(0, nest.Offer([Comment(at, " padded ")]));
        Assert.Equal(0, nest.Offer([new Entry(Guid.CreateVersion7(at), EntryKinds.Comment, ["bad"], Text: "x")]));
        Assert.Equal(0, nest.Offer([new Entry(Guid.NewGuid(), EntryKinds.Comment, ["zillow:1"], Text: "x")]));
        Assert.Equal(0, nest.Offer([new Entry(Guid.CreateVersion7(at), EntryKinds.Link, ["zillow:1"], Url: "https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/2_zpid/")]));
        Assert.Empty(nest.Entries);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeepsItsOwnEntriesWhenFullAndOffersAreOlder()
    {
        var nest = Create(o => o.Capacity = 2);
        nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("new one", "zillow:1"), "a");
        nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("new two", "zillow:1"), "b");
        Assert.Equal(0, nest.Offer([Comment(Start.AddDays(-1), "older")]));
        Assert.Equal(["new two", "new one"], nest.Entries.Select(entry => entry.Text));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SubscribersGetIdsThenDeltas()
    {
        var nest = Create(o => o.Capacity = 1);
        var first = nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("first", "zillow:1"), "a").Entry!;
        var recorder = new NestRecorder();
        nest.Subscribe(recorder);
        _time.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
        var second = nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("second", "zillow:1"), "b").Entry!;

        Assert.Equal(2, recorder.Updates.Count);
        var snapshot = recorder.Updates[0];
        Assert.Equal("snapshot", snapshot.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(1, snapshot.Capacity);
        Assert.Equal([first.Id], snapshot.Ids);
        Assert.Empty(snapshot.Added);
        var delta = recorder.Updates[1];
        Assert.Equal("delta", delta.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(snapshot.Version, delta.Previous);
        Assert.Equal(second.Id, Assert.Single(delta.Added).Id);
        Assert.Equal([first.Id], delta.Removed);
        Assert.True(nest.IsSubscribed(recorder));
        nest.Unsubscribe(recorder);
        Assert.False(nest.IsSubscribed(recorder));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DropsSubscribersThatCannotKeepUp()
    {
        var nest = Create();
        var refusing = new NestRecorder(accept: false);
        nest.Subscribe(refusing);
        Assert.False(nest.IsSubscribed(refusing));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FindsEntriesById()
    {
        var nest = Create();
        var entry = nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("find me", "zillow:1"), "a").Entry!;
        Assert.Equal([entry], nest.Find([entry.Id, Guid.NewGuid()]));
        Assert.Empty(nest.Find([]));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void BuildsListingsOncePerChange()
    {
        var nest = Create();
        nest.Submit(Drafts.Link(Zillow), "a");
        var listings = nest.Listings;
        Assert.Same(listings, nest.Listings);
        Assert.Equal("zillow:29747113", nest.FindListing("zillow:29747113")?.Key);
        nest.Submit(Drafts.Comment("new", "zillow:29747113"), "a");
        Assert.NotSame(listings, nest.Listings);
        Assert.Single(nest.FindListing("zillow:29747113")!.Comments);
        Assert.Equal(2, nest.Version);
    }

    private static Entry Comment(DateTimeOffset at, string text) =>
        new(Guid.CreateVersion7(at), EntryKinds.Comment, ["zillow:1"], Text: text);

    private NestService Create(Action<NestOptions>? configure = null) => new(_time, Limits.Create(configure));
}

public sealed class CommentTextTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void KeepsEmojiAndCutsOnGraphemes()
    {
        Assert.Equal("👍🏽 ok", CommentText.Normalize(" 👍🏽 ok "));
        var repeated = string.Concat(Enumerable.Repeat("👍🏽", CommentText.MaxLength));
        var cut = CommentText.Normalize(repeated);
        Assert.True(cut.Length <= CommentText.MaxLength);
        Assert.Equal(0, cut.Length % 4);
        Assert.True(CommentText.IsNormalized(cut));
        Assert.False(CommentText.IsNormalized(" x"));
        Assert.Equal("", CommentText.Normalize("​"));
    }
}

using Nest.Web.Infrastructure;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;

namespace Nest.Web.Tests;

public sealed class OutboxTests
{
    private static CancellationToken Cancellation => TestContext.Current.CancellationToken;

    [Fact]
    public async Task DeliversEverythingInOrder()
    {
        var received = new List<int>();
        var gate = new Lock();
        await using var outbox = new Outbox<int>(1000, 16, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), (batch, _) =>
        {
            lock (gate)
            {
                received.AddRange(batch);
            }

            return Task.CompletedTask;
        }, NullLogger.Instance);

        for (var i = 0; i < 200; i++)
        {
            Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(i));
        }

        await Eventually(() =>
        {
            lock (gate)
            {
                return received.Count == 200;
            }
        });

        Assert.Equal(Enumerable.Range(0, 200), received);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task RefusesItemsWhenFull()
    {
        var started = new TaskCompletionSource(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        var release = new TaskCompletionSource(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
        await using var outbox = new Outbox<int>(2, 1, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30), async (_, _) =>
        {
            started.TrySetResult();
            await release.Task;
        }, NullLogger.Instance);

        Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(1));
        await started.Task.WaitAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), Cancellation);
        Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(2));
        Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(3));
        Assert.False(outbox.TryPost(4));
        release.TrySetResult();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task FaultsWhenSendingFails()
    {
        await using var outbox = new Outbox<int>(10, 1, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), (_, _) => throw new InvalidOperationException("gone"), NullLogger.Instance);
        Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(1));
        await Eventually(() => outbox.IsFaulted);
        Assert.False(outbox.TryPost(2));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task FaultsWhenTheClientIsTooSlow()
    {
        await using var outbox = new Outbox<int>(10, 1, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(50), (_, token) => Task.Delay(Timeout.Infinite, token), NullLogger.Instance);
        Assert.True(outbox.TryPost(1));
        await Eventually(() => outbox.IsFaulted);
    }

    private static async Task Eventually(Func<bool> condition)
    {
        for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 200 && !condition(); attempt++)
        {
            await Task.Delay(25, Cancellation);
        }

        Assert.True(condition());
    }
}

using System.Text.Json;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using Nest.Web.Infrastructure;
using Nest.Web.Listings;

namespace Nest.Web.Tests;

internal sealed class ManualTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset start) : TimeProvider
{
    private DateTimeOffset _now = start;
    private long _timestamp;

    public override long TimestampFrequency => TimeSpan.TicksPerSecond;

    public override DateTimeOffset GetUtcNow() => _now;

    public override long GetTimestamp() => _timestamp;

    public void Advance(TimeSpan by)
    {
        _now += by;
        _timestamp += by.Ticks;
    }
}

internal static class Limits
{
    public static IOptions<NestOptions> Create(Action<NestOptions>? configure = null)
    {
        var options = new NestOptions();
        configure?.Invoke(options);
        return Options.Create(options);
    }
}

internal sealed class NestRecorder(bool accept = true) : INestSubscriber
{
    public List<NestUpdate> Updates { get; } = [];

    public bool Deliver(NestUpdate update)
    {
        if (accept)
        {
            Updates.Add(update);
        }

        return accept;
    }
}

internal sealed record LinkVector(string Input, string? Key, string? Url);

internal sealed record AddressVector(string Street, string Unit, string Zip, string? Key);

internal sealed record FieldVector(string Key, string Input, bool Ok, string? Value);

internal sealed record ListingVector(string Key, string[] Keys, string[] Links, Dictionary<string, string> Fields, string[] Comments, string Updated, string Title);

internal sealed record ListingScenario(EntryWire[] Entries, ListingVector[] Listings);

internal static class Fixtures
{
    public static T Load<T>(string name)
    {
        var path = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "fixtures", name + ".json");
        return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<T>(File.ReadAllText(path), Wire.Options)
            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException($"The fixture {name} is empty.");
    }

    public static ListingScenario Listings() => Load<ListingScenario>("listings");

    public static Entry[] ListingEntries() =>
        [.. Listings().Entries.Select(wire => wire.ToEntry() ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Bad fixture entry."))];
}

internal static class Drafts
{
    public static EntryDraft Link(string url, params string[] anchors) => new(EntryKinds.Link, anchors, url, null, null);

    public static EntryDraft Comment(string text, params string[] anchors) => new(EntryKinds.Comment, anchors, null, null, text);

    public static EntryDraft Fact(string[] anchors, params (string Key, string Value)[] fields) =>
        new(EntryKinds.Fact, anchors, null, [.. fields.Select(field => new FieldWire(field.Key, field.Value))], null);
}

using Nest.Web.Listings;

namespace Nest.Web.Tests;

public sealed class Uuid7Tests
{
    [Fact]
    public void ReadsTheTimestampBack()
    {
        var at = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 10, 12, 23, 18, 42, 123, TimeSpan.Zero);
        var id = Guid.CreateVersion7(at);
        Assert.True(Uuid7.IsValid(id));
        Assert.Equal(at, Uuid7.Timestamp(id));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ReadsAHandWrittenId()
    {
        var id = Guid.Parse("016f5e66-e800-7000-8000-000000000001");
        Assert.True(Uuid7.IsValid(id));
        Assert.Equal(new DateTimeOffset(2020, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero), Uuid7.Timestamp(id));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RejectsOtherVersions()
    {
        Assert.False(Uuid7.IsValid(Guid.NewGuid()));
        Assert.False(Uuid7.IsValid(Guid.Empty));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GuidOrderMatchesStringOrder()
    {
        var start = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
        var ids = Enumerable.Range(0, 300).Select(i => Guid.CreateVersion7(start.AddMilliseconds(i * 7919 % 1000))).ToList();
        var byGuid = ids.Order().Select(id => id.ToString()).ToList();
        var byString = ids.Select(id => id.ToString()).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal).ToList();
        Assert.Equal(byString, byGuid);
    }
}

public sealed class Uuid7OrderTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void AfterIsAlwaysLater()
    {
        var at = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 10, 12, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
        var previous = Guid.Empty;
        for (var i = 0; i < 1000; i++)
        {
            var next = Uuid7.After(previous, at);
            Assert.True(Uuid7.IsValid(next));
            Assert.True(next.CompareTo(previous) > 0);
            Assert.True(string.CompareOrdinal(next.ToString(), previous.ToString()) > 0);
            previous = next;
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AfterSurvivesTheClockGoingBack()
    {
        var at = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 10, 12, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
        var later = Guid.CreateVersion7(at.AddMinutes(5));
        var next = Uuid7.After(later, at);
        Assert.True(next.CompareTo(later) > 0);
        Assert.Equal(Uuid7.Timestamp(later), Uuid7.Timestamp(next));
    }
}

FILE:     tests/js/app.test.js
SIZE:     2.86 KB (2930 bytes)
LINES:    71
SHA256:   2044f2aa05a3c521a17734d89fd862a2e6b9a62a1c4db9c9e6e865b4571944ee
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:20:42 (UTC)

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { MAX_RETRIES, THEME_KEY, ViewHost, reconnectDelay, storedTheme, toggledTheme, viewId } from '../../src/Nest.Web/Components/App.razor.js';

describe('reconnectDelay', () => {
    it('retries at once, then backs off with jitter up to thirty seconds', () => {
        expect(reconnectDelay(0)).toBe(0);
        expect(reconnectDelay(1, () => 0)).toBe(500);
        expect(reconnectDelay(1, () => 1)).toBe(1000);
        expect(reconnectDelay(20, () => 1)).toBe(30000);
        expect(reconnectDelay(20, () => 0)).toBe(15000);
    });

    it('keeps trying for more than five minutes', () => {
        let total = 0;
        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_RETRIES; attempt += 1) {
            total += reconnectDelay(attempt, () => 0);
        }
        expect(total).toBeGreaterThan(5 * 60 * 1000);
    });
});

describe('theme', () => {
    it('reads only known values', () => {
        expect(storedTheme({ getItem: key => (key === THEME_KEY ? 'dark' : null) })).toBe('dark');
        expect(storedTheme({ getItem: () => 'blue' })).toBeNull();
        expect(storedTheme({ getItem: () => { throw new Error('denied'); } })).toBeNull();
        expect(storedTheme(null)).toBeNull();
    });

    it('toggles from the effective theme', () => {
        expect(toggledTheme(null, true)).toBe('light');
        expect(toggledTheme(null, false)).toBe('dark');
        expect(toggledTheme('dark', false)).toBe('light');
    });
});

describe('ViewHost', () => {
    function host(root) {
        const mounted = [];
        const disposed = [];
        const win = { document: { querySelector: () => root.current } };
        const views = new ViewHost(
            win,
            async path => ({ mount: element => {
                mounted.push([path, element.dataset.key]);
                return { dispose: () => disposed.push(element.dataset.key) };
            } }),
            async () => ({ nest: () => ({}) }));
        return { views, mounted, disposed };
    }

    it('mounts the view for the page and replaces it on navigation', async () => {
        const root = { current: { dataset: { view: 'homes' } } };
        const { views, mounted, disposed } = host(root);
        await views.mount();
        await views.mount();
        expect(mounted).toEqual([['./Pages/HomesPage.razor.js', undefined]]);
        root.current = { dataset: { view: 'listing', key: 'zillow:1' } };
        await views.mount();
        expect(mounted.at(-1)).toEqual(['./Pages/ListingPage.razor.js', 'zillow:1']);
        expect(disposed).toEqual([undefined]);
        root.current = null;
        await views.mount();
        expect(disposed).toEqual([undefined, 'zillow:1']);
    });

    it('names views by kind and key', () => {
        expect(viewId(null)).toBeNull();
        expect(viewId({ dataset: { view: 'listing', key: 'zillow:1' } })).toBe('listing|zillow:1');
    });
});

FILE:     tests/js/fields.test.js
SIZE:     3.03 KB (3098 bytes)
LINES:    73
SHA256:   406548d101b09a8a4f4bea84df826d7bb0be45a8099237e789885ce2687646cb
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:19:53 (UTC)

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
    FIELDS,
    changedFields,
    cleanText,
    displayValue,
    fieldOf,
    normalizeField,
    placeOf,
    streetOf,
    summaryOf,
} from '../../src/Nest.Web/wwwroot/js/fields.js';
import { fixture } from './fixtures.js';

describe('normalizeField', () => {
    for (const vector of fixture('fields')) {
        it(`${vector.key} ${JSON.stringify(vector.input)}`, () => {
            const result = normalizeField(vector.key, vector.input);
            expect(result.ok).toBe(vector.ok);
            if (vector.ok) {
                expect(result.value).toBe(vector.value);
                expect(normalizeField(vector.key, result.value).value).toBe(result.value);
            } else {
                expect(result.error).toBeTruthy();
            }
        });
    }
});

describe('fields', () => {
    it('has unique keys', () => {
        expect(new Set(FIELDS.map(field => field.key)).size).toBe(FIELDS.length);
        expect(fieldOf('nope')).toBeNull();
    });

    it('cleans text', () => {
        expect(cleanText(' a \n\n b ', 10)).toBe('a b');
        expect(cleanText('abcdef', 3)).toBe('abc');
        expect(cleanText('ab cd', 3)).toBe('ab');
        expect(cleanText(null, 3)).toBe('');
    });

    it('formats values', () => {
        expect(displayValue('price', '1250000')).toBe('$1,250,000');
        expect(displayValue('sqft', '1800')).toBe('1,800');
        expect(displayValue('year', '1925')).toBe('1925');
        expect(displayValue('beds', '3')).toBe('3');
        expect(displayValue('beds', '')).toBe('');
        expect(summaryOf({ price: '450000', beds: '3', baths: '2.5', sqft: '1800', status: 'Pending' })).toBe('$450,000 · 3 bd · 2.5 ba · 1,800 sq ft · Pending');
        expect(summaryOf({})).toBe('');
        expect(placeOf({ city: 'Glen Aubrey', state: 'NY', zip: '13777' })).toBe('Glen Aubrey, NY 13777');
        expect(placeOf({ zip: '13777' })).toBe('13777');
        expect(streetOf({ street: '1 Main St', unit: 'Apt 4' })).toBe('1 Main St, unit 4');
        expect(streetOf({ unit: '4' })).toBe('');
    });

    it('sends only what changed, with the whole address when any of it changed', () => {
        const current = { beds: '3', street: '1 Main St', zip: '13777' };
        expect(changedFields(current, { beds: '3', baths: '' }).changes).toEqual([]);
        expect(changedFields(current, { beds: '4' }).changes).toEqual([{ key: 'beds', value: '4' }]);
        expect(changedFields(current, { beds: '', street: '1 Main St', zip: '13777', city: 'Glen Aubrey', unit: '', state: '' }).changes).toEqual([
            { key: 'beds', value: '' },
            { key: 'street', value: '1 Main St' },
            { key: 'unit', value: '' },
            { key: 'city', value: 'Glen Aubrey' },
            { key: 'state', value: '' },
            { key: 'zip', value: '13777' },
        ]);
        const result = changedFields(current, { price: 'lots' });
        expect(result.changes).toEqual([]);
        expect(result.errors.map(error => error.key)).toEqual(['price']);
    });
});

FILE:     tests/js/keys.test.js
SIZE:     5.38 KB (5514 bytes)
LINES:    143
SHA256:   d73351361ca68d9e0f26a4f57a7863a3cc470df1924d1545d10d51d9f7435ff9
MODIFIED: 2026-11-05 15:19:53 (UTC)

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
    addressKey,
    compareKeys,
    extractUrl,
    fnv1a64,
    guessAddress,
    isKey,
    keyLabel,
    keyPath,
    keyUrl,
    normalizeKeys,
    parseLink,
    words,
} from '../../src/Nest.Web/wwwroot/js/keys.js';
import { fixture } from './fixtures.js';

describe('parseLink', () => {
    for (const vector of fixture('links')) {
        it(`reads ${JSON.stringify(vector.input).slice(0, 80)}`, () => {
            const parsed = parseLink(vector.input);
            expect(parsed?.key ?? null).toBe(vector.key);
            expect(parsed?.url ?? null).toBe(vector.url);
        });
    }

    it('returns valid keys only', () => {
        for (const vector of fixture('links').filter(item => item.key)) {
            expect(isKey(parseLink(vector.input).key)).toBe(true);
        }
    });
});

describe('addressKey', () => {
    for (const vector of fixture('addresses')) {
        it(`keys ${vector.street} ${vector.unit} ${vector.zip}`, () => {
            expect(addressKey(vector.street, vector.unit, vector.zip)).toBe(vector.key);
        });
    }
});

describe('keys', () => {
    it('validates each provider', () => {
        expect(isKey('zillow:29747113')).toBe(true);
        expect(isKey('zillow:029747113')).toBe(false);
        expect(isKey('redfin:1')).toBe(true);
        expect(isKey('realtor:m1-2')).toBe(true);
        expect(isKey('realtor:M1-2')).toBe(false);
        expect(isKey('address:13777-main')).toBe(true);
        expect(isKey('address:1377-main')).toBe(false);
        expect(isKey('url:0123456789abcdef')).toBe(true);
        expect(isKey('url:0123')).toBe(false);
        expect(isKey('other:1')).toBe(false);
        expect(isKey('__proto__:1')).toBe(false);
        expect(isKey(null)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('ranks providers before addresses and links', () => {
        const keys = ['url:0123456789abcdef', 'address:13777-main', 'redfin:2', 'zillow:9', 'realtor:m1-2'];
        expect(keys.sort(compareKeys)).toEqual(['zillow:9', 'redfin:2', 'realtor:m1-2', 'address:13777-main', 'url:0123456789abcdef']);
    });

    it('normalizes key lists', () => {
        expect(normalizeKeys(['zillow:1'], ['zillow:1', 'bad', 'redfin:2'])).toEqual(['redfin:2', 'zillow:1']);
        const many = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, index) => `redfin:${index + 1}`);
        const keys = normalizeKeys(['zillow:1'], many);
        expect(keys).toHaveLength(8);
        expect(keys).toContain('zillow:1');
    });

    it('labels, paths and urls', () => {
        expect(keyLabel('zillow:29747113')).toBe('Zillow 29747113');
        expect(keyLabel('realtor:m1-2')).toBe('Realtor.com M1-2');
        expect(keyLabel('address:13777-2893-state-rte-26')).toBe('2893 state rte 26, 13777');
        expect(keyLabel('url:0123456789abcdef')).toBe('Link 012345');
        expect(keyPath('zillow:1')).toBe('homes/zillow/1');
        expect(keyPath('bad')).toBeNull();
        expect(keyUrl('zillow:1')).toBe('https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/1_zpid/');
        expect(keyUrl('redfin:1')).toBe('https://www.redfin.com/home/1');
        expect(keyUrl('address:13777-main')).toBeNull();
        expect(parseLink(keyUrl('zillow:42')).key).toBe('zillow:42');
        expect(parseLink(keyUrl('redfin:42')).key).toBe('redfin:42');
    });

    it('hashes with FNV-1a', () => {
        expect(fnv1a64('')).toBe('cbf29ce484222325');
        expect(fnv1a64('a')).toBe('af63dc4c8601ec8c');
    });

    it('finds the url inside shared text', () => {
        expect(extractUrl('look: https://a.example/x?y=1, nice')).toBe('https://a.example/x?y=1');
        expect(extractUrl('none')).toBeNull();
        expect(extractUrl(42)).toBeNull();
        expect(extractUrl(`https://a.example/${'x'.repeat(3000)}`)).toBeNull();
    });

    it('splits words on anything but ASCII letters and digits', () => {
        expect(words('100 W. 57th-St')).toEqual(['100', 'w', '57th', 'st']);
        expect(words(null)).toEqual([]);
    });
});

describe('guessAddress', () => {
    it('reads a Zillow slug without separating street and city', () => {
        expect(guessAddress('https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/2893-State-Route-26-Gln-Glen-Aubrey-NY-13777/29747113_zpid/')).toEqual({
            street: '2893 State Route 26 Gln Glen Aubrey',
            unit: '',
            city: '',
            state: 'NY',
            zip: '13777',
            mixed: true,
        });
        expect(guessAddress('https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/29747113_zpid/')).toBeNull();
    });

    it('reads Redfin paths', () => {
        expect(guessAddress('https://www.redfin.com/NY/New-York/100-W-57th-St-10019/unit-12B/home/45021234')).toEqual({
            street: '100 W 57th St',
            unit: '12B',
            city: 'New York',
            state: 'NY',
            zip: '10019',
            mixed: false,
        });
        expect(guessAddress('https://www.redfin.com/home/1')).toBeNull();
    });

    it('reads Realtor.com paths', () => {
        expect(guessAddress('https://www.realtor.com/realestateandhomes-detail/2893-State-Route-26_Glen-Aubrey_NY_13777_M41234-56789')).toEqual({
            street: '2893 State Route 26',
            unit: '',
            city: 'Glen Aubrey',
            state: 'NY',
            zip: '13777',
            mixed: false,
        });
    });

    it('ignores other sites', () => {
        expect(guessAddress('https://example.com/a')).toBeNull();
        expect(guessAddress(null)).toBeNull();
    });
});

FILE:     tests/js/merge.test.js

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildListings, findListing, matches, newestFirst, oldestFirst, timestampOf } from '../../src/Nest.Web/wwwroot/js/merge.js';
import { fixture } from './fixtures.js';

const scenario = fixture('listings');

function shape(listing) {
    return {
        key: listing.key,
        keys: listing.keys,
        links: listing.links.map(link => link.url),
        fields: listing.fields,
        comments: listing.comments.map(comment => comment.id),
        updated: listing.updated,
        title: listing.title,
    };
}

describe('buildListings', () => {
    it('merges entries that share any key', () => {
        expect(buildListings(scenario.entries).map(shape)).toEqual(scenario.listings);
    });

    it('does not depend on entry order', () => {
        const reversed = [...scenario.entries].reverse();
        expect(buildListings(reversed).map(shape)).toEqual(scenario.listings);
        const shuffled = [...scenario.entries].sort((a, b) => (a.text ?? a.kind).localeCompare(b.text ?? b.kind));
        expect(buildListings(shuffled).map(shape)).toEqual(scenario.listings);
    });

    it('keeps field history and ownership', () => {
        const entries = scenario.entries.map(entry => (entry.kind === 'comment' ? { ...entry, mine: true } : entry));
        const [home] = buildListings(entries);
        expect(home.history.beds.map(item => item.value)).toEqual(['3', '4']);
        expect(home.mine).toBe(true);
        expect(home.ids).toHaveLength(8);
    });

    it('skips entries without keys', () => {
        expect(buildListings([null, { id: 'x', keys: [] }])).toEqual([]);
    });

    it('finds and filters listings', () => {
        const listings = buildListings(scenario.entries);
        expect(findListing(listings, 'address:13777-2893-state-rte-26').key).toBe('zillow:29747113');
        expect(findListing(listings, 'zillow:1')).toBeNull();
        expect(matches(listings[0], '')).toBe(true);
        expect(matches(listings[0], '2893 route')).toBe(true);
        expect(matches(listings[0], '13777')).toBe(true);
        expect(matches(listings[0], 'redfin.com')).toBe(true);
        expect(matches(listings[0], 'miami')).toBe(false);
    });
});

describe('ids', () => {
    it('sorts and reads timestamps', () => {
        const ids = [{ id: '0195aaaa-0000-7000-8000-000000000000' }, { id: '0195bbbb-0000-7000-8000-000000000000' }];
        expect([...ids].sort(newestFirst)[0].id).toBe('0195bbbb-0000-7000-8000-000000000000');
        expect([...ids].sort(oldestFirst)[0].id).toBe('0195aaaa-0000-7000-8000-000000000000');
        expect(timestampOf('016f5e66-e800-7000-8000-000000000001')).toBe(Date.UTC(2020, 0, 1));
    });
});

SHA256:   1463f4e0bb448343b77f0f0a55b019ec1f577e944197a510bacc0c2e36d89b7f

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { DRAFT_PREFIX, EntryStore, LOCAL_CAP, deleteDatabase, draftKeys, excessOf, migrations, readDraft, upgrade, writeDraft } from '../../src/Nest.Web/wwwroot/js/store.js';

class MemoryStorage {
    constructor() {
        this.map = new Map();
    }

    get length() {
        return this.map.size;
    }

    key(index) {
        return [...this.map.keys()][index] ?? null;
    }

    getItem(key) {
        return this.map.get(key) ?? null;
    }

    setItem(key, value) {
        this.map.set(key, String(value));
    }

    removeItem(key) {
        this.map.delete(key);
    }
}

describe('store', () => {
    it('caps local history', () => {
        expect(excessOf(LOCAL_CAP)).toBe(0);
        expect(excessOf(LOCAL_CAP + 5)).toBe(5);
    });

    it('runs every migration from the stored version', () => {
        const created = [];
        const db = { createObjectStore: (name, options) => created.push([name, options]) };
        upgrade(db, 0, migrations.length);
        expect(created).toEqual([['entries', { keyPath: 'id' }]]);
        created.length = 0;
        upgrade(db, 1, 1);
        expect(created).toEqual([]);
    });

    it('keeps comment drafts per home', () => {
        const storage = new MemoryStorage();
        writeDraft(storage, 'zillow:1', 'hello');
        storage.setItem('nest-theme', 'dark');
        expect(readDraft(storage, 'zillow:1')).toBe('hello');
        expect(draftKeys(storage)).toEqual([`${DRAFT_PREFIX}zillow:1`]);
        writeDraft(storage, 'zillow:1', '');
        expect(readDraft(storage, 'zillow:1')).toBe('');
        expect(readDraft(null, 'x')).toBe('');
    });

    it('works without IndexedDB', async () => {
        const store = new EntryStore(undefined);
        expect(await store.all()).toEqual([]);
        expect(await store.save([{ id: 'a' }])).toEqual([]);
        expect(await deleteDatabase(undefined)).toBe('deleted');
    });
});

SHA256:   4558cc04320d24eae4f141f1086de59cef5d8a86e79fce8f2da1cf7392264686

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Nest, chunk, chunkBySize, missingIds, needsResync, offerCandidates, wire } from '../../src/Nest.Web/wwwroot/js/sync.js';

const id = n => `0195${n.toString(16).padStart(4, '0')}-0000-7000-8000-${n.toString(16).padStart(12, '0')}`;
const comment = (n, text = 'hi') => ({ id: id(n), kind: 'comment', keys: ['zillow:1'], text });

class MemoryStore {
    constructor(records = []) {
        this.records = new Map(records.map(record => [record.id, record]));
    }

    async all() {
        return [...this.records.values()];
    }

    async save(records) {
        for (const record of records) {
            this.records.set(record.id, record);
        }
        return [];
    }
}

function fakeWindow() {
    return {
        setTimeout: () => 1,
        clearTimeout: () => {},
        setInterval: () => 1,
        clearInterval: () => {},
        addEventListener: () => {},
    };
}

function fakeServer(entries, capacity = 1000) {
    const calls = [];
    const known = new Map(entries.map(entry => [entry.id, entry]));
    return {
        calls,
        known,
        invokeMethodAsync: vi.fn(async (method, ...args) => {
            calls.push([method, ...args]);
            if (method === 'Fetch') {
                return args[0].map(value => known.get(value)).filter(Boolean);
            }
            if (method === 'Offer') {
                return args[0].length;
            }
            if (method === 'Post') {
                const entry = comment(900, args[0].text);
                return { result: 'accepted', entry, key: 'zillow:1' };
            }
            if (method === 'Ping') {
                return true;
            }
            return undefined;
        }),
        capacity,
    };
}

describe('helpers', () => {
    it('detects version gaps', () => {
        expect(needsResync(3, { previous: 3 })).toBe(false);
        expect(needsResync(3, { previous: 2 })).toBe(true);
    });

    it('wires entries without local fields', () => {
        expect(wire({ ...comment(1), mine: true })).toEqual(comment(1));
        expect(wire({ id: id(1), kind: 'link', keys: ['zillow:1'], url: 'u' })).toEqual({ id: id(1), kind: 'link', keys: ['zillow:1'], url: 'u' });
        expect(wire({ id: id(1), kind: 'fact', keys: ['zillow:1'], fields: [] })).toEqual({ id: id(1), kind: 'fact', keys: ['zillow:1'], fields: [] });
    });

    it('finds missing ids', () => {
        expect(missingIds(['a', 'b'], new Map([['a', {}]]))).toEqual(['b']);
    });

    it('offers only what the server lacks, newest first, within its capacity', () => {
        const local = [comment(1), comment(2), comment(3), comment(4)];
        expect(offerCandidates(local, new Set([id(2)]), 10).map(entry => entry.id)).toEqual([id(4), id(3), id(1)]);
        expect(offerCandidates(local, new Set([id(2), id(3)]), 2).map(entry => entry.id)).toEqual([id(4)]);
        expect(offerCandidates(local, new Set(), 2).map(entry => entry.id)).toEqual([id(4), id(3)]);
    });

    it('chunks by size and count', () => {
        const items = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, index) => comment(index, 'x'.repeat(400)));
        const chunks = chunkBySize(items, 1000);
        expect(chunks.flat()).toEqual(items);
        expect(chunks.every(part => part.length >= 1)).toBe(true);
        expect(chunkBySize([comment(1, 'x'.repeat(2000))], 1000)).toEqual([]);
        expect(chunk([1, 2, 3], 2)).toEqual([[1, 2], [3]]);
    });
});

describe('Nest', () => {
    it('fetches what it lacks and offers what the server lacks', async () => {
        const server = fakeServer([comment(2), comment(3)]);
        const shared = new Nest(fakeWindow(), new MemoryStore([comment(1)]));
        await shared.connect(server);
        expect(server.calls[0]).toEqual(['Join']);
        await shared.apply([{ kind: 'snapshot', version: 1, previous: 1, capacity: 1000, ids: [id(3), id(2)], added: [], removed: [] }], server);
        await shared.reconciling;
        expect(server.calls.find(call => call[0] === 'Fetch')[1]).toEqual([id(3), id(2)]);
        expect(server.calls.find(call => call[0] === 'Offer')[1].map(entry => entry.id)).toEqual([id(1)]);
        expect([...shared.entries.keys()].sort()).toEqual([id(1), id(2), id(3)]);
        expect(shared.status).toBe('live');
        expect(shared.ready).toBe(true);
        expect(shared.isLive(id(1))).toBe(false);
        expect(shared.listings()).toHaveLength(1);
    });

    it('applies deltas and resyncs on a gap', async () => {
        const server = fakeServer([]);
        const shared = new Nest(fakeWindow(), new MemoryStore());
        await shared.connect(server);
        await shared.apply([{ kind: 'snapshot', version: 1, previous: 1, capacity: 1000, ids: [], added: [], removed: [] }], server);
        await shared.apply([{ kind: 'delta', version: 2, previous: 1, capacity: 0, ids: [], added: [comment(5)], removed: [] }], server);
        expect(shared.isLive(id(5))).toBe(true);
        await shared.apply([{ kind: 'delta', version: 3, previous: 2, capacity: 0, ids: [], added: [], removed: [id(5)] }], server);
        expect(shared.isLive(id(5))).toBe(false);
        expect(shared.entries.has(id(5))).toBe(true);
        const joins = server.calls.filter(call => call[0] === 'Join').length;
        await shared.apply([{ kind: 'delta', version: 9, previous: 7, capacity: 0, ids: [], added: [], removed: [] }], server);
        expect(server.calls.filter(call => call[0] === 'Join').length).toBe(joins + 1);
    });

    it('ignores updates from a detached island', async () => {
        const server = fakeServer([]);
        const shared = new Nest(fakeWindow(), new MemoryStore());
        await shared.connect(server);
        shared.disconnect(server);
        await shared.apply([{ kind: 'snapshot', version: 1, previous: 1, capacity: 1000, ids: [id(1)], added: [], removed: [] }], server);
        expect(shared.status).toBe('local');
        expect(shared.server.size).toBe(0);
    });

    it('posts only while live and marks its own entries', async () => {
        const server = fakeServer([]);
        const store = new MemoryStore();
        const shared = new Nest(fakeWindow(), store);
        expect((await shared.post({ kind: 'comment', anchors: ['zillow:1'], text: 'x' })).result).toBe('offline');
        await shared.connect(server);
        await shared.apply([{ kind: 'snapshot', version: 1, previous: 1, capacity: 1000, ids: [], added: [], removed: [] }], server);
        const outcome = await shared.post({ kind: 'comment', anchors: ['zillow:1'], text: 'mine' });
        expect(outcome.result).toBe('accepted');
        expect(shared.entries.get(id(900)).mine).toBe(true);
        expect(store.records.get(id(900)).mine).toBe(true);
        expect(shared.isLive(id(900))).toBe(true);
    });

    it('notifies listeners once per burst', async () => {
        const shared = new Nest(fakeWindow(), new MemoryStore([comment(1)]));
        const listener = vi.fn();
        shared.listen(listener);
        await shared.loaded;
        await Promise.resolve();
        expect(listener).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
        await shared.clear();
        await Promise.resolve();
        expect(shared.entries.size).toBe(0);
    });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RESULT_TEXT, STATUS_TEXT, commentsLabel, isoOf, previewText, readMineOnly, timeLabel, visibleListings, writeMineOnly } from '../../src/Nest.Web/Components/Pages/HomesPage.razor.js';
import { addressSuggestion, anchorsOf, countText, previousValue } from '../../src/Nest.Web/Components/Pages/ListingPage.razor.js';
import { parseLink } from '../../src/Nest.Web/wwwroot/js/keys.js';
import { buildListings } from '../../src/Nest.Web/wwwroot/js/merge.js';
import { fixture } from './fixtures.js';

const listings = buildListings(fixture('listings').entries);
const ID = '016f5e66-e800-7000-8000-000000000001';

describe('homes view helpers', () => {
    it('describes a pasted link', () => {
        expect(previewText(null, null)).toBe('');
        expect(previewText(parseLink('https://www.zillow.com/homedetails/1_zpid/'), null)).toBe('Zillow home 1.');
        expect(previewText(parseLink('https://example.com/a'), listings[1])).toBe(`A link from another site. Already here: ${listings[1].title}.`);
    });

    it('filters by text and ownership', () => {
        expect(visibleListings(listings, '', false)).toHaveLength(2);
        expect(visibleListings(listings, 'miami', false)).toHaveLength(0);
        expect(visibleListings(listings, '2893', false)).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(visibleListings(listings, '', true)).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('formats counts and times', () => {
        expect(commentsLabel(1)).toBe('1 comment');
        expect(commentsLabel(0)).toBe('0 comments');
        expect(isoOf(ID)).toBe('2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z');
        expect(timeLabel(ID, null)).toBe('2020-01-01 00:00');
        expect(timeLabel(ID, { format: () => 'formatted' })).toBe('formatted');
        expect(timeLabel(ID, { format: () => { throw new Error('x'); } })).toBe('2020-01-01 00:00');
        expect(STATUS_TEXT.live).toBe('Live');
        expect(RESULT_TEXT.offline).toMatch(/Not connected/);
    });

    it('remembers the ownership filter', () => {
        const map = new Map();
        const storage = { getItem: key => map.get(key) ?? null, setItem: (key, value) => map.set(key, value), removeItem: key => map.delete(key) };
        writeMineOnly(storage, true);
        expect(readMineOnly(storage)).toBe(true);
        writeMineOnly(storage, false);
        expect(readMineOnly(storage)).toBe(false);
        expect(readMineOnly(null)).toBe(false);
    });
});

describe('listing view helpers', () => {
    it('anchors new entries to the known keys', () => {
        expect(anchorsOf(listings[0], 'zillow:29747113')).toEqual(listings[0].keys);
        expect(anchorsOf(null, 'zillow:5')).toEqual(['zillow:5']);
    });

    it('shows the previous value of a field', () => {
        expect(previousValue(listings[0].history, 'beds', '4')).toBe('3');
        expect(previousValue(listings[0].history, 'street', '2893 State Route 26')).toBeNull();
        expect(previousValue(undefined, 'beds', '4')).toBeNull();
    });

    it('suggests an address from a link only when none is known', () => {
        expect(addressSuggestion(listings[0])).toBeNull();
        const [fresh] = buildListings([{ id: ID, kind: 'link', keys: ['realtor:m1-2'], url: 'https://www.realtor.com/realestateandhomes-detail/1-Main-St_Glen-Aubrey_NY_13777_M1-2' }]);
        expect(addressSuggestion(fresh)).toMatchObject({ street: '1 Main St', city: 'Glen Aubrey', state: 'NY', zip: '13777' });
        expect(addressSuggestion(null)).toBeNull();
    });

    it('counts characters', () => {
        expect(countText(3, 4000)).toBe('3 / 4000');
    });
});
